## 700199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よぉ、<value:$28><line-break>邪魔していいか？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Mind if I interrupt?<end>"
KO: "여어, <value:$28>. 방해 좀 해도 될까?<end>"

## 700200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セラ見てるとホント<line-break>リディオンを思い出すよ。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>アミラルで最強の剣士。<line-break>アミラルの銀狼騒ぎのとき、銀狼を<line-break>退治して、旅に出たことになってる。<end>"
EN: "Seeing Sera always reminds me of Lidion. Lidion was the finest swordsman in Amiral. During the Silver Wolf incident there, Lidion killed the Silver Wolf, then supposedly set off on a journey.<end>"
KO: "세라를 보고 있으면 정말 리디온이 떠올라. 아미랄 최강의 검사. 아미랄 은랑 사건 때 은랑을 퇴치하고 여행을 떠난 걸로 되어 있지.<end>"

## 700201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつ…、<line-break>ずっと、昔の心の傷を負って、　　　　<line-break>心を閉ざして、荒れてた。　<line-break>あいつ、ガキのころ、<line-break>ロベルトさんとこの娘さんを<line-break>町の外へデート気分で連れ出したんだ。<end>"
EN: "Lidion had carried an old emotional wound for years. Shut everyone out and went off the rails. When Lidion was a kid, Lidion took Roberto's daughter outside town as if the two of them were going on a date.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석… 오래전 마음의 상처를 안고 마음을 닫고 거칠게 살았어. 어릴 때 로베르토 씨네 딸을 데이트 기분으로 마을 밖에 데리고 나갔거든.<end>"

## 700202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのとき、モンスターに襲われて　　　<line-break>リディオンは助かったんだが<line-break>…娘さんの方は…ダメだった。<line-break>彼女を守れなかった…<line-break>そのことでリディオンは<line-break>変わっちまった…。<end>"
EN: "The two of them were attacked by monsters. Lidion survived, but Roberto's daughter... didn't. Lidion couldn't protect her. That was what changed Lidion.<end>"
KO: "그때 몬스터에게 습격당해서 리디온은 살아남았지만… 따님은… 안 됐어. 그녀를 지키지 못했다는 일 때문에 리디온은 변해 버렸지….<end>"

## 700203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もともと意固地な奴だったけど<line-break>それからは、周囲の誰にも心を閉ざし、<line-break>ひたすら、怪物退治と剣に没頭した。<line-break>あいつ…、<line-break>俺によく突っかかってきたよな。<line-break>剣の勝負だとか、なんだとか言ってさ。<end>"
EN: "Lidion had always been stubborn, but after that, Lidion shut everyone out and devoted everything to hunting monsters and mastering the sword. Lidion was always coming after me, challenging me to sword matches and whatever else.<end>"
KO: "원래 고집 센 녀석이었지만 그 뒤로는 주변 누구에게도 마음을 닫고 오직 괴물 퇴치와 검에만 몰두했어. 그 녀석… 나한테 자주 시비 걸었지. 검으로 승부하자느니 뭐라느니 하면서.<end>"

## 700204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれをみんなは、ケンカだとか<line-break>槍もでき、人の受けもいい俺のことを<line-break>嫉妬してだとか言ってたけど、違う。　<line-break>その証拠に俺は奴に１回も<line-break>勝てたことがない。<line-break>それでも、奴は俺に何度も挑み続けた。<end>"
EN: "Everyone called it fighting. Some said Lidion was jealous because I was good with a spear and people liked me. But they were wrong. I never beat Lidion. Not once. And still, the challenges kept coming.<end>"
KO: "다들 그걸 싸움이라느니, 창도 잘 쓰고 사람들에게 평판 좋은 나를 질투해서라느니 했지만 아니야. 그 증거로 난 그 녀석에게 단 한 번도 이긴 적이 없어. 그래도 녀석은 몇 번이고 내게 계속 덤볐지.<end>"

## 700205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴が、最後にすがろうとした　　　　　<line-break>人とのつながりが<line-break>俺…だったんじゃないかな。<line-break>あいつはあいつの心の中の様々な気持ち<line-break>…それは口にすればいいわけになる…<line-break>それを剣に託して伝えたかったんだ。<end>"
EN: "Maybe I was the last human connection Lidion tried to cling to. Lidion had all kinds of feelings inside, but putting them into words would only have turned them into excuses. So Lidion tried to convey them through the sword instead.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석이 마지막으로 매달리려 했던 사람과의 연결이… 나였던 게 아닐까. 그 녀석은 마음속의 여러 감정들… 입으로 말하면 변명이 되어 버릴 그것들을 검에 실어 전하고 싶었던 거야.<end>"

## 700206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…だけど俺の槍は未熟で、<line-break>結局、奴の剣に応えられなかった。<end>"
EN: "But I wasn't skilled enough with my spear. In the end, I couldn't answer that sword.<end>"
KO: "…하지만 내 창은 미숙해서 결국 녀석의 검에 응하지 못했다.<end>"

## 700207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あいつさ。旅に出たことに<line-break>なってるけどさ。…実は、<line-break>銀狼との死闘での傷で死んだんだ。　　<line-break>奴は、町の連中に奴らのために死んだと<line-break>思われるのがイヤで、旅に出たことに<line-break>してくれって、死に際に頼んだんだ。<end>"
EN: "The story is that Lidion went off traveling. But the truth is, Lidion died from wounds suffered in that fight with the Silver Wolf. Lidion didn't want the people in town thinking Lidion had died for them, so before dying, Lidion asked for everyone to be told that Lidion had gone traveling.<end>"
KO: "…그 녀석 말이야. 여행을 떠났다고 알려져 있지만… 사실은 은랑과의 사투에서 입은 상처로 죽었어. 녀석은 마을 사람들이 자기가 그들을 위해 죽었다고 생각하는 게 싫다며, 여행을 떠난 걸로 해 달라고 죽기 직전에 부탁했지.<end>"

## 700208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺と奴のおやじさんに、さ…。　　　　<line-break>あとは、一言もない。<line-break>奴らしい片意地な最期だった…。<end>"
EN: "Asked me and Lidion's father, that is. Not another word after that. Stubborn to the bitter end. Just like Lidion.<end>"
KO: "나와 그 녀석 아버지에게 말이야…. 그게 전부였어. 녀석다운 고집스러운 최후였지….<end>"

## 700209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セラって奴さ。見てると<line-break>リディオンのこと、思い出すんだ。　<line-break>　<line-break>あいつも、不器用で片意地で<line-break>心の中の言葉を、<line-break>うまく、口にできない。<end>"
EN: "Looking at Sera reminds me of Lidion. He's awkward and stubborn too, and he can't seem to put what's inside him into words.<end>"
KO: "세라라는 녀석 말이야. 보고 있으면 리디온이 생각나. 그 녀석도 서툴고 고집 세고, 마음속 말을 제대로 입 밖에 못 냈지.<end>"

## 700210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴もリディオンみたいな重いものを　<line-break>心に持ってるんだ…って思う。<line-break>　<line-break>例えば、<line-break>奴とアーギルシャイアとが<line-break>不自然なほど似てることとか、さ。<end>"
EN: "I think he's carrying something heavy inside, just like Lidion was. Like how unnaturally alike he and Argyleshire are.<end>"
KO: "녀석도 리디온처럼 무거운 걸 마음에 품고 있는 것 같아…. 예를 들면 녀석과 아르길샤이어가 부자연스러울 정도로 닮은 것 같은 거.<end>"

## 700211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺、お前に<line-break>奴を見放さないようにしてほしい。<line-break>そうじゃなきゃ、あとで後悔する…。<end>"
EN: "I'm asking you not to give up on him. Otherwise, you'll regret it later.<end>"
KO: "난 네가 그 녀석을 포기하지 않았으면 해. 안 그러면 나중에 후회하게 될 거야….<end>"

## 700212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすがに夜は冷え込むよな…<line-break><value:$28>、中に入ろうぜ。<end>"
EN: "It really does get cold at night. Come on, <value:$28>. Let's go inside.<end>"
KO: "역시 밤은 춥네…. <value:$28>, 안으로 들어가자.<end>"

## 700213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやー、きれいだわ。<line-break>セラちゃんは、もう。　<line-break>　<line-break>きゃー、セラ様ー！<line-break>って感じかな。<line-break>いやー、かっこいいわ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, Sera is just gorgeous. It's like, “Eee! Lord Sera!” Oh, he's so cool.<end>"
KO: "이야, 예쁘네. 세라는 정말. 꺄아, 세라 님~! 이런 느낌인가. 이야, 멋있다.<end>"

## 700214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おんやー？　あたしが彼ばっかり<line-break>ほめるから、すねちった？<end>"
EN: "Oh? Are you sulking because I keep praising him?<end>"
KO: "어라~? 내가 그 사람만 칭찬하니까 삐친 거야?<end>"

## 700215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アハハハ、剣闘士<value:$28>も<line-break>めちゃ、かっこいいってばさ。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>なんつーかなー、あんたは正統派の<line-break>かっこいい男なんだけどさ。<line-break>彼は陰のかっこよさなのよ。<end>"
EN: "Ahahaha! Gladiator <value:$28> is incredibly handsome too, I'm telling you. How do I put it? You're the classic, straight-up kind of handsome man. He's got a darker kind of appeal.<end>"
KO: "아하하하, 검투사 <value:$28>도 엄청 멋있다니까. 뭐랄까, 넌 정통파로 멋있는 남자인데, 그는 어딘가 음지의 멋이 있어.<end>"

## 700216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クールでさ。どことなく影とか<line-break>あってさ…。マイナスの美学ってやつ？<line-break>ま、ガキには受け悪いかもしれないね。<line-break>女にしてもいいくらいきれいってのも<line-break>そそるよね～。しかもさ、女にしても<line-break>小動物的かわいい系にはならないとこ。<end>"
EN: "He's cool, with a hint of darkness about him. The beauty of the negative, I guess? Maybe kids wouldn't go for it. And he's so beautiful he could pass for a woman. That's a turn-on too. Especially since even as a woman, he wouldn't be the cute little-animal type.<end>"
KO: "쿨하고 말이지. 어딘가 그늘도 있고…. 마이너스의 미학이랄까? 뭐, 애들한테는 별로 안 먹힐지도 모르지. 여자라고 해도 될 만큼 예쁘다는 것도 끌리고~. 게다가 여자로 치면 작은 동물 같은 귀여운 타입도 아니고.<end>"

## 700217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪女、妖女系だよね。しかも特上の。　<line-break>円卓騎士アーギルシャイアとか、<line-break>ああいう感じになるんじゃないかな。<end>"
EN: "He'd be a wicked, bewitching woman―and a first-rate one at that. Maybe something like Argyleshire, Knight of the Round Table.<end>"
KO: "악녀, 요녀 계열이지. 그것도 최상급. 원탁기사 아르길샤이어 같은 느낌이 되지 않을까.<end>"

## 700218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イックショイ！！　ちくしょ！<line-break>うー、夜はさぶいやね～。<line-break>中、入んね？　<value:$28>。<end>"

KO: "에취!! 젠장! 으으, 밤은 춥네~. 안에 들어갈래, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 700219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "아, <value:$28>다! 저기, 저기, 엄청난 걸 발견했어! 들어 봐!<end>"

## 700220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのね、あのね、<line-break>セラとアーギルシャイアって<line-break>似てるでしょ？<end>"
EN: "You know what? You know what? Sera and Argyleshire are kind of alike, aren't they?<end>"

## 700221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふー、それだけ。<line-break>そんなところでいつまでも<line-break>難しいこと考えてたら、風邪ひくよぉ。<end>"

## 700222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もうイヤ、もうイヤ！　もぉうイヤ！！<end>"
EN: "I can't stand it! I can't stand it! I can't stand it anymore!<end>"
KO: "이제 싫어, 싫어! 정말 싫어!!<end>"

## 700223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうね。優雅で高貴なエルフの<line-break>アタクシともあろうものがはしたない…<line-break>　<line-break>って、そのセキニンの一端はあなたに<line-break>あるんじゃない、<value:$28>！<line-break>信じられないわ！　信じられないわ！！<end>"
EN: "Y-yes, you're right. Such unseemly behavior is beneath a graceful, noble Elf like me... But you bear some of the responsibility for this, <value:$28>! I simply cannot believe it! I cannot believe it!<end>"
KO: "그, 그렇네. 우아하고 고귀한 엘프인 내가 체면없게…. 아니, 그 책임의 일부는 너한테도 있잖아, <value:$28>! 믿을 수 없어! 정말 믿을 수 없어!!<end>"

## 700224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セ・ラ！　セラよ。セラのことよ！<line-break>あの生意気なド下等生物のことよー！　<line-break>　<line-break>あのすかした態度がイヤなのよ！<line-break>自分は強いのだ、自分は美形なのだ、<line-break>って自信ありありなのがイヤなの！<end>"
EN: "Se-ra! Sera! I'm talking about Sera! That insolent, utterly inferior creature! I can't stand that smug attitude of his! He thinks he's so strong, so handsome―and he's so insufferably sure of it!<end>"
KO: "세·라! 세라 말이야, 세라! 그 건방진 최하등생물 말이야! 그 잘난 척하는 태도가 싫어! 자기는 강하다, 자기는 잘생겼다 하는 자신만만한 꼴이 싫다고!<end>"

## 700225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そーよ、美形美形って、顔だって<line-break>あの性格悪い魔人アーギルシャイアに　<line-break>そっくりなんじゃないのー！<line-break>そーんなの、自慢にもならなくてよー！<line-break>それなのに、あのすかした態度は何？<line-break>もうイヤ、もうイヤ！　もぉうイヤ！！<end>"
EN: "Handsome this, handsome that―why, even his face looks just like that nasty demon Argyleshire! That's hardly something to boast about! So what's with that smug attitude? I can't stand it! I can't stand it! I can't stand it anymore!<end>"
KO: "그래, 미남 미남 하는데 얼굴도 그 성격 나쁜 마인 아르길샤이어랑 똑같은 거 아냐! 그런 건 자랑거리도 아니라고! 그런데도 그 건방진 태도는 뭐야? 정말 싫어, 싫어! 정말 싫다고!!<end>"

## 700226
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こら。高貴なエルフであるアタクシの<line-break>言うことをちゃんと聞きなさいよー！<line-break>　<line-break>信じられない！　信じられないわ！！<line-break>お待ちなさい！　<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "You there! Listen when a noble Elf like me is speaking to you! I cannot believe this! I simply cannot believe it! Wait right there, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "이봐. 고귀한 엘프인 이 몸의 말을 제대로 들으란 말이야! 믿을 수 없어! 정말 믿을 수가 없어!! 기다려! <value:$28>!<end>"

## 700228
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セラってさ、それにしても<line-break>きれいな顔してるよね。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ボクって長い髪が似合わないし、<line-break>ああいうのって…<line-break>少し、あこがれるかな。<end>"

## 700229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんかさ、たとえが悪いんだけど　　　<line-break>彼、ア－ギルシャイアに似てない？<line-break>　<line-break>妖艶で…、きれいだけど冷酷で…、<line-break>どことなく影があって…。セラが<line-break>女の子だったらああいう感じだろうな。<end>"
EN: "This may be a poor comparison, but doesn't he resemble Argyleshire? Alluring... beautiful but cruel... with a certain darkness about him... If Sera were a girl, I think he'd be like that.<end>"
KO: "비유가 좀 나쁘긴 한데, 그 사람 아르길샤이어랑 닮지 않았어? 요염하고… 아름답지만 냉혹하고… 어딘가 그늘이 있고…. 세라가 여자라면 그런 느낌일 거야.<end>"

## 700230

## 700231

## 700232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セラやシャリの言葉…<line-break>ボクは役立たずだって言うアレ…<line-break>結構、くるものあるね。<end>"
EN: "What Sera and Shalli said... that I'm useless... It really gets to me.<end>"
KO: "세라나 샤리의 말… 내가 쓸모없다는 그거…. 꽤 가슴에 꽂히네.<end>"

## 700233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、事実だよ。<line-break>ボク、セラほど強くない。　　<line-break>　<line-break>セラは強いし、美しいよ。<line-break>ボクもセラみたいに強くて<line-break>きれいだったらなって思う…。<end>"
EN: "But it's true. I'm not as strong as Sera. Sera is strong, and beautiful. Sometimes I wish I were strong and beautiful like him...<end>"
KO: "하지만 사실이야. 난 세라만큼 강하지 않아. 세라는 강하고 아름다워. 나도 세라처럼 강하고 예뻤으면 좋겠다고 생각해….<end>"

## 700234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんかさ、たとえが悪いんだけど<line-break>彼、ア－ギルシャイアに似てるよね。　<line-break>　<line-break>妖艶で…、きれいだけど冷酷で…、<line-break>どことなく影があって…。<line-break>セラが女だったら、あんな感じかな。<end>"
EN: "This may be a poor comparison, but Sera resembles Argyleshire, doesn't he? Alluring... beautiful but cruel... with a certain darkness about him... If Sera were a woman, I think he'd be like that.<end>"
KO: "비유가 좀 이상하긴 한데, 그 사람 아르길샤이어랑 닮았지? 요염하고… 아름답지만 냉혹하고… 어딘가 그늘이 있고…. 세라가 여자라면 그런 느낌일까.<end>"

## 700235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…アーギルシャイアも<line-break>強くてきれいだよね。<line-break>　<line-break>ボク、弱くて、役に立てないくらいなら<line-break>闇に堕ちても、強くなって、<line-break>キミを…守りたい。<end>"
EN: "...Argyleshire is strong and beautiful too, isn't she? If I'm weak and useless, then even if I have to fall into darkness, I want to become strong enough to... protect you.<end>"
KO: "…아르길샤이어도 강하고 아름답지. 내가 약해서 도움이 안 되느니 차라리 어둠에 떨어져서라도 강해져 널… 지키고 싶어.<end>"

## 700236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボク、フレアが道具として<line-break>役に立てないことをあせる気持ち、　　<line-break>よくわかる気がするんだ。<end>"

## 700238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お星様がきれいだね。<line-break>いつかはお別れする風景。<line-break>だからこそ、いっそういとおしい。<line-break>…しっかりと<line-break>目に焼きつけておかなくちゃ。<line-break>お星様も…、キミの姿もね…。<end>"
EN: "The stars are beautiful. A sight we'll have to leave behind someday. That's why they're all the more precious. I have to etch them into my memory... the stars, and you too...<end>"
KO: "별이 예쁘네. 언젠가는 헤어질 풍경. 그래서 더 사랑스러워. …확실히 눈에 새겨 둬야겠어. 별도… 네 모습도…<end>"

## 700239

## 700240
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 700242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、ぼく、セラが苦手だよ。<line-break>そう、確かにきれいなんだけど　　<line-break>冷たいんだよ、彼。<line-break>あ、そういう点では<line-break>アーギルシャイアと似てるよね。<line-break>きれいだけど、冷たいところ。<end>"
EN: "You know, I have a hard time with Sera. Sure, he's beautiful, but he's cold. Oh, he's like Argyleshire in that way, isn't he? Beautiful, but cold.<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 나 세라가 좀 어려워. 그래, 분명 예쁘긴 한데 차갑잖아. 아, 그런 점에서는 아르길샤이어와 닮았네. 아름답지만 차가운 점.<end>"

## 700243
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 700246
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気づいてた？<line-break>セラとアーギルシャイアって<line-break>似てるよね。<line-break>ねぇ、<value:$28>、<line-break>何かセラから聞いてないの？<end>"

## 700247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そっか、まだ、そこまでの信頼は<line-break>得てはいないんだね。<line-break>　<line-break>最近、セラが<value:$28>に<line-break>ちょっと、心を許したかなって<line-break>思ったんだけどな…。<end>"

KO: "그런가, 아직 그 정도까지 신뢰를 얻진 못했구나. 요즘 세라가 <value:$28>에게 조금 마음을 연 것 같다고 생각했는데….<end>"

## 700248
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セラって、はたから見てて<line-break>感情的に危なっかしいんだ。<line-break>セラの力になってあげてよ。ね？<end>"
EN: "From where I stand, Sera's in a pretty precarious place emotionally. Be there for him, okay?<end>"
KO: "세라는 옆에서 보면 감정적으로 위태로워. 세라의 힘이 되어 줘. 응?<end>"

## 700249
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むかつくんだよな。<line-break>あのセラって奴！<line-break>自分は何様のつもりなんだよ！　　　　<line-break>ちっ、ちょっと強くて<line-break>きれいな顔してるからってよ。<line-break>顔がいい奴なんてろくなのがいないぜ。<end>"
EN: "That Sera guy really gets on my nerves! Who does he think he is? Just because he's a little strong and has a pretty face... Guys with good looks are never any good.<end>"
KO: "짜증 나. 그 세라라는 녀석! 자기가 뭐라도 되는 줄 알아! 쳇, 조금 강하고 얼굴 좀 예쁘다고. 잘생긴 녀석 중 제대로 된 놈은 없다니까.<end>"

## 700250
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "例えば、アーギルシャイアとかさ。<line-break>そう、アーギルシャイアとセラって<line-break>そっくりだぜ。性格の悪いところもな。<line-break>あー、強くなりてえ！<line-break>あんな奴にバカにされないように　　<line-break>強くなりてよな！<end>"
EN: "Take Argyleshire, for example. Yeah, Argyleshire and Sera are exactly alike―right down to their rotten personalities. Man, I want to get stronger! Strong enough that a guy like him can't look down on me!<end>"
KO: "예를 들면 아르길샤이어 같은 거지. 그래, 아르길샤이어랑 세라는 똑같아. 성격 나쁜 것도 말이야. 아, 강해지고 싶다! 저런 녀석한테 무시당하지 않을 만큼 강해지고 싶어!<end>"

## 700251
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>、<line-break>最近、セラと仲がいいみたいだな。<end>"

KO: "…<value:$28>, 요즘 세라랑 사이가 좋은 것 같네.<end>"

## 700252
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのさ、俺、セラについて<line-break>ちょっと気になることがあるんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>セラってさ<line-break>アーギルシャイアに似てないか？<end>"
EN: "You know, there's something about Sera that's been bothering me. Don't you think he resembles Argyleshire?<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 나 세라에 대해 좀 신경 쓰이는 게 있어. 세라 말이야, 아르길샤이어랑 닮지 않았어?<end>"

## 700253
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつ、絶対、あの魔人と<line-break>何か関わりがある…。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>最悪、奴があの魔人のしもべで<line-break>俺たちをねらってるってことも…。<end>"
EN: "He definitely has some connection to that demon... At worst, he might be that demon's servant, targeting us...<end>"
KO: "그 녀석, 분명 그 마인과 뭔가 관계가 있어…. 최악의 경우 놈이 그 마인의 부하라 우리를 노리는 걸 수도….<end>"

## 700254
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無論、最悪の話さ。<line-break>俺もそんな風には思っちゃいない。　　<line-break>　<line-break>だけど、間違いなく、魔人と奴とは<line-break>関わりがある。奴と行動をともにすると<line-break>絶対、魔人と対決することになる。<end>"
EN: "Of course, that's the worst-case scenario. I don't truly believe that. But he definitely has some connection to the demon. If we travel with him, we're bound to face that demon eventually.<end>"
KO: "물론 최악의 경우를 말한 거야. 나도 그렇게 생각하진 않아. 하지만 틀림없이 마인과 그 녀석은 관계가 있어. 그와 함께 행동하면 반드시 마인과 맞서게 될 거다.<end>"

## 700255
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…<line-break>お前は月からの使者としての　　　　　<line-break>使命があるだろ？<line-break>魔人なんかと関わって、<line-break>使命を達成しないうちに<line-break>命を落とすわけにはいかないだろ？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... You have a mission as an emissary from the moon, don't you? You can't get involved with a demon and lose your life before you fulfill it, can you?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>… 너는 달의 사자로서 사명이 있잖아? 마인 같은 것과 얽혀 사명을 이루기 전에 목숨을 잃을 수는 없지?<end>"

## 700256
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…、俺だって　　　　<line-break>お前に命を落とさせたくない。<line-break>俺はお前を守りたいんだ。<line-break>セラと一緒に行動するのをやめてくれ。<line-break>奴と関わっていてもロクなことはない。<line-break>奴を仲間から追い出してくれ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I don't want you to lose your life either. I want to protect you. Stop traveling with Sera. Nothing good will come of associating with him. Make him leave the party.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>… 나도 네가 목숨을 잃는 건 싫어. 난 널 지키고 싶어. 세라와 함께 다니는 걸 그만둬. 그 녀석과 엮여 봐야 좋은 일 없어. 녀석을 동료에서 내보내 줘.<end>"

## 700258
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ただ、これだけは覚えていてくれ。<line-break>俺は純粋にお前のことが守りたいんだ。<line-break>…お前のことが好きだから、な。<end>"
EN: "...But remember this much. I truly do want to protect you. Because I love you.<end>"

## 700267

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 700268
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホントよ。絶対に、彼なら<line-break>あのまま女の子になっても美人よ。　<line-break>　<line-break>ほら、魔人アーギルシャイア！<line-break>あんな感じの妖艶な美女になるわ。<line-break>そう、そういえば彼、そっくりよね。<end>"

## 700269

## 700272
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホントだって。あいつが女だったら<line-break>お前だってかなわないほどの美人だぜ。<line-break>　<line-break>そう、魔人アーギルシャイア！<line-break>あんな感じの妖艶な美女になるぜ！<line-break>そういや、奴、そっくりだよな。<end>"

## 700273

## 700275

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガハハハ！　そうじゃ！<line-break>セラとアーギルシャイアはよく似とる！<end>"
EN: "Gahahaha! That's right! Sera and Argyleshire are very much alike!<end>"
KO: "가하하하! 그렇지! 세라와 아르길샤이어는 아주 닮았어!<end>"

## 700279
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec070-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ、酔うてなぞ、おらんわい！<line-break>あれしきの酒で誰が酔うか！　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ガハハハ！　セラと…、セラと…<line-break>セラと…誰が似とると言うたかの？<line-break>ガハハハ！　ヒック！<end>"
EN: "I-I'm not drunk! Who could get drunk on that little bit of liquor? Gahahaha! Sera and... Sera and... Sera and... who did I say Sera was like again? Gahahaha! Hic!<end>"
KO: "취, 취한 거 아니다! 이 정도 술에 누가 취하냐! 가하하하! 세라랑… 세라랑… 세라랑… 누가 닮았다고 했더라? 가하하하! 딸꾹!<end>"

## 710001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>か。<line-break>話がしたい。　　　　　<end>"
EN: "…<value:$28>. I need to talk to you.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>인가. 할 말이 있다.<end>"

## 710002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺にはひとり、姉がいる。　　　　<line-break>名をシェスターという。<line-break>　<line-break>ロストールの先王フェロヒアが<line-break>ディンガルに侵攻した時、<line-break>幼かった俺たちは両親を失った。<end>"
EN: "I have an older sister. Her name is Shester. When Ferohea, the former king of Rostorl, invaded Dyneskal, we were still children. We lost both our parents.<end>"
KO: "…나에겐 누나가 하나 있다. 이름은 셰스터. 로스톨의 선왕 페로히아가 딩갈을 침공했을 때 어렸던 우리는 부모를 잃었다.<end>"

## 710003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それから、俺たちは<line-break>姉弟ふたりきりで生きてきた。<line-break>姉がいなければ今の俺はいなかった。<line-break>俺にとって、姉は<line-break>かけがえのない存在だった。<line-break>…その姉が姿を消した。<end>"
EN: "From then on, it was just the two of us. I wouldn't be who I am today without her. There was no one more precious to me. Then she disappeared.<end>"
KO: "그 뒤 우리는 남매 둘이서 살아왔다. 누나가 없었다면 지금의 나는 없었어. 나에게 누나는 무엇과도 바꿀 수 없는 존재였다. …그 누나가 사라졌다.<end>"

## 710004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉はディンガル魔道アカデミーを<line-break>卒業したあと、アンティノ商会で<line-break>研究員として働くのだと聞いていた。<line-break>だが、リベルダムのアンティノ商会に<line-break>姉はいなかった…。<line-break>俺は姉を捜して旅を続けた。<end>"
EN: "I'd heard that after graduating from the Dyneskal Academy of Magic, she was going to work as a researcher for the Antino Trading Company. But she wasn't at the Antino Trading Company in Liberdam. So I kept traveling, searching for her.<end>"
KO: "누나는 딘갈 마도 아카데미를 졸업한 뒤 안티노 상회에서 연구원으로 일한다고 들었어. 하지만 리벨덤의 안티노 상회에 누나는 없었지…. 난 누나를 찾아 계속 여행했다.<end>"

## 710005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、その旅の途中　　<line-break>俺はお前に出会った。<line-break>　<line-break>姉は今、非常に危険な奴の<line-break>手の内にある…。<line-break>俺は奴から姉を救い出す。<end>"
EN: "It was during that journey that I met you. My sister is now in the hands of someone extremely dangerous. I will free her.<end>"
KO: "그리고 그 여행 도중 나는 너를 만났다. 누나는 지금 매우 위험한 녀석의 손아귀에 있어…. 난 놈에게서 누나를 구할 거다.<end>"

## 710007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…フッ、…愚痴だな。<line-break>俺としたことが。<end>"
EN: "Hah… Listen to me complain. That's not like me.<end>"
KO: "…훗, …푸념이군. 나답지 않게.<end>"

## 710008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "初めて会ったとき、俺は<line-break>ロイは生きていると言ったな。<end>"
EN: "When we first met, I told you Roy was alive.<end>"
KO: "처음 만났을 때 나는 로이가 살아 있다고 했지.<end>"

## 710009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…それはこの剣、月光が　　　　　　　<line-break>教えてくれたのだ。<line-break>　<line-break>昔、俺とロイは一緒に冒険していた。<line-break>奴は強く、大きな男だった。<line-break>俺は、世界でただあの男だけを認めた。<end>"
EN: "This sword, Moonlight, told me. Long ago, Roy and I traveled together. He was strong, a giant of a man. He was the only man in the world I ever respected.<end>"
KO: "…이 검, 월광이 알려 줬다. 예전에 나와 로이는 함께 모험했어. 녀석은 강하고 큰 사내였지. 난 이 세상에서 오직 그 남자만 인정했다.<end>"

## 710010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はロイと冒険をし、<line-break>一対の宝剣を見つけた。<line-break>…それがこの月光と、ロイの日光だ。　<line-break>聖剣・日光はこの妖刀・月光をかざせば<line-break>反応し、光を発する。<line-break>あのとき試したが、反応がなかった。<end>"
EN: "Roy and I found a pair of treasured swords during our travels: this blade, Moonlight, and Roy's Sunlight. If the Demon Blade Moonlight is held aloft, the Holy Sword Sunlight responds by giving off light. I tried it then, but there was no response.<end>"
KO: "나는 로이와 모험하며 한 쌍의 보검을 찾았다. …그게 이 월광과 로이의 일광이다. 성검·일광은 이 요도·월광을 들이대면 반응해 빛을 낸다. 그때 시험해 봤지만 반응이 없었어.<end>"

## 710011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、闇の者は聖剣・日光に<line-break>触れることすらできない。<line-break>アーギルシャイアには持ち去れない。　<line-break>…つまり、ロイが日光を持って、<line-break>ミイスを去ったのだ。<line-break>自分の足でな…。<end>"
EN: "And beings of darkness cannot so much as touch the Holy Sword Sunlight. Argyleshire could not have taken it. Which means Roy left Miis with Sunlight, on his own two feet.<end>"
KO: "그리고 어둠의 자는 성검 일광에 손조차 댈 수 없다. 아르길샤이어가 가져갈 수는 없어. …즉 로이가 일광을 들고 미이스를 떠난 거야. 자기 발로….<end>"

## 710012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロイは生きている。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>そして、俺の考えに<line-break>間違いなければ…。<end>"
EN: "Roy is alive. And if I'm right...<end>"
KO: "로이는 살아 있다. 그리고 내 생각이 틀리지 않았다면…<end>"

## 710013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いや、間違いならば<line-break>いいのだがな…。<end>"
EN: "No. I only wish I were wrong.<end>"
KO: "…아니, 내가 틀렸다면 좋겠지만….<end>"

## 710014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺にはかつてともに冒険をし、<line-break>生死をともにした連れがいた。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ロイ・ミイスという。<line-break>俺が、世界中で、ただひとり<line-break>認めた男だ。<end>"
EN: "I once had a companion at my side through adventures and brushes with death. His name was Roy Miis. He was the only man in all the world I ever respected.<end>"
KO: "내겐 한때 함께 모험하고 생사를 같이한 동료가 있었다. 로이 미이스라고 하지. 내가 세상에서 유일하게 인정한 남자다.<end>"

## 710015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はロイに姉の捜索を<line-break>助けてもらうべく<line-break>ミイスという隠れ里を訪ねた。　　　　<line-break>ロイはそこで神器を守ると言っていた。<line-break>今思えば、それは闇の神器だったのだ。<line-break>そして、ミイスは奴の標的となった。<end>"
EN: "I went to a hidden village called Miis to ask Roy for help finding my sister. He said he was guarding a divine artifact there. Looking back, it must have been a Dark Artifact. And that made Miis her target.<end>"
KO: "나는 로이에게 누나 수색을 도와달라고 하려고 미이스라는 숨은 마을을 찾아갔다. 로이는 거기서 신기를 지키고 있다고 했다. 지금 생각하면 그건 어둠의 신기였던 거야. 그리고 미이스는 그자의 표적이 됐다.<end>"

## 710016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔人アーギルシャイアは　　　　　　　<line-break>ミイスを襲い、<line-break>闇の神器を奪った。<line-break>俺が着いたときには、奴が持ってきた<line-break>デスギガースという戦闘用モンスターに<line-break>ミイスは焼き尽くされていた。<end>"
EN: "The demon Argyleshire attacked Miis and stole the Dark Artifact. By the time I arrived, Miis had been burned to the ground by a combat monster she had brought with her, called Death Gigas.<end>"
KO: "마인 아르길샤이어는 미이스를 습격해 어둠의 신기를 빼앗았다. 내가 도착했을 때는 녀석이 데려온 데스기가스라는 전투용 몬스터에게 미이스가 전부 불타 버린 뒤였어.<end>"

## 710017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ミイスにロイの姿はなかった。　　　　<line-break>…だが、ロイは死んではいない。<line-break>俺にはそれがわかった。<end>"
EN: "Roy was nowhere to be found in Miis. But he wasn't dead. I knew that much.<end>"
KO: "미이스에 로이의 모습은 없었다. …하지만 로이는 죽지 않았어. 나는 그걸 알 수 있었지.<end>"

## 710018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この剣、妖刀・月光が　　　　　　　　<line-break>俺に教えてくれたのだ。<line-break>　<line-break>俺とロイが一緒に冒険していたころ<line-break>一対の宝剣を見つけた。<line-break>…それがこの月光と、聖剣・日光だ。<end>"
EN: "This sword, the Demon Blade Moonlight, told me. When Roy and I traveled together, we found a pair of treasured swords: the Demon Blade Moonlight and the Holy Sword Sunlight.<end>"
KO: "이 검, 요도 월광이 내게 가르쳐 줬다. 나와 로이가 함께 모험하던 시절 한 쌍의 보검을 발견했지. …그게 바로 이 월광과 성검 일광이다.<end>"

## 710019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺が月光を持ち、ロイが日光を取った。<line-break>この一対の剣は、一方をかざせば、<line-break>もう一方が共鳴し、輝き出す。<line-break>俺は、あのとき、月光をかざした。<line-break>しかし、なんの反応も<line-break>返ってこなかった。<end>"
EN: "I took Moonlight, and Roy took Sunlight. When one of the pair is held aloft, the other resonates with it and begins to shine. At the time, I held Moonlight aloft. But there was no response.<end>"
KO: "내가 월광을 가지고, 로이가 일광을 가져갔어. 한 쌍인 이 검은 한쪽을 들면 다른 한쪽이 공명하며 빛나지. 나는 그때 월광을 들었어. 하지만 아무 반응도 돌아오지 않았어.<end>"

## 710020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "日光は聖剣で魔人など闇の者は<line-break>触れることすらできない。<line-break>アーギルシャイアには持ち去れない。　<line-break>…つまり、ロイが日光を持って、<line-break>ミイスを去ったのだ。<line-break>自分の足でな…。<end>"
EN: "Sunlight is a holy sword. Demons and other beings of darkness cannot so much as touch it. Argyleshire could not have taken it. Which means Roy left Miis with Sunlight, on his own two feet.<end>"
KO: "일광은 성검이라 마인 같은 어둠의 자는 손조차 댈 수 없다. 아르길샤이어가 가져갈 수는 없어. …즉 로이가 일광을 들고 미이스를 떠난 거야. 자기 발로….<end>"

## 710021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そしてロイは再び俺の前に姿を現した。<line-break>アーギルシャイアの下僕、<line-break>サイフォスと名を代えてな。<end>"
EN: "Then Roy appeared before me again, now calling himself Xiphos and serving Argyleshire.<end>"
KO: "그리고 로이는 다시 내 앞에 나타났다. 아르길샤이어의 하인, 사이포스라고 이름까지 바꿔서 말이지.<end>"

## 710022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ。サイフォスこそ<line-break>俺の親友だった男、ロイだ。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Xiphos is Roy, the man who was once my closest friend.<end>"
KO: "그래. 사이포스야말로 내 절친했던 남자, 로이다.<end>"

## 710023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロイは生きている。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>そして、俺の考えに<line-break>間違いなければ…。<end>"
EN: "Roy is alive. And if I'm right...<end>"
KO: "로이는 살아 있다. 그리고 내 생각이 틀리지 않았다면…<end>"

## 710024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様、神聖語…<line-break>失われた古代の言葉に詳しいか？<end>"
EN: "How much do you know about the sacred tongue... the lost language of the ancients?<end>"
KO: "네놈, 신성어… 잃어버린 고대의 언어를 잘 아나?<end>"

## 710025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、まあ、少しなら…<line-break>なんでしょうか？<end>"
EN: "A little, yes... What is it?<end>"
KO: "네, 뭐, 잠깐이라면…. 무슨 일이죠?<end>"

## 710026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…サイフォスというのは<line-break>どういう意味だ。<end>"
EN: "What does “Xiphos” mean?<end>"
KO: "…사이포스라는 건 무슨 뜻이지?<end>"

## 710027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "日蝕、です。　　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "A solar eclipse.<end>"

## 710029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml

## 710030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今、なんと…おっしゃったのです？<line-break>聖杯を持ったゴブリンと戦い、<line-break>勝利したと？<line-break>そうですか…。<line-break>フフ…あのお方も、お喜びに…。<end>"
EN: "What... did you just say? You fought a Goblin carrying the Grail―and won? I see... Heh... The honored one will be pleased as well...<end>"
KO: "지금 뭐라고… 하셨습니까? 성배를 가진 고블린과 싸워 이겼다고요? 그렇군요…. 후후… 그분도 기뻐하시겠군요….<end>"

## 710031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんのつもりです？<line-break>私の剣に触ろうとは無礼ですよ？<end>"
EN: "What is the meaning of this? Trying to touch my sword is most discourteous.<end>"
KO: "무슨 짓입니까? 제 검에 손대려 하다니 무례하군요.<end>"

## 710032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…確かめたかっただけだ。<line-break>こんなふうにな。<end>"
EN: "I merely wanted to be sure. Like this.<end>"
KO: "…확인하고 싶었을 뿐이다. 이렇게 말이지.<end>"

## 710033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の月光に反応する剣…<line-break>それはひとつしかない。<line-break>そして、その剣を持つ男もな…。<end>"
EN: "Only one sword responds to my moonlight. And only one man bears that sword...<end>"
KO: "내 월광에 반응하는 검… 그건 하나뿐이다. 그리고 그 검을 가진 남자도….<end>"

## 710034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "큭!!<end>"

## 710035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…今は、特に、<line-break>お前に言うことはない。　　<line-break>　<line-break>…だが、おそらく状況は<line-break>お前の考えているとおりだ。<end>"
EN: "For now, I have nothing in particular to say to you. But the situation is probably just as you suspect.<end>"

## 710036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 710037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 710038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それによると奴は<line-break>ある女の束縛のもとにあるらしい。<line-break>そして、助けに来てほしいらしい。<line-break>俺の話にシラを切りとおしたのは<line-break>俺たちを危険に巻き込むのを<line-break>恐れてのこと…と書いてある。<end>"
EN: "According to the letter, some woman apparently has him under her control, and he wants me to come help him. It says he pretended not to know what I was talking about because he was afraid of putting us in danger.<end>"
KO: "그 편지에 따르면 녀석은 어떤 여자에게 속박되어 있는 모양이야. 그리고 도와주러 와 달라고 하더군. 내 말에 끝까지 모른 척한 건 우리를 위험에 말려들게 할까 봐 두려웠기 때문이라고 적혀 있어.<end>"

## 710039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何か引っかかるものはあるが、すべては<line-break>この手紙に従い、虹色の山脈まで行けば<line-break>はっきりするだろう。<end>"
EN: "Something about this doesn't sit right, but if we follow the letter and go to the Rainbow Mountains, everything should become clear.<end>"
KO: "뭔가 마음에 걸리긴 하지만, 이 편지대로 무지개빛 산맥까지 가면 모든 게 분명해지겠지.<end>"

## 710040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺は行きたい、　　　　　<line-break>と考えている。<line-break>　<line-break>しかし、共に旅している以上、<line-break>俺はお前の決定に従う。<line-break>勝手に行くつもりはない。<end>"
EN: "I want to go. But as long as we're traveling together, I'll abide by your decision. I won't go off on my own.<end>"
KO: "…나는 가고 싶다고 생각한다. 하지만 함께 여행하는 이상 네 결정에 따르겠다. 멋대로 갈 생각은 없어.<end>"

## 710042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親分、腹減ったゴブ！<line-break>親分、腹減ったゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Boss, I'm hungry, gob! Boss, I'm hungry, gob!<end>"
KO: "두목, 배고프다 고브! 두목, 배고프다 고브!<end>"

## 710043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ～、もう、うるさいゴブ。<line-break>オルナット、黙るゴブ。<line-break>　<line-break>このクエストに失敗したら、<line-break>我らゴブゴブ団は<line-break>面目丸つぶれゴブよ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, shut up already, gob. Ornut, be quiet, gob. If we fail this quest, the Gob-Gob Gang will be utterly humiliated, gob!<end>"
KO: "아아~ 정말 시끄럽다 고브. 오르나트, 조용히 해라 고브. 이 퀘스트에 실패하면 우리 고브고브단 체면이 말이 아니다 고브!<end>"

## 710044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも団長、道に迷ってるゴブよ？<line-break>もう、どうせ間に合わないゴブ！<end>"
EN: "But Captain, we're lost, gob! We're never going to make it in time now, gob!<end>"
KO: "하지만 단장, 길을 잃었다 고브? 이제 어차피 늦었다 고브!<end>"

## 710045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、遅れてもいいというゴブか？<line-break>２年、３年遅れようがいいんでゴブか？<end>"
EN: "So you're saying it's all right to be late, gob? That it wouldn't matter if we were two or three years late, gob?<end>"
KO: "그럼, 늦어도 괜찮다는 고브냐?2년, 3년 늦어도 괜찮다는 고브냐?<end>"

## 710046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（ガント親分は気が立ってるゴブ。<line-break>　ごちゃごちゃ言っちゃダメゴブよ。）<end>"
EN: "(Boss Gant is on edge, gob. Don't start arguing, gob.)<end>"
KO: "(간트 두목은 신경이 곤두서 있다 고브. 이러쿵저러쿵 말하면 안 된다 고브.)<end>"

## 710047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、間に合わないものは　　　　<line-break>もう、しょうがないゴブ！<line-break>　<line-break>それよりも、<line-break>なんかイヤーな予感がするゴブ。<line-break>引き返すべきゴブよ、団長！<end>"
EN: "But if we can't make it in time, there's nothing we can do about it, gob! More importantly, I've got a bad feeling about this, gob. Captain, we should turn back, gob!<end>"
KO: "하지만 늦은 건 이제 어쩔 수 없는 고브! 그보다 뭔가 불길한 예감이 드는 고브. 돌아가야 하는 고브, 단장!<end>"

## 710048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな時間はないゴブ！<line-break>ゴブゴブ団、前進ゴブ！<end>"
EN: "There's no time for that, gob! Gob-Gob Gang, advance, gob!<end>"
KO: "그럴 시간 없다 고브! 고브고브단, 전진 고브!<end>"

## 710049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "아, 너희들! 성배를 돌려줘!<end>"

## 710050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、ガサツ女と<line-break>聖杯をねらう悪い人間ゴブ！<end>"

KO: "으, 거친 여자와 성배를 노리는 나쁜 인간이다 고브!<end>"

## 710051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그, 그러니까 철수하자고 했잖아 고브~!<end>"

## 710052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マルーン！　何を言ってるゴブ！<line-break>自分の幸せは自分で守るものゴブ！<end>"

KO: "마룬! 무슨 소리 하는 고브! 자기 행복은 자기가 지키는 거 고브!<end>"

## 710053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "핫!<end>"

## 710054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、だ、団長！<line-break>じ、自分が間違っていたでゴブ！<line-break>　<line-break>つい、疲れでイライラして団長に<line-break>当たってしまったでゴブ！<line-break>許してほしいゴブ！<end>"
EN: "C-Captain! I-I was wrong, gob! I was tired and irritable, and I ended up taking it out on you, Captain, gob! Please forgive me, gob!<end>"
KO: "다, 단장! 내, 내가 잘못했다 고브! 피곤해서 짜증이 나 단장에게 화풀이했다 고브! 용서해 줘 고브!<end>"

## 710055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかればいい、わかればいいゴブよ。<line-break>マルーン。<line-break>さあ、ゴブゴブ団！<line-break>幸せのために突撃ゴブ！<line-break>聖杯は死守するでゴブよ！<end>"
EN: "As long as you understand, that's all that matters, gob. Maroon. Now, Gob-Gob Gang! Charge for happiness, gob! The Grail must be defended at all costs, gob!<end>"
KO: "알았으면 됐다, 알았으면 됐다 고브. 마룬. 자, 고브고브단! 행복을 위해 돌격이다 고브! 성배는 죽어도 지킨다 고브!<end>"

## 710056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ、やっと…<line-break>やっと聖杯を取り返せたわ！<end>"

KO: "후후, 드디어… 드디어 성배를 되찾았어!<end>"

## 710057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああっ！　聖杯が！<line-break>我らの幸せをもたらすものが！<end>"
EN: "Ah! The Grail! The source of our happiness!<end>"
KO: "아앗! 성배가! 우리에게 행복을 가져다줄 것이!<end>"

## 710058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪い人間に取られてしまったゴブ！<line-break>あれがないと幸せになれないゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Those wicked humans took it, gob! We can't be happy without it, gob!<end>"
KO: "나쁜 인간에게 빼앗겼다 고브! 그게 없으면 행복해질 수 없다 고브!<end>"

## 710059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルナット！　何をするゴブ！<line-break>そこをどくゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Ornut! What are you doing, gob? Get out of the way, gob!<end>"
KO: "오르나트! 뭐 하는 거냐 고브! 비켜라 고브!<end>"

## 710060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聖杯がないと、あの聖杯がないと<line-break>幸せになれないんでゴブよ！<end>"
EN: "Without the Grail―without that Grail―we can't be happy, gob!<end>"
KO: "성배가 없으면, 그 성배가 없으면 행복해질 수 없다 고브!<end>"

## 710061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんな聖杯がなくても<line-break>幸せにはなれるゴブ！！<end>"
EN: "We can be happy without that stupid Grail, gob!!<end>"
KO: "그런 성배가 없어도 행복해질 수 있다 고브!!<end>"

## 710062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我らが聖杯の力を借りたのは　　<line-break>最初だけゴブ！<line-break>　<line-break>あとは、ずーっと<line-break>自分たちの力でやってきたゴブ！<line-break>これからもやれるゴブ！<end>"
EN: "The only time we relied on the Grail's power was at the very beginning, gob! Since then, we've always managed on our own, gob! We can keep doing it, gob!<end>"
KO: "우리가 성배의 힘을 빌린 건 처음뿐이다 고브! 그 뒤로는 계속 우리 힘으로 해 왔다 고브! 앞으로도 할 수 있다 고브!<end>"

## 710063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "逆に今、聖杯を取り戻すために<line-break>親分かマルーンが死んだら<line-break>自分は不幸になるでゴブ。<end>"
EN: "But if the boss or Maroon died now trying to get the Grail back, I'd be miserable, gob.<end>"
KO: "반대로 지금 성배를 되찾으려다 두목이나 마룬이 죽으면 나는 불행해진다 고브.<end>"

## 710064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルナット…。<end>"
EN: "Ornut...<end>"
KO: "오르나트….<end>"

## 710065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのガサツ女たちとも<line-break>本当は仲よくなれたかもしれないゴブ。<line-break>　<line-break>だけど、聖杯のことで争っていたから<line-break>友達にはなれなかったゴブ。<line-break>それは幸せではないゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Maybe we could have been friends with those rough women, gob. But we were fighting over the Grail, so we couldn't be friends, gob. That isn't happiness, gob.<end>"
KO: "그 거친 여자들과도 사실 친해질 수 있었을지 모른다 고브. 하지만 성배 때문에 싸웠으니 친구가 되지 못했다 고브. 그건 행복이 아니다 고브.<end>"

## 710066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…オルナットの言うことが　　　　　　<line-break>今回は正しいゴブ。<line-break>もう、我ら誇り高いゴブゴブ団には<line-break>その聖杯は、必要ないでゴブ！<line-break>別の新しい幸せのために前進するゴブ！<end>"
EN: "This time, Ornut is right, gob. We, the proud Gob-Gob Gang, don't need that Grail anymore, gob! We'll march on toward a new kind of happiness, gob!<end>"
KO: "…오르나트 말이 이번엔 맞다 고브. 이제 우리 자랑스러운 고브고브단에는 그 성배가 필요 없다 고브! 새로운 행복을 위해 전진한다 고브!<end>"

## 710067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聖杯よ！　幸せのきっかけを感謝ゴブ！<line-break>我らは幸せになるゴブ！　さらばゴブ！<end>"
EN: "O Grail! Thank you for setting us on the path to happiness, gob! We will be happy, gob! Farewell, gob!<end>"
KO: "성배여! 행복의 계기를 줘서 감사 고브! 우리는 행복해질 거 고브! 잘 있어라 고브!<end>"

## 710068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セラ…、セラ…<line-break>来てくれたのか…。<end>"

## 710069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ロイ、なのだな。<end>"

## 710070

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セラ…会いたかったぞ。<end>"

## 710071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml

## 710072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お前は確かにロイだ。<line-break>だが、ロイは俺が唯一認めた男なのだ。<line-break>　<line-break>別人格になっていても<line-break>せめて、もう少しましな<line-break>セリフを吐いて欲しかったものだな。<end>"

KO: "…너는 분명 로이다. 하지만 로이는 내가 유일하게 인정한 남자다. 다른 인격이 되었다 해도 적어도 좀 더 나은 말을 해 주길 바랐는데.<end>"

## 710073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ…抜け目ないのね、セラ。<line-break>親友に対しても心を開かないなんて…。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、それってさびしくないのかしら？<line-break>過剰とも言える警戒心…。<line-break>いやされることのない孤独…。ウフフ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... Nothing gets past you, does it, Sera? You won't open your heart even to your closest friend. But isn't that lonely? Such excessive caution... such loneliness that can never be healed... Heh heh.<end>"
KO: "후후후… 빈틈없네, 세라. 친한 친구에게조차 마음을 열지 않다니…. 하지만 그거 외롭지 않아? 지나치다 싶을 정도의 경계심…. 치유될 수 없는 고독…. 후후.<end>"

## 710074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなものはどうでもいい。<line-break>姉を返してもらおう。<end>"
EN: "None of that matters. Give me back my sister.<end>"
KO: "그런 건 아무래도 좋아. 누나를 돌려받겠다.<end>"

## 710075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イヤだっていったら？<end>"
EN: "And if I refuse?<end>"
KO: "싫다고 하면?<end>"

## 710076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前の生命をもらってから<line-break>姉シェスターを取り戻す。<end>"
EN: "I'll take your life, then take my sister Shester back.<end>"
KO: "네 목숨을 받은 뒤 누나 셰스터를 되찾겠다.<end>"

## 710077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、恐い。<line-break>くすっ、じゃあ、私も私の欲しいものを<line-break>同じ方法でいただくことにするわ。<line-break>私が操っている親友に<line-break>殺されるという屈辱的な死によってね。<end>"
EN: "My, how frightening. Heh heh. Then I'll take what I want the same way. I'll have your closest friend, under my control, kill you. What a humiliating way to die.<end>"
KO: "어머, 무서워라. 후후, 그럼 나도 내가 원하는 걸 같은 방법으로 가져갈게. 내가 조종하는 친한 친구에게 살해당하는 굴욕적인 죽음으로 말이지.<end>"

## 710078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、ホント、容赦ないのね。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. You truly are merciless.<end>"
KO: "후훗, 정말 봐주질 않네.<end>"

## 710079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、この美しい肉体を持った私を<line-break>果たして、あなたは、斬れるのかしら？<end>"
EN: "But can you really bring yourself to cut me down in this beautiful body?<end>"
KO: "하지만 이 아름다운 육체를 지닌 나를 과연 당신이 베어 버릴 수 있을까?<end>"

## 710080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この月光は精神を斬れる。<line-break>…心配は無用だ。<line-break>　<line-break>死にたくなければ、<line-break>まず、姉を返すのだな。<line-break>アーギルシャイア！<end>"
EN: "This Moonlight can cut the spirit. You needn't worry. If you don't want to die, return my sister first. Argyleshire!<end>"
KO: "이 월광은 정신을 벨 수 있다. …걱정할 것 없다. 죽고 싶지 않으면 우선 누나를 돌려줘라. 아르길샤이어!<end>"

## 710081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホント…昔から人間って　　　　<line-break>好きになれなかったわ…。<line-break>弱いくせに、生意気で<line-break>いつでも裏をかくことを考えてて<line-break>油断ならなくて…。<end>"
EN: "I truly never have liked humans... Weak, yet insolent, always scheming to outwit others. You can never let your guard down around them...<end>"
KO: "정말… 옛날부터 인간은 좋아할 수가 없었어…. 약하면서 건방지고, 늘 뒤통수칠 생각이나 하고, 방심할 수가 없어서….<end>"

## 710082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "分をわきまえなさい！<line-break>あなたたちは私のオモチャなの。　　<line-break>　<line-break>暇つぶしに殺そうと思って、<line-break>断末魔の悲鳴を楽しもうと思って、<line-break>ここに呼んだだけの存在なの。<end>"
EN: "Know your place! You are my toys. I brought you here for no other reason than to kill you for amusement and savor your dying screams.<end>"
KO: "분수를 알아! 너희는 내 장난감이야. 심심풀이로 죽이고, 단말마의 비명을 즐기려고 여기 부른 것뿐이야.<end>"

## 710083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それなのに私を傷つけ、<line-break>かわいいロイも傷つけて…。<line-break>少し、おイタが過ぎるわね。<end>"
EN: "And yet you hurt me, and dear Roy as well... You've been rather too naughty.<end>"
KO: "그런데도 나를 다치게 하고 귀여운 로이까지 다치게 했지…. 장난이 좀 지나치네.<end>"

## 710084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、無様ね。もう少しは<line-break>楽しませてくれると思ったけど。<line-break>おもしろくないオモチャは<line-break>消えなさい。<line-break>ウフフフ！<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. How pathetic. I thought you might entertain me a little longer. Boring toys should simply disappear. Ah ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "후후, 꼴사납네. 조금은 즐겁게 해 줄 줄 알았는데. 재미없는 장난감은 사라져. 우후후후!<end>"

## 710086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聖杯はいただいておくわ。<line-break>私、作りたいものがあるから。<end>"
EN: "I'll be taking the Grail. There's something I want to make.<end>"
KO: "성배는 내가 가져갈게. 만들고 싶은 게 있거든.<end>"

## 710087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親分、腹減ったゴブ！<line-break>親分、腹減ったゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Boss, I'm hungry, gob! Boss, I'm hungry, gob!<end>"
KO: "두목, 배고프다 고브! 두목, 배고프다 고브!<end>"

## 710088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ～、もう、うるさいゴブ。<line-break>オルナット、黙るゴブ。<line-break>　<line-break>このクエストに失敗したら、<line-break>我らゴブゴブ団は<line-break>面目丸つぶれゴブよ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, enough! You're too noisy, gob. Ornut, be quiet, gob. If we fail this quest, the Gob-Gob Gang will be utterly disgraced, gob!<end>"
KO: "아아~ 정말 시끄럽다 고브. 오르나트, 조용히 해라 고브. 이 퀘스트에 실패하면 우리 고브고브단 체면이 말이 아니다 고브!<end>"

## 710089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも団長、道に迷ってるゴブよ？<line-break>もう、どうせ間に合わないゴブ！<end>"
EN: "But Captain, we're lost, gob! We'll never make it in time now, gob!<end>"
KO: "하지만 단장, 길을 잃었다 고브? 이제 어차피 늦었다 고브!<end>"

## 710090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、遅れてもいいというゴブか？<line-break>２年、３年遅れようがいいんでゴブか？<end>"
EN: "So you're saying it's fine to be late, gob? That it doesn't matter if we're two or three years late, gob?<end>"
KO: "그럼, 늦어도 괜찮다는 고브냐?2년, 3년 늦어도 괜찮다는 고브냐?<end>"

## 710091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（ガント親分は気が立ってるゴブ。<line-break>　ごちゃごちゃ言っちゃダメゴブよ。）<end>"
EN: "(Boss Gant is on edge, gob. Don't argue with him, gob.)<end>"
KO: "(간트 두목은 신경이 곤두서 있다 고브. 이러쿵저러쿵 말하면 안 된다 고브.)<end>"

## 710092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、間に合わないものは　　　　<line-break>もう、しょうがないゴブ！<line-break>　<line-break>それよりも、<line-break>なんかイヤーな予感がするゴブ。<line-break>引き返すべきゴブよ、団長！<end>"
EN: "But if we can't make it in time, there's nothing we can do, gob! Besides, I've got a really bad feeling, gob. We should turn back, Captain!<end>"
KO: "하지만 늦은 건 이제 어쩔 수 없는 고브! 그보다 뭔가 불길한 예감이 드는 고브. 돌아가야 하는 고브, 단장!<end>"

## 710093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな時間はないゴブ！<line-break>ゴブゴブ団、前進ゴブ！<end>"

KO: "그럴 시간 없다 고브! 고브고브단, 전진 고브!<end>"

## 710094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Hm! Bad humans after the Grail, gob!<end>"
KO: "으, 성배를 노리는 나쁜 인간들 고브!<end>"

## 710095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、だ、だから、<line-break>引き上げようって言ったゴブ～！<end>"
EN: "Th-That's why I said we should turn back, gob!<end>"
KO: "그, 그러니까 철수하자고 했잖아 고브~!<end>"

## 710096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "마룬! 무슨 소리 하는 고브! 자기 행복은 자기가 지키는 거 고브!<end>"

## 710097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml

KO: "핫!<end>"

## 710098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、だ、団長！<line-break>じ、自分が間違っていたでゴブ！<line-break>　<line-break>つい、疲れでイライラして団長に<line-break>当たってしまったでゴブ！<line-break>許してほしいゴブ！<end>"
EN: "C-Captain! I-I was wrong, gob! I was tired and irritable, and I took it out on you, gob! Please forgive me, gob!<end>"
KO: "다, 단장! 내, 내가 잘못했다 고브! 피곤해서 짜증이 나 단장에게 화풀이했다 고브! 용서해 줘 고브!<end>"

## 710099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかればいい、<line-break>わかればいいゴブよ。　　<line-break>マルーン。　<line-break>さあ、ゴブゴブ団！<line-break>幸せのために突撃ゴブ！<line-break>聖杯を死守するでゴブよ！<end>"
EN: "As long as you understand, gob. Maroon. Now, Gob-Gob Gang! Charge for happiness, gob! Defend the Grail with your lives, gob!<end>"
KO: "알았으면 됐다, 알았으면 됐다 고브. 마룬.<end>"

## 710100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああっ！　聖杯が！<line-break>我らの幸せをもたらすものが！<end>"
EN: "Ah! The Grail! The thing that brings us happiness!<end>"
KO: "아앗! 성배가! 우리에게 행복을 가져다줄 것이!<end>"

## 710101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪い人間に取られてしまったゴブ！<line-break>あれがないと幸せになれないゴブ！<end>"

## 710102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルナット！　何をするゴブ！<line-break>そこをどくゴブ！<end>"

KO: "오르나트! 뭐 하는 거냐 고브! 비켜라 고브!<end>"

## 710103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聖杯がないと、あの聖杯がないと<line-break>幸せになれないんでゴブよ！<end>"
EN: "Without the Grail―without that Grail―we can't be happy, gob!<end>"
KO: "성배가 없으면, 그 성배가 없으면 행복해질 수 없다 고브!<end>"

## 710104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんな聖杯がなくても<line-break>幸せにはなれるゴブ！！<end>"
EN: "We can be happy without that Grail, gob!!<end>"
KO: "그런 성배가 없어도 행복해질 수 있다 고브!!<end>"

## 710105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我らが聖杯の力を借りたのは　　<line-break>最初だけゴブ！<line-break>　<line-break>あとは、ずーっと<line-break>自分たちの力でやってきたゴブ！<line-break>これからもやれるゴブ！<end>"
EN: "We only borrowed the Grail's power at the very beginning, gob! Ever since, we've done everything on our own, gob! We can keep it up, gob!<end>"
KO: "우리가 성배의 힘을 빌린 건 처음뿐이다 고브! 그 뒤로는 계속 우리 힘으로 해 왔다 고브! 앞으로도 할 수 있다 고브!<end>"

## 710106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "逆に今、聖杯を取り戻すために<line-break>親分かマルーンが死んだら<line-break>自分は不幸になるでゴブ。<end>"
EN: "If Boss or Maroon dies now while we're trying to get the Grail back, I'll be unhappy, gob.<end>"
KO: "반대로 지금 성배를 되찾으려다 두목이나 마룬이 죽으면 나는 불행해진다 고브.<end>"

## 710107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルナット…。<end>"
EN: "Ornut...<end>"
KO: "오르나트….<end>"

## 710108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの変な人間たちとも<line-break>本当は仲よくなれたかもしれないゴブ。<line-break>　<line-break>だけど、聖杯のことで争っていたから<line-break>友達にはなれなかったゴブ。<line-break>それは幸せではないゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Maybe we could have become friends with those strange humans, gob. But we fought over the Grail, so we couldn't be friends, gob. That's not happiness, gob.<end>"
KO: "그 이상한 인간들과도 사실 친해질 수 있었을지 모른다 고브. 하지만 성배 때문에 싸웠으니 친구가 되지 못했다 고브. 그건 행복이 아니다 고브.<end>"

## 710109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…オルナットの言うことが　　　　　　<line-break>今回は正しいゴブ。<line-break>　<line-break>もう、我ら誇り高いゴブゴブ団には<line-break>その聖杯は、必要ないゴブ！<line-break>別の新しい幸せのために前進するゴブ！<end>"
EN: "This time, Ornut is right, gob. The proud Gob-Gob Gang no longer needs that Grail, gob! We'll march on toward a new and different happiness, gob!<end>"
KO: "…오르나트 말이 이번엔 맞다 고브. 이제 우리 긍지 높은 고브고브단에게 그 성배는 필요 없다 고브! 다른 새로운 행복을 위해 전진한다 고브!<end>"

## 710110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聖杯よ！　幸せのきっかけを感謝ゴブ！<line-break>我らは幸せになるゴブ！　さらばゴブ！<end>"
EN: "O Grail! Thank you for setting us on the path to happiness, gob! We will be happy, gob! Farewell, gob!<end>"
KO: "성배여! 행복의 계기를 줘서 감사 고브! 우리는 행복해질 거 고브! 잘 있어라 고브!<end>"

## 710111

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セラ…、セラ…<line-break>来てくれたのか…。<end>"

KO: "세라… 세라… 와 준 건가….<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml

## 710115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お前は確かにロイだ。<line-break>だが、ロイは俺が唯一認めた男なのだ。<line-break>　<line-break>別人格になっていても<line-break>せめて、もう少しましな<line-break>セリフを吐いて欲しかったものだな。<end>"
EN: "You are Roy, that much is certain. But Roy is the only man I have ever acknowledged. Even if another personality has taken over, I would have expected something better from his lips.<end>"
KO: "…너는 분명 로이다. 하지만 로이는 내가 유일하게 인정한 남자다. 다른 인격이 되었다 해도 적어도 좀 더 나은 말을 해 주길 바랐는데.<end>"

## 710116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ…、抜け目ないのね、セラ。<line-break>親友に対しても心を開かないなんて…。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、それってさびしくないのかしら？<line-break>過剰とも言える警戒心…。<line-break>いやされることのない孤独…。ウフフ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... Ever vigilant, aren't you, Sera? You won't open your heart even to your closest friend. But isn't that lonely? That almost excessive wariness... that loneliness nothing can soothe... Heh heh.<end>"
KO: "후후후… 빈틈이 없구나, 세라. 친한 친구에게도 마음을 열지 않다니…. 하지만 그거 외롭지 않아? 지나치다 싶을 만큼의 경계심…. 치유되지 않는 고독…. 우후후.<end>"

## 710117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなものはどうでもいい。<line-break>姉を返してもらおう。<end>"
EN: "None of that matters. Give me back my sister.<end>"
KO: "그런 건 아무래도 좋아. 누나를 돌려받겠다.<end>"

## 710118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イヤだっていったら？<end>"
EN: "And if I say no?<end>"
KO: "싫다고 하면?<end>"

## 710119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前の生命をもらってから<line-break>姉シェスターを取り戻す。<end>"
EN: "I'll take your life, then take back my sister, Shester.<end>"
KO: "네 목숨을 받은 뒤 누나 셰스터를 되찾겠다.<end>"

## 710120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、恐い。<line-break>くすっ、じゃあ、私も私の欲しいものを<line-break>同じ方法でいただくことにするわ。<line-break>私が操っている親友に殺されるという<line-break>あなたの屈辱的な死によってね。<line-break>くすっ。<end>"
EN: "My, how frightening. Hee hee. Then I'll take what I want the same way: through the humiliating death you'll suffer at the hands of your own closest friend, whom I control. Hee hee.<end>"
KO: "어머, 무서워라. 후후, 그럼 나도 내가 원하는 걸 같은 방법으로 얻도록 하지. 내가 조종하는 친한 친구에게 죽는다는 굴욕적인 죽음으로 말이야. 후후.<end>"

## 710121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、ホント、容赦ないのね。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. You truly show no mercy.<end>"
KO: "후훗, 정말 봐주질 않네.<end>"

## 710122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、この美しい肉体を持った私を<line-break>果たして、あなたは、斬れるのかしら？<end>"
EN: "But can you really bring yourself to cut me down in this beautiful body?<end>"
KO: "하지만 이 아름다운 육체를 지닌 나를 과연 당신이 베어 버릴 수 있을까?<end>"

## 710123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この月光は精神を斬れる。<line-break>…心配は、無用だ。<line-break>　<line-break>死にたくなければ、<line-break>まず、姉を返すのだな。<line-break>アーギルシャイア！<end>"
EN: "Moonlight can cut the spirit. There is no cause for concern. If you wish to live, return my sister first, Argyleshire!<end>"
KO: "이 월광은 정신을 벨 수 있다. …걱정은 필요 없다. 죽고 싶지 않다면 먼저 누나를 돌려줘라. 아르길샤이어!<end>"

## 710124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホント…昔から人間って　　　　<line-break>好きになれなかったわ…。<line-break>　<line-break>弱いくせに、生意気で<line-break>いつでも裏をかくことを考えてて<line-break>油断ならなくて…。<end>"
EN: "I truly never could stand humans... Weak, yet insolent. Always plotting some way to outwit you. Never to be trusted...<end>"
KO: "정말… 옛날부터 인간은 좋아할 수가 없었어…. 약하면서 건방지고, 늘 뒤통수칠 생각이나 하고, 방심할 수가 없어서….<end>"

## 710125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "分をわきまえなさい！<line-break>あなたたちは私のオモチャなの。　　<line-break>　<line-break>暇つぶしに殺そうと思って、<line-break>断末魔の悲鳴を楽しもうと思って、<line-break>ここに呼んだだけの存在なの。<end>"
EN: "Know your place! You are my toys. I brought you here merely to kill you for amusement and enjoy your dying screams.<end>"
KO: "분수를 알아! 너희는 내 장난감이야. 심심풀이로 죽이고, 단말마의 비명을 즐기려고 여기 부른 것뿐이야.<end>"

## 710126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが聖杯を持ってきたお礼に<line-break>機嫌をよくして遊んであげてたら、　<line-break>いい気になって…。<line-break>私の本体を傷つけて…。<line-break>私の操り人形のロイを傷つけて…。<line-break>おイタが過ぎるわ、調教が必要ね…。<end>"
EN: "But you brought me the Grail, so I was in a good mood and decided to play with you. Then you grew presumptuous... You wounded my true body... You wounded Roy, my puppet... This misbehavior has gone far enough. You need to be disciplined...<end>"
KO: "성배를 가져온 보답으로 기분 좋게 놀아 줬더니 우쭐해져서는…. 내 본체를 다치게 하고…. 내 꼭두각시 로이까지 다치게 하고…. 장난이 지나치네. 조교가 필요하겠어….<end>"

## 710127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、無様ね。もう少しは<line-break>楽しませてくれると思ったけど。<line-break>　<line-break>おもしろくないオモチャは<line-break>消えなさい。<line-break>ウフフフ！<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. How pathetic. I thought you might entertain me a little longer. A boring toy should simply disappear. Ah ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "후후, 꼴사납네. 조금은 즐겁게 해 줄 줄 알았는데. 재미없는 장난감은 사라져. 우후후후!<end>"

## 710128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、最後だし、楽しませてもらうわ。<line-break>じっくり、じらしながら殺してあげる。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Since this is the end, I may as well enjoy myself. I'll take my time killing you, drawing it out.<end>"
KO: "후후, 마지막이니 즐겨 주겠어. 천천히 애태우면서 죽여 줄게.<end>"

## 710129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "させないっ！<end>"
EN: "I won't let you!<end>"
KO: "그렇게는 못 해!<end>"

## 710130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "早く逃げて！<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Run, <value:$28>! Hurry!<end>"
KO: "빨리 도망쳐! <value:$28>!<end>"

## 710131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何よ！？　この娘！<line-break>離れなさい！<end>"
EN: "What?! You! Get away from me, girl!<end>"
KO: "뭐야!? 이 여자애! 떨어져!<end>"

## 710132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>！<line-break>何やってんの！　早く逃げて！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! What are you doing? Hurry up and run!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>! 뭐 하는 거야! 빨리 도망쳐!<end>"

## 710133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、逃げられたのかしら？<line-break>今度こそ楽しめると思ったのに…。<line-break>　<line-break>残念だけど、いいわ。<line-break>じゃ、聖杯はいただいておくわね。<line-break>あなたたちには不要でしょう？<end>"
EN: "Another escape? I thought I'd finally have some fun this time... A pity, but never mind. I'll be taking the Grail, then. You don't need it anymore, do you?<end>"
KO: "또 도망친 걸까? 이번에는 즐길 수 있을 줄 알았는데…. 아쉽지만 됐어. 그럼 성배는 받아 갈게. 너희한테는 필요 없잖아?<end>"

## 710134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…アイリーンは<line-break>どうした？<end>"
EN: "What happened to Irene?<end>"
KO: "…아이린은 어떻게 됐지?<end>"

## 710135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうか。<line-break>お前が目を覚ましたときには<line-break>もういなかったか…。<line-break>アーギルシャイアに奪われた<line-break>聖杯を追ったのか、<line-break>あるいは…。<end>"
EN: "I see. Irene was already gone when you woke up... Perhaps she went after the Grail Argyleshire stole. Or perhaps...<end>"
KO: "…그런가. 네가 눈을 떴을 때는 이미 없었나… 아르길샤이어에게 빼앗긴 성배를 쫓은 건가, 아니면…<end>"

## 710136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…来い。<line-break>…俺は何があっても　<line-break>姉を取り戻す。<line-break>…お前もあいつに<line-break>つけるべき、<line-break>けじめがあるだろう。<end>"
EN: "Come. No matter what happens, I will get my sister back. You have unfinished business to settle with her as well, don't you?<end>"
KO: "…와라. …나는 무슨 일이 있어도 누나를 되찾겠다. …너도 녀석에게 매듭지어야 할 일이 있잖아.<end>"

## 710137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前は先に行っていろ。<line-break>俺は少し奴に話がある。<end>"
EN: "Go on ahead. I need to have a word with that one.<end>"
KO: "너는 먼저 가 있어라. 나는 녀석과 할 얘기가 조금 있다.<end>"

## 710138

KO: "<value:$28>인가.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "虹色の山脈…。<line-break>そこへ行けば、すべてが<line-break>わかるというのか…。<end>"
EN: "The Rainbow Mountains... Is that where everything will become clear...?<end>"
KO: "무지개빛 산맥…. 그곳에 가면 모든 걸 알 수 있다는 건가….<end>"

## 710140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml

## 710141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、おい！　すごいぞ！<line-break>さっき酒場からゴブリンが出てきたんだ！　<line-break>それも３匹！　しかもしゃべってた！！<line-break>町の門の方に走っていったから<line-break>もう町にはいないんだろうけど、<line-break>いや、まったく、世の中って不思議だよな。<end>"
EN: "H-hey! You won't believe this! Three Goblins just came out of the tavern―and they could talk! They ran toward the town gate, so they're probably gone by now, but still... what a strange world we live in.<end>"
KO: "이, 이봐! 대단해! 아까 술집에서 고블린이 나왔어! 세 마리나! 게다가 말을 하더라고!! 마을 문 쪽으로 달려갔으니 이제 마을엔 없겠지만, 정말 세상은 신기하구나.<end>"

## 710142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたも見たかい？<line-break>さっき、しゃべるゴブリンが３匹も<line-break>酒場から出てきたんだよ。<line-break>もう町を出ただろうとはいっても、<line-break>酒場にゴブリンがいただなんて<line-break>衛生的に大丈夫なのかねえ？<end>"
EN: "Did you see them too? Three talking Goblins came out of the tavern just now. They've probably left town by now, but can the tavern really be sanitary after three Goblins have been in there?<end>"
KO: "너도 봤니? 아까 말하는 고블린이 세 마리나 술집에서 나오더라. 벌써 마을을 나갔다고 해도 술집에 고블린이 있었다니 위생은 괜찮은 건가 몰라.<end>"

## 710143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近は、エルフやリルビーだけでなく<line-break>ゴブリンも町にいるようじゃ。<line-break>今さっき、はじめて見たんじゃが……。<line-break>しかも、しゃべっておったのじゃ。<line-break>ひとまずこの町を離れるゴブ！　とな。<line-break>何があったんじゃろうな。<end>"
EN: "It seems one finds Goblins in town these days, not just Elves and Lilubees. I saw them for the first time only moments ago... and they could speak, too. “For now, we leave this town, gob!” they said. I wonder what happened.<end>"
KO: "요즘은 엘프나 릴비뿐 아니라 고블린도 마을에 있는 모양이야. 방금 처음 봤는데…. 게다가 말까지 하더군. '일단 이 마을을 떠나는 고브!'라던데. 무슨 일이 있었던 걸까.<end>"

## 710144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ！　ねえねえねえ！　見た！？　　　<line-break>すごいわ！しゃべるゴブリンよ！<line-break>　<line-break>もう町から出て行くみたいだったから<line-break>見た人は少ないかもしれないわ。<line-break>さっそくみんなに教えてあげなくっちゃ！<end>"
EN: "Hey! Hey, hey, hey! Did you see them?! Amazing! Talking Goblins! They looked like they were leaving town, so not many people may have seen them. I have to go tell everyone right away!<end>"
KO: "있잖아! 있잖아 있잖아! 봤어!? 대단해! 말하는 고블린이야! 이제 마을에서 나가는 것 같아서 본 사람은 적을지도 몰라. 얼른 모두에게 알려 줘야지!<end>"

## 710145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec071-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待て、<value:$28>。<line-break>あの魔人はこの森の奥に<line-break>いるはずだ。<line-break>外に出るのなら<line-break>この森を探してからにしろ。<end>"
EN: "Wait, <value:$28>. That demon should be somewhere deeper in the forest. If you're going to leave, search the forest first.<end>"
KO: "기다려, <value:$28>. 그 마인은 이 숲 안쪽에 있을 거다. 밖으로 나갈 거라면 이 숲부터 찾아봐.<end>"

## 720001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親分、腹減ったゴブ！<line-break>親分、腹減ったゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Boss, I'm hungry, gob! Boss, I'm hungry, gob!<end>"
KO: "두목, 배고프다 고브! 두목, 배고프다 고브!<end>"

## 720002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ～、もう、うるさいゴブ。<line-break>オルナット、黙るゴブ。<line-break>　<line-break>このクエストに失敗したら、<line-break>我らゴブゴブ団は<line-break>面目丸つぶれゴブよ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, enough! You're too noisy, gob. Ornut, be quiet, gob. If we fail this quest, the Gob-Gob Gang will be utterly disgraced, gob!<end>"
KO: "아아~ 정말 시끄럽다 고브. 오르나트, 조용히 해라 고브. 이 퀘스트에 실패하면 우리 고브고브단 체면이 말이 아니다 고브!<end>"

## 720003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも団長、道に迷ってるゴブよ？<line-break>もう、どうせ間に合わないゴブ！<end>"
EN: "But Captain, we're lost, gob! We're not going to make it in time anyway, gob!<end>"
KO: "하지만 단장, 길을 잃었다 고브? 이제 어차피 늦었다 고브!<end>"

## 720004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、遅れてもいいと言うゴブか？<line-break>２年、３年遅れようがいいんでゴブか？<end>"
EN: "So you're saying it's fine to be late, gob? That it doesn't matter if we're two or three years late, gob?<end>"
KO: "그럼, 늦어도 괜찮다는 고브냐?2년, 3년 늦어도 괜찮다는 고브냐?<end>"

## 720005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（ガント親分は気が立ってるゴブ。<line-break>　ごちゃごちゃ言っちゃダメゴブよ。）<end>"
EN: "(Boss Gant is on edge, gob. Better not argue with him, gob.)<end>"
KO: "(간트 두목은 신경이 곤두서 있다 고브. 이러쿵저러쿵 말하면 안 된다 고브.)<end>"

## 720006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、間に合わないものは　　　　<line-break>もう、しょうがないゴブ！<line-break>　<line-break>それよりも、<line-break>なんかイヤーな予感がするゴブよ。<line-break>引き返すべきゴブよ、団長！<end>"
EN: "But if we can't make it in time, there's nothing we can do, gob! Besides, I've got a really bad feeling about this, gob. We should turn back, Captain!<end>"
KO: "하지만 늦은 건 이제 어쩔 수 없다 고브! 그보다 뭔가 싫은 예감이 든다 고브. 돌아가야 한다 고브, 단장!<end>"

## 720007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 720009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 720010

## 720012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、だ、団長！<line-break>じ、自分が間違っていたでゴブ！<line-break>　<line-break>つい、疲れでイライラして団長に<line-break>当たってしまったでゴブ！<line-break>許してほしいゴブ！<end>"

## 720013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかればいい、<line-break>わかればいいゴブよ。　　<line-break>マルーン。<line-break>さあ、ゴブゴブ団！<line-break>幸せのために突撃ゴブ！<line-break>聖杯は死守するゴブよ！<end>"
EN: "As long as you understand, Maroon. That's what matters, gob. Now, Gob-Gob Gang! Charge for happiness, gob! We defend the Grail at all costs, gob!<end>"

## 720014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "아앗! 성배가! 우리에게 행복을 가져다줄 것이!<end>"

## 720015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 720016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml

## 720017

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 720018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 720019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我らが聖杯の力を借りたのは　　<line-break>最初だけゴブ！<line-break>　<line-break>あとは、ずーっと<line-break>自分たちの力でやってきたゴブ！<line-break>これからもやれるゴブ！<end>"
EN: "We only borrowed the Grail's power at the very beginning, gob! Ever since then, we've managed on our own, gob! And we can keep doing it, gob!<end>"
KO: "우리가 성배의 힘을 빌린 건 처음뿐이다 고브! 그 뒤로는 계속 우리 힘으로 해 왔다 고브! 앞으로도 할 수 있다 고브!<end>"

## 720020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 720021

## 720022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの変な人間たちとも<line-break>本当は仲よくなれたかもしれないゴブ。<line-break>　<line-break>だけど、聖杯のことで争っていたから<line-break>友達にはなれなかったゴブ。<line-break>それは幸せではないゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Maybe we could have become friends with those strange humans, gob. But we fought over the Grail, so we couldn't be friends, gob. That's not happiness, gob.<end>"
KO: "그 이상한 인간들과도 사실 친해질 수 있었을지 모른다 고브. 하지만 성배 때문에 싸웠으니 친구가 되지 못했다 고브. 그건 행복이 아니다 고브.<end>"

## 720023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…オルナットの言うことが　　　　　　<line-break>今回は正しいゴブ。<line-break>　<line-break>もう、我ら誇り高いゴブゴブ団には<line-break>その聖杯は、必要ないゴブ！<line-break>別の新しい幸せのために前進するゴブ！<end>"
EN: "This time, Ornut is right, gob. The proud Gob-Gob Gang no longer needs that Grail, gob! We'll march on toward a new and different happiness, gob!<end>"
KO: "…오르나트 말이 이번엔 맞다 고브. 이제 우리 긍지 높은 고브고브단에게 그 성배는 필요 없다 고브! 다른 새로운 행복을 위해 전진한다 고브!<end>"

## 720024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聖杯よ！　幸せのきっかけを感謝ゴブ！<line-break>我らは幸せになるゴブ！　さらばゴブ！<end>"
EN: "O Grail! Thank you for setting us on the path to happiness, gob! We will be happy, gob! Farewell, gob!<end>"

## 720025

## 720029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お前は確かにロイだ。<line-break>だが、ロイは俺が唯一認めた男なのだ。<line-break>　<line-break>別人格になっていても<line-break>せめて、もう少しましなセリフを<line-break>吐いてほしかったものだな。<end>"
EN: "You certainly are Roy. But Roy is the only man who has ever earned my respect. Even if you're a different person now, I would have expected a somewhat better line from you.<end>"
KO: "…너는 분명 로이다. 하지만 로이는 내가 유일하게 인정한 남자다. 다른 인격이 되었다 해도 적어도 좀 더 나은 말을 해 주길 바랐는데.<end>"

## 720030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ…、抜け目ないのね、セラ。<line-break>親友に対しても心を開かないなんて…。<line-break>　<line-break>でも…それってさびしくないのかしら？<line-break>過剰とも言える警戒心…<line-break>いやされることのない孤独…、ウフフ。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee... Ever wary, aren't you, Sera? You won't open your heart even to your closest friend... But doesn't that get lonely? Such excessive vigilance... such an incurable loneliness... Hee hee.<end>"
KO: "후후후… 빈틈없네, 세라. 친한 친구에게조차 마음을 열지 않다니…. 하지만… 그거 외롭지 않아? 지나치다 싶을 정도의 경계심… 치유될 수 없는 고독… 후후.<end>"

## 720031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなものはどうでもいい。<line-break>姉を返してもらおう。<end>"

## 720032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イヤだって言ったら？<end>"
EN: "And if I refuse?<end>"
KO: "싫다고 하면?<end>"

## 720033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前の生命をもらってから<line-break>姉シェスターを取り戻す。<end>"
EN: "I'll take your life, then take back my sister Shester.<end>"
KO: "네 목숨을 받은 뒤 누나 셰스터를 되찾겠다.<end>"

## 720034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、恐い。<line-break>くすっ、じゃあ、私も私の欲しいものを<line-break>同じ方法でいただくことにするわ。<line-break>私が操っている親友に殺されるという<line-break>あなたにとっては屈辱的な方法でね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, how frightening. Hee hee. Then I'll take what I want in the same way: by having your close friend, now under my control, kill you. What a humiliating way for you to die.<end>"
KO: "어머, 무서워라. 후훗, 그럼 나도 내가 원하는 걸 같은 방법으로 가져가기로 하지. 내가 조종하는 절친한 친구에게 죽는다는, 너에게는 굴욕적인 방법으로 말이야.<end>"

## 720035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、ホント、容赦ないのね。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. You truly are merciless.<end>"
KO: "후훗, 정말 봐주질 않네.<end>"

## 720036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、この美しい肉体を持った私を<line-break>果たして、あなたは斬れるのかしら？<end>"
EN: "But can you truly cut me down while I possess this beautiful body?<end>"
KO: "하지만 이 아름다운 육체를 가진 나를 과연 네가 벨 수 있을까?<end>"

## 720037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この月光は精神を斬れる。<line-break>…心配は、無用だ。<line-break>　<line-break>死にたくなければ<line-break>まず、姉を返すのだな。<line-break>アーギルシャイア！<end>"
EN: "This Moonlight can cut the spirit. There is no cause for concern. If you wish to live, return my sister first. Argyleshire!<end>"
KO: "이 월광은 정신을 벨 수 있다. …걱정은 필요 없다. 죽고 싶지 않다면 먼저 누나를 돌려줘라. 아르길샤이어!<end>"

## 720038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホント…昔から人間って　　　　<line-break>好きになれなかったわ…。<line-break>　<line-break>弱いくせに、生意気で<line-break>いつでも裏をかくことを考えてて<line-break>油断ならなくて…。<end>"
EN: "I truly never could stand humans... Weak, yet insolent. Always plotting some way to outwit you. Never to be trusted...<end>"
KO: "정말… 옛날부터 인간은 좋아할 수가 없었어…. 약하면서 건방지고, 늘 뒤통수칠 생각이나 하고, 방심할 수가 없어서….<end>"

## 720039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "分をわきまえなさい！<line-break>あなたたちは私のオモチャなの。　　<line-break>　<line-break>暇つぶしに殺そうと思って、<line-break>断末魔の悲鳴を楽しもうと思って、<line-break>ここに呼んだだけの存在なの。<end>"
EN: "Know your place! You are my toys. I summoned you here for no other reason than to kill you for amusement and enjoy your dying screams.<end>"
KO: "분수를 알아! 너희는 내 장난감이야. 심심풀이로 죽이고, 단말마의 비명을 즐기려고 여기 부른 것뿐이야.<end>"

## 720040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それなのに私を傷つけ、<line-break>かわいいロイも傷つけて…。<line-break>少し、おイタが過ぎるわね。<end>"
EN: "And yet you hurt me, and dear Roy as well... You've been rather too naughty.<end>"
KO: "그런데도 나를 다치게 하고 귀여운 로이까지 다치게 했지…. 장난이 좀 지나치네.<end>"

## 720041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、無様ね。もう少しは<line-break>楽しませてくれると思ったけど。<line-break>　<line-break>おもしろくないオモチャは<line-break>消えなさい。<line-break>ウフフフ！<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. How pathetic. I thought you might entertain me a little longer. A boring toy should simply disappear. Ah ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "후후, 꼴사납네. 조금은 즐겁게 해 줄 줄 알았는데. 재미없는 장난감은 사라져. 우후후후!<end>"

## 720042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聖杯はいただいておくわ。<line-break>あなたたちには不要でしょう？<end>"
EN: "I'll be taking the Grail. You have no need of it, do you?<end>"
KO: "성배는 내가 가져갈게. 너희한텐 필요 없잖아?<end>"

## 720043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…行くぞ。<line-break>…俺は何があっても<line-break>姉を取り戻す。<end>"

KO: "…간다. …나는 무슨 일이 있어도 누나를 되찾겠다.<end>"

## 720044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そして、<value:$28>、<line-break>お前もな。<end>"
EN: "...And you too, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…그리고 <value:$28>, 너도 말이야.<end>"

## 720045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、こわい。　<line-break>くすっ、じゃあ、私も私の欲しいものを<line-break>同じ方法でいただくことにするわ。<line-break>セラ、あなたからは親友による死を…<line-break><value:$28>からは兄による死を。<end>"
EN: "My, how frightening. Hee hee. Then I think I'll use the same method to get what I want. Sera, you'll die at the hands of your best friend... and <value:$28>, at the hands of your older brother.<end>"
KO: "어머, 무서워라. 후훗, 그럼 나도 내가 원하는 걸 같은 방법으로 받아가야겠네. 세라, 너에게서는 절친에 의한 죽음을… <value:$28>에게서는 형제에 의한 죽음을.<end>"

## 720046

## 720047

## 720048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私はある女の束縛のもとにあった。　　<line-break>残虐で冷酷な女だ…。<line-break>　<line-break>それで、お前に累が及ばないよう<line-break>昔のことは、しらを切り通していた。<line-break>…だましていて、すまなかった。<end>"
EN: "I was under the thumb of a woman―a cruel, merciless woman. I pretended to know nothing about the past so you wouldn't be dragged into it. I'm sorry I deceived you.<end>"
KO: "나는 어떤 여자의 속박 아래 있었다. 잔혹하고 냉혹한 여자였지…. 그래서 너에게 피해가 가지 않도록 옛일은 끝까지 모르는 척했다. …속여서 미안했다.<end>"

## 720049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、私は今、あの女のもとを抜け、<line-break>お前たちを探していたのだ。<line-break>一緒に旅をしようと思ってな。<line-break>セラ、…会いたかったぞ。<end>"
EN: "But I'm free of that woman now, and I've been looking for all of you. I hoped we could travel together. Sera... I've missed you.<end>"
KO: "하지만 나는 지금 그 여자 곁을 떠나 너희를 찾고 있었다. 함께 여행하려고 말이지. 세라… 만나고 싶었다.<end>"

## 720051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お前…なのだな。<end>"
EN: "So... it was you.<end>"
KO: "…너…구나.<end>"

## 720053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聖杯はいただいておくわ。<line-break>あなたたちには不要でしょう？<end>"
EN: "I'll be taking the Grail. You have no need of it, do you?<end>"
KO: "성배는 내가 가져갈게. 너희한텐 필요 없잖아?<end>"

## 720054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、最後だし、楽しませてもらうわ。<line-break>じっくり、じらしながら殺してあげる。<end>"

KO: "후후, 마지막이니 즐겨 주겠어. 천천히 애태우면서 죽여 줄게.<end>"

## 720055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec072-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
