## 750001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>ようやくお姫様に会いに来てくれたね。<line-break>うれしいよ。あははっ。<end>"
EN: "Hello, <value:$28>. You've finally come to see the princess. I'm glad. Ha ha!<end>"
KO: "안녕, <value:$28>. 드디어 공주님을 만나러 와 줬구나. 기뻐. 아하하!<end>"

## 750002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "前に少し話したよね？　彼女が<line-break>先王の遺子にしてティアナ王女の従姉、<line-break>アトレイア王女さ。<end>"
EN: "We talked about her briefly before, remember? This is Princess Atleia, the late king's daughter and Princess Tiana's older cousin.<end>"
KO: "전에 조금 이야기했지? 그녀가 선왕의 유자이자 티아나 왕녀의 사촌인 아트레이아 왕녀야.<end>"

## 750003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰？<end>"
EN: "Who's there?<end>"
KO: "누구?<end>"

## 750004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君に光を与える勇者だよ、<line-break>アトレイア王女。<line-break>くすっ。さ、ごあいさつを。<end>"
EN: "The hero who will give you light, Princess Atleia. Heh. Go on, say hello.<end>"
KO: "너에게 빛을 가져다줄 용사야, 아트레이아 왕녀. 후훗. 자, 인사해.<end>"

## 750005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ようこそ、<line-break>勇者<value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "Welcome, Hero <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "어서 오십시오, 용사 <value:$28>님.<end>"

## 750006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼女は先のロストール王の娘、<line-break>アトレイア王女。<line-break>　<line-break>ティアナ王女の従姉で、<line-break>彼女に次ぐ王位継承権の<line-break>持ち主さ。<end>"
EN: "She is Princess Atleia, daughter of the former king of Rostorl. She is Princess Tiana's older cousin and stands next in line to the throne after her.<end>"
KO: "그녀는 선대 로스톨 왕의 딸 아트레이아 왕녀야. 티아나 왕녀의 사촌이며 그녀 다음가는 왕위 계승권자지.<end>"

## 750007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして僕はシャリ、<line-break>知ってるよね？<end>"
EN: "And I'm Shalli. You know that, right?<end>"
KO: "그리고 나는 샤리, 알고 있지?<end>"

## 750008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリ、どうしたのです？<line-break>殺気が…。<end>"
EN: "Shalli, what is it? I sense murderous intent...<end>"
KO: "샤리, 무슨 일이죠? 살기가….<end>"

## 750009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何でもありませんよ、王女様。<end>"
EN: "It's nothing, Your Highness.<end>"
KO: "아무것도 아닙니다, 공주님.<end>"

## 750010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼女は光を知らないんだ。<line-break>ここでそういうの、よさない？<end>"
EN: "She has never known light. Let's not have that in here, all right?<end>"
KO: "그녀는 빛을 몰라. 여기서 그런 말은 그만하지?<end>"

## 750011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、僕はシャリ。<line-break>この王宮に出入りする学者。　　<line-break>　<line-break>この大陸のはるか東から来たから<line-break>東方の博士って呼ばれてる。<line-break>よろしくね、フフフ。<end>"
EN: "And I'm Shalli, a scholar who frequents the palace. I came from the far east of this continent, so they call me the Scholar from the East. A pleasure. Heh heh heh.<end>"
KO: "그리고 나는 샤리. 이 왕궁을 드나드는 학자야. 대륙의 먼 동쪽에서 와서 동방의 박사라고 불려. 잘 부탁해, 후후후.<end>"

## 750012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア様。こちらの勇者だよ、<line-break>君に光を与えてくれた宝石、<line-break>色惑の瞳を持ってきてくれたのは。<end>"
EN: "Lady Atleia, this is the hero who brought you the jewel that gave you light: the Eye of Allure.<end>"
KO: "아트레이아 님. 이쪽이 용사야. 당신에게 빛을 준 보석, 색혹의 눈동자를 가져와 준 사람이지.<end>"

## 750013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>様、<line-break>本当にありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "...<value:$28>, thank you so very much.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>님, 정말 감사합니다.<end>"

## 750014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お礼はもういい、アトレイア様？<line-break>それじゃ、僕が<value:$28>を<line-break>王宮の外までご案内するよ。<end>"
EN: "That's enough thanks, don't you think, Lady Atleia? I'll escort <value:$28> out of the palace now.<end>"
KO: "인사는 이제 됐죠, 아트레이아 님? 그럼 제가 <value:$28>을 왕궁 밖까지 안내할게요.<end>"

## 750015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの…。<end>"
EN: "U-um...<end>"
KO: "저, 저기….<end>"

## 750016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、王女様は病弱なんでしょう？<line-break>お静かに。お体に障りますよ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. The princess is frail, isn't she? Be quiet. It could harm her health.<end>"
KO: "후후, 공주님은 병약하시잖아요? 조용히 하세요. 몸에 해로워요.<end>"

## 750017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "は、はい…。<end>"
EN: "Y-yes...<end>"
KO: "네, 네….<end>"

## 750018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、行こう、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Come, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "자, 가자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 750019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの…。<end>"
EN: "Um, excuse me...<end>"
KO: "저, 저기….<end>"

## 750020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの、よろしければ…その<line-break>また、いらしてくださいませんか？<end>"
EN: "Um, if you wouldn't mind... would you come again sometime?<end>"
KO: "저기, 괜찮으시다면… 그… 또 와주시겠어요?<end>"

## 750021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア王女、君は純真だね。　　<line-break>だけど、君のそういう行動を見て<line-break>他人はこう思うかもしれない。<line-break>…ちょっと親切にしてやったら<line-break>すぐなついてきた。さもしいことだ。<line-break>これだからさびしい奴は…てね。<end>"
EN: "Princess Atleia, you are so innocent. But others might see your behavior and think this: “Be kind to her once and she clings to you right away. How pathetic. That's what lonely people are like...”<end>"
KO: "아트레이아 왕녀, 자넨 순수하군. 하지만 자네의 그런 행동을 보고 다른 사람은 이렇게 생각할 수도 있어. …조금 친절하게 대해 줬더니 금세 들러붙네. 참 궁상맞아. 외로운 녀석들은 이래서…. 하고 말이지.<end>"

## 750022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君はそう思うかい、<line-break><value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "Is that what you think, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "넌 그렇게 생각하니, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 750023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未使用：その通りだ。だからさびしい奴はイヤだ。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 그 말이 맞다. 그래서 외로운 녀석은 싫어.<end>"

## 750024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未使用：そんなことはない。また、まいります。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 그렇지 않습니다. 다시 오겠습니다.<end>"

## 750025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君に光を与える勇者だよ、<line-break>アトレイア王女。<end>"
EN: "The hero who will give you light, Princess Atleia.<end>"
KO: "너에게 빛을 가져다줄 용자야, 아트레이아 공주.<end>"

## 750026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう気付いてると思うけど、彼女が<line-break>この前話したかわいそうな王女、<line-break>アトレイア様さ。<line-break>僕は是非、彼女に光を与えてあげたい。<line-break>色惑の瞳、楽しみに待ってるからね。<end>"
EN: "I'm sure you've realized by now, but she's the poor princess I told you about: Lady Atleia. I truly want to give her light. I'll be eagerly awaiting the Eye of Allure.<end>"
KO: "이미 눈치챘겠지만 그녀가 전에 말한 가엾은 공주, 아트레이아 님이야. 난 꼭 그녀에게 빛을 주고 싶어. 색혹의 눈동자, 기대하고 있을게.<end>"

## 750027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、王女様。<line-break>僕は<value:$28>を<line-break>王宮の外まで送ります。<end>"
EN: "Well then, Your Highness. I shall escort <value:$28> out of the palace.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 공주님. 제가 <value:$28>을 왕궁 밖까지 모셔다드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 750028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、アトレイア王女もああ言ってるし、<line-break>君もたまには顔を出してよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, you heard Princess Atleia. Drop by once in a while.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 아트레이아 왕녀도 저렇게 말하니 너도 가끔 얼굴 좀 비춰줘.<end>"

## 750029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、王女様、<line-break>失礼いたします。<end>"
EN: "Well then, Your Highness, if you'll excuse me.<end>"
KO: "그럼 공주님, 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 750030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ってたよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>この前、少し話したよね？<line-break>この方がアトレイア王女さ。　　　<line-break>じゃあ、さっそくで悪いんだけど、<line-break>それ、もらっちゃうね。<end>"
EN: "I've been waiting for you, <value:$28>. We spoke briefly the other day, remember? This is Princess Atleia. Sorry to get straight to business, but I'll take that now.<end>"
KO: "기다렸어, <value:$28>. 전에 조금 이야기했지? 이분이 아트레이아 왕녀야. 자, 바로라 미안하지만 그거 받을게.<end>"

## 750031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、ありがとう。<line-break>これで彼女に世界を見せてあげられる。<line-break>　<line-break>悲しみと憎悪に彩られた世界をね。<line-break>くすっ、あははははは！<line-break>ホント、ありがとうね！<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>. Now I can show her the world―a world steeped in sorrow and hatred. Hee hee... Ahahahahaha! Thank you so much!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 고마워. 이제 그녀에게 세상을 보여 줄 수 있어. 슬픔과 증오로 물든 세상을 말이야. 후후, 아하하하하! 정말 고마워!<end>"

## 750032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア様、<line-break>あなたの勇者が光を持ってきました。<end>"
EN: "Lady Atleia, your hero has brought you light.<end>"
KO: "아트레이아 님, 당신의 용자가 빛을 가져왔습니다.<end>"

## 750033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きゃっ。<end>"
EN: "Ah!<end>"
KO: "꺅.<end>"

## 750034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア様、それがあなたの<line-break>新しい光、色惑の瞳です。<line-break>いかがですか、世界の色は？<end>"
EN: "Lady Atleia, this is your new light: the Eye of Allure. How do the colors of the world look?<end>"
KO: "아트레이아 님, 그것이 당신의 새로운 빛, 색혹의 눈동자입니다. 어떠신가요, 세상의 색은?<end>"

## 750035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見える…、見えます…！<line-break>私…私…今、見えています！<end>"
EN: "I can see... I can see! I... I can see now!<end>"
KO: "보여… 보여요…! 저… 저… 지금 보여요!<end>"

## 750036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、お礼をおっしゃってください。<line-break>光をくれた<value:$28>に。<end>"
EN: "Now, thank <value:$28> for giving you light.<end>"
KO: "자, 감사 인사를 해 주세요. 빛을 준 <value:$28>에게.<end>"

## 750037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ありがとうございます。　<line-break><value:$28>様。<line-break>　<line-break>ご恩は一生忘れません！<line-break>光を、ああ、光を<line-break>本当にありがとうございます！<end>"
EN: "Oh, thank you, <value:$28>. I will never forget your kindness! Thank you for the light―oh, thank you so much for the light!<end>"
KO: "가, 감사합니다, <value:$28>님. 이 은혜는 평생 잊지 않겠습니다! 빛을, 아아, 빛을 정말 감사합니다!<end>"

## 750038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これはお礼だよ。<line-break>ホントにありがとう。<end>"
EN: "This is my way of thanking you. I mean it―thank you.<end>"
KO: "이건 답례야. 정말 고마워.<end>"

## 750039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "３０００ギア手に入れた。<end>"
EN: "Obtained 3,000 Gea.<end>"
KO: "3000기어를 손에 넣었다.<end>"

## 750040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、僕が<value:$28>を<line-break>王宮の外までご案内します。<end>"
EN: "Then I'll escort <value:$28> out of the palace.<end>"
KO: "그럼 제가 <value:$28>을 왕궁 밖까지 안내하겠습니다.<end>"

## 750041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日は本当にありがとう。<line-break>これで彼女に世界を見せてあげられる。<line-break>　<line-break>悲しみと憎悪に彩られた世界をね。<line-break>くすっ、あはははは！<line-break>ホント、ありがとうね！<end>"
EN: "Thank you so much for today. Now I can show her the world―a world steeped in sorrow and hatred. Heh heh... Ahahahaha! Thank you, truly!<end>"
KO: "오늘 정말 고마워. 이제 그녀에게 세상을 보여 줄 수 있어. 슬픔과 증오로 물든 세상을 말이야. 후후, 아하하하! 정말 고마워!<end>"

## 750042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい。お気をつけて…。<end>"
EN: "All right. Take care...<end>"
KO: "네. 조심히 가세요….<end>"

## 750043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で、いきなりかもしれないけど、　　<line-break><value:$28>にお願いがあるんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>ここにいるアトレイア様は、幼いころに<line-break>大人の醜い争いに巻き込まれて、<line-break>それっきり光を知らないんだ。<end>"
EN: "This may seem rather sudden, but I have a favor to ask of you, <value:$28>. Lady Atleia was caught up in an ugly struggle among adults when she was a child, and she has known nothing of light ever since.<end>"
KO: "갑작스러울지도 모르지만 <value:$28>에게 부탁이 있어. 여기 있는 아트레이아 님은 어릴 때 어른들의 추한 다툼에 휘말린 뒤로 지금까지 빛을 보지 못했어.<end>"

## 750044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ王女が華やかな日々を送る間、<line-break>彼女は孤独で光なき人生を送っていた。<line-break>この部屋が彼女の世界のすべてなのさ。<end>"
EN: "While Princess Tiana lived a life of splendor, Atleia lived alone, without light. This room has been her entire world.<end>"
KO: "티아나 왕녀가 화려한 나날을 보내는 동안 그녀는 고독하고 빛 없는 삶을 보냈지. 이 방이 그녀 세계의 전부였어.<end>"

## 750045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かわいそうに、<line-break>大人たちの陰謀に関わって<line-break>赤ん坊のときから光を知らない。　　　<line-break>まぁ、そのおかげで陰謀が飛び交う<line-break>この王宮の中で、そのあとも<line-break>生き延びることができたんだけどね。<end>"
EN: "Poor thing. Caught up in adults' schemes, she has known no light since infancy. Though thanks to that, she was able to survive in this palace, where plots have swirled ever since.<end>"
KO: "불쌍하게도 어른들의 음모에 휘말려 갓난아기 때부터 빛을 모르고 살았지. 뭐, 그 덕분에 음모가 난무하는 이 왕궁에서도 이후 살아남을 수 있었지만 말이야.<end>"

## 750046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ね、そろそろ、アトレイア王女が<line-break>幸せになってもいいころだと思わない？<line-break>だから、君に協力してほしいんだ。<end>"
EN: "Don't you think it's about time Princess Atleia was allowed some happiness? That's why I want your help.<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 이제 슬슬 아트레이아 왕녀도 행복해져도 되지 않을까? 그래서 네가 도와줬으면 해.<end>"

## 750047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "色惑の瞳という魔法の宝石がある。　　<line-break>この色惑の瞳の力を使えば、<line-break>王女に光を与えられる。<line-break>彼女のために、<line-break>取ってきてくれないかな？<line-break>もちろん、報酬は用意するよ。<end>"
EN: "There is a magical gemstone called the Eye of Allure. Its power can give the princess light. Would you retrieve it for her? There will be a reward, of course.<end>"
KO: "색혹의 눈동자라는 마법의 보석이 있어. 그 힘을 쓰면 왕녀에게 빛을 되찾아 줄 수 있지. 그녀를 위해 가져와 주지 않을래? 물론 보수는 준비할게.<end>"

## 750048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受ける<end>"
EN: "Accept<end>"
KO: "수락한다<end>"

## 750049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受けない<end>"
EN: "Decline<end>"
KO: "맡지 않는다<end>"

## 750050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア、喜んで！<line-break>勇者殿は引き受けてくれるってさ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Good news, Atleia! Our Hero says yes.<end>"
KO: "아트레이아, 기뻐해! 용자님이 맡아 주겠대.<end>"

## 750051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフッ…<line-break>じゃあ、<value:$28>、<line-break>待ってるよ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh... Well then, <value:$28>. I'll be waiting.<end>"
KO: "후후훗… 그럼 <value:$28>, 기다릴게.<end>"

## 750052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます。<line-break>どうか、よろしくお願いいたします。<line-break><value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. I place myself in your hands, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 부디 잘 부탁드립니다, <value:$28>님.<end>"

## 750053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、王女様。<line-break>僕は<value:$28>を<line-break>王宮の外まで送ります。<end>"
EN: "Well then, Princess, I'll see <value:$28> out of the palace.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 공주님. 제가 <value:$28>을 왕궁 밖까지 모셔다드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 750054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でね、色惑の瞳のある場所なんだけど、<line-break>ここロストールから北西に見える、<line-break>邪眼の迷宮にあるんだ。<line-break>中には魔物がいると思うけど、<line-break>大丈夫。たぶん、君なら勝てるよ。<line-break>僕が見込んだ勇者様だからね。<end>"
EN: "Now, about where to find the Eye of Allure: it's in the Evil Eye Labyrinth, which you can see to the northwest from here in Rostorl. There are probably monsters inside, but don't worry. You'll be fine. You can probably beat them. You're the Hero I have such high hopes for, after all.<end>"
KO: "그리고 색혹의 눈동자가 있는 곳인데, 여기 로스톨에서 북서쪽으로 보이는 사안의 미궁에 있어. 안에는 마물이 있겠지만 괜찮아. 아마 너라면 이길 거야. 내가 점찍은 용사님이니까.<end>"

## 750055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼女に光を。<line-break>彼女の願い、かなえてほしい。<line-break>　<line-break>じゃあね。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Give her light. Please grant her wish. Bye, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그녀에게 빛을. 그녀의 소원을 이루어 줬으면 해. 그럼 안녕, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 750056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう…ですか。<line-break>残念です。<end>"
EN: "I... see. That's a shame.<end>"
KO: "그렇…군요. 유감입니다.<end>"

## 750057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア、気を落とさないで。<line-break><value:$28>も気が変わって<line-break>引き受けてくれることもあるだろうし。<end>"
EN: "Don't be too discouraged, Atleia. <value:$28> may change course and agree to help after all.<end>"
KO: "아트레이아, 기운 내. <value:$28>도 마음이 바뀌어 맡아 줄지 모르잖아.<end>"

## 750058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "중복 미사용: 그럼 공주님. 049: 제가 <value:$28>을 왕궁 밖까지 바래다드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 750059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "미사용(하지만 아트레이아 공주의 방 위치가 저렇게 창문도 없는 곳은 아닌 것 같은데…)<end>"

## 750060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ホント、冷たい人だね、君って。<line-break>正直、ガッカリだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>フフフ、でも、あの娘なら、<line-break>やってくれそうだな…。<end>"
EN: "You really are cold, aren't you? Honestly, I'm disappointed. Heh heh... Still, that girl looks like she might do it...<end>"
KO: "…정말 차가운 사람이네, 너. 솔직히 실망했어. 후후후, 그래도 그 아이라면 해낼 것 같군….<end>"

## 750061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、また来てよ。<line-break>気が変わるってこともあるだろうしさ。<end>"
EN: "Well, come back sometime. You might change your mind.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 또 와. 마음이 바뀔 수도 있잖아.<end>"

## 750062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア王女、<line-break>この人に頼んだんだ。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>この<value:$28>こそが、<line-break>きっと君に色惑の瞳を…光を<line-break>持ってきてくれるはずさ。<end>"
EN: "Princess Atleia, I've asked this person to help. <value:$28> is sure to bring you the Eye of Allure... to bring you light.<end>"
KO: "아트레이아 왕녀, 이 사람에게 부탁했어. 이 <value:$28>이 분명 네게 색혹의 눈동자를… 빛을 가져다줄 거야.<end>"

## 750063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 750073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…で、この前の話だけど、　　　<line-break>考えてくれたかなあ？<line-break>　<line-break>アトレイア王女に光を与えるために<line-break>宝石、色惑の瞳を探し出して<line-break>持ってきてほしいんだけど…。<end>"
EN: "So, about what we discussed before... Have you thought it over? To bring light to Princess Atleia, I'd like you to find the jewel known as the Eye of Allure and bring it here...<end>"

## 750091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ありがとうございます。　<line-break><value:$28>様。<line-break>　<line-break>ご恩は一生忘れません！<line-break>光を、ああ、光を<line-break>本当にありがとうございます！<end>"

## 750092

## 750093

## 750094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 750095

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 750096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、王女様は病弱なんでしょう？<line-break>お静かに。お体に障りますよ？<end>"
EN: "Heh. The princess is frail, isn't she? Be quiet. It could harm her health.<end>"
KO: "후후, 공주님은 병약하시잖아요? 조용히 하세요. 몸에 해로워요.<end>"

## 750097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "は、はい…。<end>"
EN: "Y-yes...<end>"
KO: "네, 네….<end>"

## 750098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 750099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "저, 저기….<end>"

## 750100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの、よろしければ…その<line-break>また、いらしてくださいませんか？<end>"
EN: "Um, if you wouldn't mind... would you come again sometime?<end>"
KO: "저기, 괜찮으시다면… 그… 또 와주시겠어요?<end>"

## 750101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア王女、君は純真だね。　　<line-break>だけど、君のそういう行動を見て<line-break>他人はこう思うかもしれない。<line-break>…ちょっと親切にしてやったら<line-break>すぐなついてきた。さもしいことだ。<line-break>これだからさびしい奴は…てね。<end>"
EN: "Princess Atleia, you are so innocent. But others might see your behavior and think this: “Be kind to her once and she clings to you right away. How pathetic. That's what lonely people are like...”<end>"
KO: "아트레이아 왕녀, 자넨 순수하군. 하지만 자네의 그런 행동을 보고 다른 사람은 이렇게 생각할 수도 있어. …조금 친절하게 대해 줬더니 금세 들러붙네. 참 궁상맞아. 외로운 녀석들은 이래서…. 하고 말이지.<end>"

## 750102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君はそう思うかい？<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Is that what you think, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "넌 그렇게 생각하니? <value:$28>.<end>"

## 750103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、残酷だなあ。君って。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ま、世界というのは<line-break>目を開けてみれば<line-break>結構、汚い所なんだけどさ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. You're cruel, aren't you? Still, once you open your eyes, the world is quite a dirty place.<end>"
KO: "후후, 잔인하네. 너도. 뭐, 세상이라는 건 눈을 뜨고 보면 꽤 더러운 곳이지만 말이야.<end>"

## 750104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、やさしいんだなあ。<line-break>君って。<end>"
EN: "Heh. You're kind, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "후후, 상냥하구나. 너는.<end>"

## 750105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…うれしい…です。<end>"
EN: "...I'm happy.<end>"
KO: "…기뻐요….<end>"

## 750106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、アトレイア王女もああ言ってるし、<line-break>君もたまには顔を出してよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, you heard Princess Atleia. Drop by once in a while.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 아트레이아 왕녀도 저렇게 말하니 너도 가끔 얼굴 좀 비춰줘.<end>"

## 750107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、王女様<line-break>失礼いたします。<end>"
EN: "Well then, Your Highness, if you'll excuse me.<end>"
KO: "그럼 공주님, 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 750108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日は本当にありがとう。<line-break>これで彼女に世界を見せてあげられる。<line-break>　<line-break>悲しみと憎悪に彩られた世界をね。<line-break>くすっ、あははは！<line-break>ホント、ありがとうね！<end>"
EN: "Thank you so much for today. Now I can show her the world―a world steeped in sorrow and hatred. Heh heh... Ahahahaha! Thank you, truly!<end>"
KO: "오늘 정말 고마워. 이걸로 그녀에게 세계를 보여 줄 수 있어. 슬픔과 증오로 물든 세계를 말이야. 후훗, 아하하하! 정말 고마워!<end>"

## 750113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こらあ、<value:$28>！<line-break>フゴーの所で何を売るつもりなの？　　<line-break>　<line-break>まさか、あのお姫様を助けるために<line-break>取ってきた色惑の瞳って宝石を<line-break>売り飛ばすつもりじゃないでしょうね。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>! What are you planning to sell to Hugo? You aren't thinking of selling off the Eye of Allure you brought back to help that princess, are you?<end>"
KO: "야, <value:$28>! 휴고한테 뭘 팔 생각이야? 설마 그 공주님을 돕기 위해 가져온 색혹의 눈동자라는 보석을 팔아넘기려는 건 아니겠지.<end>"

## 750114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのつもりだ。<end>"
EN: "That's exactly what I intend to do.<end>"
KO: "그럴 생각이야.<end>"

## 750115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなつもりはない。<end>"
EN: "I have no intention of doing that.<end>"
KO: "그럴 생각은 없어.<end>"

## 750116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんで、そんなひどいことができるの？<line-break>あんなにやさしい人間だったのに！<line-break>　<line-break>とにかく、そんなこと絶対に<line-break>この私が許さないんだから！<line-break>ほぉら！　ここから出ていくの！<end>"
EN: "How can you do something so cruel? You used to be so kind! In any case, I absolutely won't allow it! Come on! Get out of here!<end>"
KO: "어떻게 그런 끔찍한 짓을 할 수 있어? 그렇게나 상냥한 사람이었는데! 아무튼 그런 건 절대로 내가 용서 못 해! 자! 여기서 나가는 거야!<end>"

## 750117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、なんでこんな所にいるのよ。<line-break>ほぉら！　ここから出ていくの！<end>"
EN: "Then what are you doing here? Come on! Get out of here!<end>"
KO: "그럼 왜 이런 데 있는 거야. 자아! 여기서 나가!<end>"

## 750118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう！　いい？　その色惑の瞳は<line-break>ちゃんとお姫様に渡すの。わかった？<end>"
EN: "Honestly! Listen: make sure you give that Eye of Allure to the princess. Understand?<end>"
KO: "정말! 알겠어? 그 색혹의 눈동자는 제대로 공주님께 드리는 거야. 알았지?<end>"

## 750119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ってよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>フゴーの所で何を売るつもりなの？<line-break>　<line-break>まさか、あのお姫様を助けるために<line-break>取ってきた色惑の瞳って宝石を<line-break>売り飛ばすつもりじゃないよね。<end>"
EN: "Wait, <value:$28>. What are you planning to sell to Hugo? You aren't thinking of selling off the Eye of Allure you brought back to help that princess, are you?<end>"
KO: "잠깐, <value:$28>. 휴고에게 뭘 팔 생각이야? 설마 그 공주를 돕기 위해 가져온 색혹의 눈동자라는 보석을 팔아 버릴 생각은 아니겠지.<end>"

## 750120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしてそんなひどいことができるの？<line-break>あのお姫様がかわいそうじゃない！<line-break>　<line-break>僕、そんなこと絶対に許せないよ！<line-break>絶対、フゴーの家に入れない！<line-break>さ、行くんだ！<end>"
EN: "How could you do something so cruel? That poor princess! I will never forgive this! I won't let you into Hugo's house! Now, get going!<end>"
KO: "어떻게 그런 끔찍한 짓을 할 수 있어? 그 공주가 불쌍하잖아! 난 그런 거 절대 용서 못 해! 절대 휴고의 집에는 못 들어가! 자, 가!<end>"

## 750121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかった…。<line-break>じゃ、別の所に行こうよ。ね？<end>"
EN: "What a relief... Let's go somewhere else, all right?<end>"
KO: "다행이다…. 그럼 다른 곳으로 가자. 응?<end>"

## 750122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…とにかく　その色惑の瞳は<line-break>ちゃんとお姫様に渡そうよ。ね？<end>"
EN: "Anyway, let's make sure that Eye of Allure gets to the princess. All right?<end>"
KO: "…어쨌든 그 색혹의 눈동자는 제대로 공주님께 전해 주자. 응?<end>"

## 750123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こぉらあ、<value:$28>！<line-break>フゴーの所で何を売る気なのぉ？　　　<line-break>　<line-break>まさか、あのお姫様を助けるために<line-break>取ってきた色惑の瞳って宝石を<line-break>売り飛ばすつもりじゃないでしょうね。<end>"
EN: "Heeey, <value:$28>! What are you planning to sell to Hugo? You aren't thinking of selling off the Eye of Allure you brought back to help that princess, are you?<end>"
KO: "이봐아, <value:$28>! 휴고한테 뭘 팔 생각이야? 설마 그 공주님을 돕기 위해 가져온 색혹의 눈동자라는 보석을 팔아넘길 생각은 아니겠지.<end>"

## 750124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひっどーいよぉ！　それって、<line-break>あのお姫様をだましてるんだよぉ！　　<line-break>　<line-break>お姫様、かわいそうだよぉ！<line-break>ルルアンタ、そんなの絶対に許せない！<line-break>ほら！　ここから出ていくのぉ！<end>"
EN: "That's awful! You're deceiving that princess! I feel so sorry for her! Lulluantha will never allow that! Come on! Get out of here!<end>"
KO: "너무해애! 그건 그 공주님을 속이는 거잖아! 공주님 불쌍해! 릴비는 그런 거 절대 못 참아! 자! 여기서 나가는 거야!<end>"

## 750125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、なんでこんな所にいるのよ？<line-break>ほら！　ここから出ていくのぉ！<end>"
EN: "Then why are you here? Come on! Get out of here!<end>"
KO: "그럼 왜 이런 데 있는 거야? 자! 여기서 나가라고!<end>"

## 750126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "정말! 잘 들어? 그 보석은 제대로 공주님께 전해 줘야 해!<end>"

## 750127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>、<line-break>フゴーの所で何か売るつもりなの？<line-break>　<line-break>まさか、あのお姫様を助けるために<line-break>取ってきた色惑の瞳って宝石を<line-break>売り飛ばすつもりじゃないよ、ね？<end>"
EN: "...<value:$28>, are you planning to sell something to Hugo? You aren't thinking of selling off the Eye of Allure you brought back to help that princess, are you?<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>, 휴고에게 뭔가 팔 생각이야? 설마 그 왕녀를 구하려고 가져온 색혹의 눈동자라는 보석을 팔아넘길 생각은 아니지?<end>"

## 750128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…う、うそだよ、そんなの！<line-break>だって、お姫様、かわいそうじゃない！<line-break>　<line-break>そんなひどいこと、キミはしないよね。<line-break>冗談だよね？　ここに用はないんだよ。<line-break>さ、別の所に行こうよ！<end>"
EN: "N-no, you can't mean that! That poor princess! You wouldn't do something so cruel, would you? This is a joke, right? We have no business here. Come on, let's go somewhere else!<end>"
KO: "…거, 거짓말이지, 그런 거! 공주님 불쌍하잖아! 그런 끔찍한 짓, 넌 안 하지? 농담이지? 여기엔 볼일 없어. 자, 다른 데로 가자!<end>"

## 750129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだよね。うん。キミなら当然だよ。<line-break>じゃ、別の所に行こうよ。ね？<end>"
EN: "Of course. I knew you wouldn't. Let's go somewhere else, all right?<end>"
KO: "그렇지. 응. 너라면 당연하지. 그럼 다른 곳으로 가자. 응?<end>"

## 750130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その色惑の瞳はちゃんとお姫様に渡す。<line-break>そうだよね、<value:$28>？<end>"

KO: "그 색혹의 눈동자는 제대로 공주님께 드릴 거야. 그렇지, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 750131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 750132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…つまり、<line-break>俺たちをだましたのか？　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>お姫様を救うという言葉で<line-break>お前が俺たちをだましてないなら<line-break>ここからすぐに立ち去れ。<end>"

## 750133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…さもなくば。<end>"
EN: "...Otherwise.<end>"
KO: "…그렇지 않으면.<end>"

## 750134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 750135

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "…그런가.<end>"

## 750137

## 750139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お姫様を助けるんだって話は<line-break>じゃあ、なんだったんだい？<line-break>　<line-break>俺たちをだましてまで<line-break>金が欲しかったのかい？<line-break>ゲスだねえ。<end>"
EN: "Then what was all that talk about helping the princess? Did you want money badly enough to deceive us? How low.<end>"
KO: "공주님을 구한다는 얘기는 그럼 뭐였어? 우리까지 속여 가며 돈이 필요했던 거야? 비열하네.<end>"

## 750140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、考えられる理由は　　　　　　　　<line-break>もうひとつあるな…。<line-break>　<line-break>色惑の瞳は確かに<line-break>強力過ぎる力を持つ魔道器だ。<line-break>これを誰かに渡していいかってことだ。<end>"
EN: "Well, there is one other possible reason... The Eye of Allure is certainly a magical device with power far too great. The question is whether it is right to hand it over to anyone.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 생각할 수 있는 이유는 하나 더 있어…. 색혹의 눈동자는 분명 지나치게 강한 힘을 가진 마도기야. 이걸 누군가에게 넘겨도 되느냐는 문제지.<end>"

## 750141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その点、フゴーに売っちまえば<line-break>警備状態のいいフゴーの倉なら<line-break>魔人からでもアレを守れるだろう。　　<line-break>だが、それにしても気に入らんな。<line-break>どうにも、事なかれ主義で<line-break>大上段からものを見過ぎてないか？<end>"
EN: "If you sell it to Hugo, his well-guarded storehouse could keep it safe even from a demon. Still, I don't like it. Aren't you being too high-minded and too eager to avoid trouble?<end>"
KO: "그 점에선 휴고에게 팔아버리면 경비가 좋은 휴고 창고라면 마인에게서도 그걸 지킬 수 있겠지. 하지만 그래도 마음에 안 드는군. 너무 무사안일하고 위에서 내려다보듯 보는 거 아냐?<end>"

## 750142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フゴーに売れば、アトレイア王女から<line-break>永遠に光を得る機会を奪うことになる。<line-break>それはいいことなのか？<line-break>たとえ、それが、世界に降りかかる<line-break>災いを防止するための処置だったとして<line-break>それは許されることなのか？<end>"
EN: "Selling it to Hugo would forever rob Princess Atleia of her chance to see. Is that right? Even if it would prevent a calamity from befalling the world, can that truly be justified?<end>"
KO: "휴고에게 팔면 아트레이아 왕녀가 영원히 빛을 얻을 기회를 빼앗게 된다. 그게 옳은 일인가? 설령 세계에 닥칠 재앙을 막기 위한 조치라 해도 허용될 수 있는 일인가?<end>"

## 750143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の答えはノーだ。俺は欲張りでな。<line-break>アトレイア王女も助けるし、世界にも<line-break>災いが降りかからないようにする。<end>"
EN: "My answer is no. I'm greedy: I will save Princess Atleia and keep calamity from befalling the world.<end>"
KO: "내 대답은 노야. 난 욕심이 많거든. 아트레이아 왕녀도 구하고 세계에도 재앙이 닥치지 않게 할 거야.<end>"

## 750144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "災いを防止する方法ならあるさ。<line-break>アトレイア王女の所に足しげく通って<line-break>そのあとのことに目を光らせるとかな。<line-break>…てなわけで、他に反対理由がなければ<line-break>アトレイア王女に色惑の瞳を<line-break>渡すべきだと思うが、どうだ？<end>"
EN: "There is a way to prevent calamity. Keep visiting Princess Atleia and keep a close watch on what happens afterward. So, unless you have another reason to object, I say we should give the Eye of Allure to Princess Atleia. What do you say?<end>"
KO: "재앙을 막을 방법이라면 있지. 아트레이아 왕녀에게 자주 찾아가 그 뒤의 일을 지켜보는 거야. …그러니 다른 반대 이유가 없다면 아트레이아 왕녀에게 색혹의 눈동자를 건네는 게 맞다고 보는데, 어때?<end>"

## 750145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いい子だ。<end>"
EN: "Good.<end>"
KO: "착한 아이야.<end>"

## 750146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、そこには用はないな。<line-break>別の所に行くといい。<line-break>　<line-break>俺はお前が行くまで<line-break>見張ってることにする。<end>"
EN: "Then you have no business there. Go somewhere else. I'll keep watch until you do.<end>"
KO: "그럼 거긴 볼일 없겠네. 다른 곳으로 가. 나는 네가 갈 때까지 지켜보고 있지.<end>"

## 750147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "色惑の瞳、フゴーに売る気なの？<line-break>君が根っからゲスなのか。<line-break>それとも僕を信用できないのか…。<line-break>ま、どっちかわからないけど。<line-break>とにかく契約違反は許せないな。<end>"
EN: "You mean to sell the Eye of Allure to Hugo? Are you rotten to the core, or do you not trust me? I can't say which. Either way, I cannot forgive a breach of contract.<end>"
KO: "색혹의 눈동자를 휴고에게 팔 생각이야? 네가 원래 쓰레기인지, 아니면 날 못 믿는 건지…. 어느 쪽이든 계약 위반은 용서 못 해.<end>"

## 750148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かに色惑の瞳、受け取ったよ。<line-break>これでアトレイアに光が与えられる。　<line-break>　<line-break>アトレイアには君の契約違反は<line-break>黙っていてあげるよ。<line-break>そのかわり、報酬はなし、ね。アハハ！<end>"
EN: "I have received the Eye of Allure. Now Atleia can be given light. I'll keep your breach of contract from Atleia. But you will receive no reward. Hahaha!<end>"
KO: "색혹의 눈동자, 확실히 받았어. 이걸로 아트레이아에게 빛을 줄 수 있어. 아트레이아에게는 네 계약 위반을 말하지 않을게. 대신 보수는 없어. 아하하!<end>"

## 750149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょいと待ちな。　　　　　　　<line-break>そこの悪党！<line-break>　<line-break>忘れられた王女を救うはずの<line-break>色惑の瞳…それをフゴーに売って<line-break>もうけようとは言語道断…。<end>"
EN: "Hold it right there, villain! To sell the Eye of Allure, which was meant to save the forgotten princess, to Hugo for profit is utterly unforgivable...<end>"
KO: "잠깐 기다려. 거기 악당! 잊힌 왕녀를 구할 색혹의 눈동자를 휴고에게 팔아 돈을 벌려 하다니 말도 안 돼….<end>"

## 750150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "忘れられた王女の悲しみの<line-break>声にならない雄叫びが<line-break>悪を倒せと俺様を呼ぶ！<end>"
EN: "The voiceless cry of the forgotten princess's sorrow calls upon me to defeat evil!<end>"
KO: "잊힌 왕녀의 슬픔이 담긴소리 없는 포효가악을 쓰러뜨리라며 이 몸을 부른다!<end>"

## 750151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう！　俺様は最高の人生の伴侶を得て<line-break>世界最強の幸せ者、ガルドランだ！<end>"
EN: "That's right! I am Girldlan, the happiest man in the world, for I have found the finest partner in life!<end>"
KO: "그래! 이 몸은 최고의 인생 동반자를 얻은 세상에서 가장 행복한 사나이, 갈드란이다!<end>"

## 750152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おうっ！　なんということだ！<line-break>悪党の正体がお姉さまだったなんて！　<line-break>　<line-break>しかし、たとえ、相手がお姉さまでも<line-break>悪を見逃すわけにはまいりません！！<line-break>俺様の正義の槍を受けてもらいます！<end>"
EN: "Oh! What is this? The villain was my dear sister! Still, even if it is you, Sister, I cannot let evil go unpunished! Receive my spear of justice!<end>"
KO: "오오! 이 무슨 일이란 말인가! 악당의 정체가 누님이었다니! 하지만 상대가 누님이라 해도 악을 눈감아 줄 수는 없습니다!! 이 몸의 정의의 창을 받아라!<end>"

## 750153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おうっ！　なんということだ！<line-break>悪党の正体がお姉さまだったなんて！<end>"
EN: "Oh! What is this? The villain was my dear sister!<end>"
KO: "오오! 이게 무슨 일이야! 악당의 정체가 누님이었다니!<end>"

## 750154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お姉さま。報告が遅れまして　　　　　<line-break>まことに申しわけありません！<line-break>　<line-break>この俺様ガルドランはルフレンちゃんと<line-break>このたび、結婚いたしました。<line-break>２人で幸せな家庭を築く所存です！<end>"
EN: "Sister, I am terribly sorry to have delayed my report! I, Girldlan, have married dear Lufren. We intend to build a happy home together!<end>"
KO: "누님. 보고가 늦어 정말 죄송합니다! 이 나님 갈드란은 루프렌과 이번에 결혼했습니다. 둘이 행복한 가정을 꾸려 나가겠습니다!<end>"

## 750155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて！　たとえ、相手がお姉さまでも<line-break>悪を見逃すわけにはまいりません！！<line-break>俺様の正義の槍を受けてもらいます！<end>"
EN: "Now then! Even if it is you, Sister, I cannot let evil go unpunished! Receive my spear of justice!<end>"
KO: "자! 상대가 누님이라 해도 악을 눈감아 줄 순 없습니다!! 나의 정의의 창을 받아라!<end>"

## 750156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪党め。新婚の勇者である俺様に<line-break>倒されることを幸せに思うがいい！<end>"
EN: "Villain, consider it an honor to be defeated by me, a newlywed hero!<end>"
KO: "악당 놈. 신혼의 용자인 이 몸에게 쓰러지는 걸 행복으로 알아라!<end>"

## 750157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "説明しよう！　この俺様ガルドランは、<line-break>アミラルの宿屋の娘、かわいい<line-break>ルフレンちゃんと結婚したのだ！　<line-break>さあ、説明はここまでだ。<line-break>悪党め。新婚の勇者である俺様に<line-break>倒されることを幸せに思うがいい！<end>"
EN: "Allow me to explain! I, Girldlan, have married dear Lufren, the innkeeper's daughter in Amiral! And now the explanation is over. Villain, consider it an honor to be defeated by me, a newlywed hero!<end>"
KO: "설명해 주지! 이 몸 갈드란은 아미랄 여관의 딸, 귀여운 루프렌 짱과 결혼했다! 자, 설명은 여기까지다. 악당아. 신혼의 용사인 이 몸에게 쓰러지는 것을 영광으로 알아라!<end>"

## 750158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バ、バカな。この俺様が負ける…？<line-break>いや、そんなはずはない！<line-break>我が最愛の妻ルフレンよ！　我に力を！<end>"
EN: "I-impossible. I, of all people, have lost...? No, that cannot be! My beloved wife, Lufren! Grant me strength!<end>"
KO: "마, 말도 안 돼. 이 몸이 지다니…? 아니, 그럴 리 없어! 나의 사랑하는 아내 루프렌이여! 내게 힘을!<end>"

## 750159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフッ、ガルドランじゃ、<line-break>やっぱり無理だったよね。アハハ！<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. I knew Girldlan could not handle it. Hahaha!<end>"
KO: "후후, 갈드란으로는 역시 무리였네. 아하하!<end>"

## 750160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言ったはず…ルフレンちゃんと結婚した<line-break>俺様は最強だと！！<end>"
EN: "I told you... I am invincible now that I have married dear Lufren!!<end>"
KO: "말했을 텐데… 루프렌과 결혼한 이 몸은 최강이라고!!<end>"

## 750161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これか？　悲しみの王女の<line-break>求めし色惑の瞳とは！？<end>"
EN: "Is this it? The Eye of Allure sought by the sorrowful princess!?<end>"
KO: "이건가? 슬픔의 왕녀가 찾던 색혹의 눈동자란 것이!?<end>"

## 750162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだよ。ありがとう。<line-break>勇者様！<end>"
EN: "That is it. Thank you, Hero!<end>"
KO: "맞아. 고마워, 용사님!<end>"

## 750163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "礼には及ばない。<line-break>俺様は悪ある所必ず現れるのだ！<line-break>　<line-break>では、さらばだ！<line-break>世界中が俺様を呼んでるぜ！<line-break>でやっ！<end>"
EN: "Think nothing of it. I appear wherever evil is found! Farewell! The whole world is calling for me! Hyah!<end>"
KO: "감사는 필요 없다. 이 몸은 악이 있는 곳엔 반드시 나타난다! 그럼 안녕이다! 온 세상이 나를 부르고 있다! 이야앗!<end>"

## 750164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺様はガルドラン改・激・烈！！<line-break>暗闇の中の王女の光を奪う悪党め！<line-break>　<line-break>この世に正義がある限り、いやさ<line-break>俺様がある限り、色惑の瞳を<line-break>売り飛ばさせはせん！　いくぞ！<end>"
EN: "I am Girldlan Revised: Fierce and Furious!! Villain, you would steal the light of a princess in darkness! So long as justice exists in this world―no, so long as I exist―I will not let you sell the Eye of Allure! Here I come!<end>"
KO: "나님은 갈드란 개·격·렬!! 어둠 속 왕녀의 빛을 빼앗는 악당 놈! 이 세상에 정의가 있는 한, 아니 나님이 있는 한 색혹의 눈동자를 팔아넘기게 두지 않겠다! 간다!<end>"

## 750165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バ、バカな。<line-break>この俺様が負ける…？　　　<line-break>　<line-break>いや、そんなはずはない！<line-break>そんなはずはない！！！<line-break>そんなはずはないんだあ！！<end>"
EN: "I-impossible. I, of all people, have lost...? No, that cannot be! It cannot be! It cannot be!!!<end>"
KO: "마, 말도 안 돼. 이 내가 진다고…? 아니, 그럴 리 없다! 그럴 리 없어!!!<end>"

## 750167

## 750172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "き、貴様は我が永遠のライバル…！<line-break>くっ、まだ強化完了していないのに！　<line-break>　<line-break>今は、まだ決戦の時ではない！！<line-break>貴様が悪事をくり返すのを放っとくのは<line-break>心が痛むが、さらば、我が永遠の宿敵！<end>"
EN: "Y-you are my eternal rival...! Damn it, I have not finished upgrading yet! This is not yet the time for our final showdown!! It pains me to leave you free to repeat your evil deeds, but farewell, my eternal nemesis!<end>"
KO: "네, 네놈은 나의 영원한 라이벌…! 큭, 아직 강화가 끝나지 않았는데! 지금은 아직 결전의 때가 아니다!! 네놈이 악행을 반복하는 걸 두고 가는 건 마음 아프지만 잘 있어라, 나의 영원한 숙적!<end>"

## 750173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフッ、ガルドランじゃ、<line-break>やっぱり無理だったよね。アハハ！<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. I knew Girldlan could not handle it. Hahaha!<end>"
KO: "후후, 갈드란으로는 역시 무리였네. 아하하!<end>"

## 750174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺様は未来の勇者ガルドラン！！<line-break>暗闇の中の王女の光を奪う悪党め！<line-break>　<line-break>この世に正義がある限り、いやさ<line-break>俺様がある限り、色惑の瞳を<line-break>売り飛ばさせはせん！　いくぞ！<end>"
EN: "I am Girldlan, hero of the future!! Villain, you would steal the light of a princess in darkness! So long as justice exists in this world―no, so long as I exist―I will not let you sell the Eye of Allure! Here I come!<end>"
KO: "나님은 미래의 용사 갈드란!! 어둠 속 왕녀의 빛을 빼앗는 악당 놈! 이 세상에 정의가 있는 한, 아니 나님이 있는 한 색혹의 눈동자를 팔아넘기게 두지 않겠다! 간다!<end>"

## 750175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バ、バカな。<line-break>この俺様が負ける…？　　　<line-break>　<line-break>いや、そんなはずはない！<line-break>そんなはずはない！！！<line-break>そんなはずはないんだあ！！<end>"
EN: "I-impossible. I, of all people, have lost...? No, that cannot be! It cannot be! It cannot be!!!<end>"
KO: "마, 말도 안 돼. 이 내가 진다고…? 아니, 그럴 리 없다! 그럴 리 없어!!!<end>"

## 750176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフッ、ガルドランじゃ、<line-break>やっぱり無理だったよね。アハハ！<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. I knew Girldlan could not handle it. Hahaha!<end>"
KO: "후후, 갈드란으로는 역시 무리였네. 아하하!<end>"

## 750177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふん！！<line-break>味わったか、俺の正義の熱い魂を！<end>"
EN: "Hmph!! Have you felt the blazing soul of my justice?<end>"
KO: "흥!! 맛보았느냐, 내 정의의 뜨거운 혼을!<end>"

## 750178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これか？　悲しみの王女の<line-break>求めし色惑の瞳とは！？<end>"
EN: "Is this it? The Eye of Allure sought by the sorrowful princess!?<end>"
KO: "이건가? 슬픔의 왕녀가 찾던 색혹의 눈동자란 것이!?<end>"

## 750179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだよ。ありがとう。<line-break>勇者様！<end>"
EN: "That is it. Thank you, Hero!<end>"
KO: "맞아. 고마워, 용사님!<end>"

## 750180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "礼には及ばない。<line-break>俺様は悪ある所必ず現れるのだ！<line-break>　<line-break>では、さらばだ！<line-break>世界中が俺様を呼んでるぜ！<line-break>でやっ！<end>"
EN: "Think nothing of it. I appear wherever evil is found! Farewell! The whole world is calling for me! Hyah!<end>"
KO: "감사는 필요 없다. 이 몸은 악이 있는 곳엔 반드시 나타난다! 그럼 안녕이다! 온 세상이 나를 부르고 있다! 이야앗!<end>"

## 750188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "役立たずだけど、使いたくなる　　　<line-break>奴だよねえ。ガルドランって。<line-break>　<line-break>ちょっと、こういうことがあるよって<line-break>吹き込んでやったら、あのザマさ。<line-break>熱いよねえ？　アハハハ！<end>"
EN: "He is useless, but you cannot help wanting to use him, can you? Girldlan, I mean. I merely planted the idea that something like this might happen, and look at him. He is so passionate, is he not? Hahaha!<end>"
KO: "쓸모없는데도 자꾸 쓰고 싶어지는 녀석이지, 갈드란은. 이런 일이 있을 거라고 살짝 불어넣었더니 저 꼴이야. 뜨겁지? 아하하하!<end>"

## 750190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かに色惑の瞳、受け取ったよ。<line-break>これでアトレイアに光が与えられる。　<line-break>　<line-break>アトレイアには君の契約違反は<line-break>黙っていてあげるよ。<line-break>そのかわり、報酬はなし、ね。アハハ！<end>"
EN: "I have received the Eye of Allure. Now Atleia can be given light. I'll keep your breach of contract from Atleia. But you will receive no reward. Hahaha!<end>"
KO: "색혹의 눈동자, 확실히 받았어. 이걸로 아트레이아에게 빛을 줄 수 있어. 아트레이아에게는 네 계약 위반을 말하지 않을게. 대신 보수는 없어. 아하하!<end>"

## 750191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あ、<value:$28>様、<line-break>ようこそ、いらっしゃいました。　<line-break>　<line-break>この間はありがとうございました。<line-break><value:$28>様のおかげで、<line-break>目が見えるようになって…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>... Welcome. I'm so glad you've come. Thank you for what you did the other day. Thanks to you, <value:$28>, I can see now...<end>"
KO: "…아, <value:$28> 님, 어서 오세요. 지난번에는 정말 감사했습니다. <value:$28> 님 덕분에 눈이 보이게 돼서…<end>"

## 750192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それに、また来ていただけるなんて。<line-break>私、本当にうれしいです。<line-break>　<line-break>…こんなによくしていただいて<line-break>本当にいいのかと思うくらいです。<end>"
EN: "And now you've come to see me again. I'm truly happy. You've been so kind to me, I almost wonder whether I deserve it.<end>"
KO: "게다가 또 와 주시다니. 정말 기뻐요. …이렇게까지 잘해 주셔도 되는 건지 생각할 정도예요.<end>"

## 750193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すみません<line-break>この部屋…暗い…ですか？　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>でも、目が見えることが周りに知れると<line-break>この身が危険になるだろうと<line-break>シャリ様がおっしゃいますので…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. Is it... dark in here? Shalli says I would be in danger if anyone learned that I can see, so...<end>"
KO: "죄송해요. 이 방… 어두운가요? 하지만 눈이 보인다는 걸 주변에서 알면 위험해질 거라고 샤리 님이 말씀하셔서….<end>"

## 750194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "明かりも普段は消すようにしています。<line-break>侍女にも暇を出しました。<line-break>必要ないですし、秘密が知れると…。<end>"
EN: "I usually keep the lights out. I also dismissed my maid. There was no need to keep a maid, and if the secret got out...<end>"
KO: "불도 평소엔 꺼 두려고 합니다. 시녀도 쉬게 했어요. 필요도 없고, 비밀이 알려지면….<end>"

## 750195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "平気です。さびしくは…ありません。<line-break>孤独と闇には、なれていますもの。<end>"
EN: "I'm all right. I'm not... lonely. I am accustomed to solitude and darkness.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아요. 외롭지는… 않아요. 고독과 어둠에는 익숙하니까요.<end>"

## 750196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…それに、少しの光は、余計に<line-break>闇を濃くしてしまうものですから。<end>"
EN: "Besides, even a little light only makes the darkness seem deeper.<end>"
KO: "…게다가 작은 빛은 오히려 어둠을 더 짙게 만들기도 하니까요.<end>"

## 750197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>様、<line-break>どうか、またいらしてください。<line-break>…お願い、します。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Please come and see me again. Please.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28> 님, 부디 또 와 주세요. …부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 750198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…すみません。<line-break>お恥ずかしい姿ですね。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. What an embarrassing sight I must be.<end>"
KO: "…죄송합니다. 부끄러운 모습을 보였네요.<end>"

## 750199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます。<line-break>もう…大丈夫です。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. I'm... all right now.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 이제… 괜찮아요.<end>"

## 750200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様<line-break>ようこそいらっしゃいました。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Welcome.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 어서 오세요.<end>"

## 750201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ様は王宮では<line-break>光の王女と呼ばれているそうです。<line-break>　<line-break>輝くようなお美しさと<line-break>明るく、やさしいお心を<line-break>みなが讃えているのでしょうね。<end>"
EN: "They say Lady Tiana is known at court as the Princess of Light. I suppose everyone admires her radiant beauty and her bright, gentle heart.<end>"
KO: "티아나 님은 왕궁에서 빛의 왕녀라고 불린다고 합니다. 빛나는 아름다움과 밝고 상냥한 마음을 모두가 칭송하는 것이겠지요.<end>"

## 750202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうすると、私はさしずめ<line-break>闇の王女でしょうか。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Then I suppose that would make me the Princess of Darkness.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 나는 말하자면 어둠의 왕녀일까요.<end>"

## 750203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなことは言わない方がいい<end>"
EN: "You shouldn't say that.<end>"
KO: "그런 말은 하지 않는 편이 좋아<end>"

## 750204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その通りだ<end>"
EN: "That's right.<end>"
KO: "그 말대로야<end>"

## 750205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…す、すみません。<line-break>私、変なことを言ってしまって…。　<line-break>　<line-break>でも、つい…、比べてしまって……。<end>"
EN: "I-I'm sorry. That was a strange thing to say. I just couldn't help comparing myself to her...<end>"
KO: "…죄, 죄송합니다. 제가 이상한 말을 해 버려서…. 하지만 저도 모르게… 비교하게 돼서…….<end>"

## 750206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そう…ですよね。<line-break>…その通りですよね。<end>"
EN: "Yes... you're right. Quite right.<end>"
KO: "…그렇…군요. …말씀하신 대로예요.<end>"

## 750207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　ええ…。<line-break>確かに、闇は、<line-break>心に安らぎを与えてくれます。<end>"
EN: "What? Oh... yes. It is true that darkness can bring peace to the heart.<end>"
KO: "예? 네…. 확실히 어둠은 마음에 안식을 줍니다.<end>"

## 750208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私といると、<line-break>ホッとする…のですか？<end>"
EN: "You find being with me... comforting?<end>"
KO: "저와 있으면 마음이 놓이시나요…?<end>"

## 750209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、ありがとうございます。　<line-break>とてもうれしいです。<line-break>　<line-break>私、今、みなが本当に<line-break>闇の王女って呼んでくれたら、<line-break>いいなと思いました。<end>"
EN: "Hehe, thank you. That makes me very happy. Just now, I found myself wishing everyone really would call me the Princess of Darkness.<end>"
KO: "후후, 감사합니다. 정말 기뻐요. 지금은 모두가 정말 저를 어둠의 왕녀라고 불러 줬으면 좋겠다고 생각했어요.<end>"

## 750210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの…、<value:$28>様。<line-break>ひとつ、伺ってよろしいでしょうか？<line-break>　<line-break>ティアナ様っておきれいですか？<end>"
EN: "Um, <value:$28>... may I ask you something? Is Lady Tiana beautiful?<end>"
KO: "저기… <value:$28>님. 하나 여쭤봐도 될까요? 티아나 님은 예쁜가요?<end>"

## 750211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きれいだ<end>"
EN: "Yes, she is.<end>"
KO: "예쁘다<end>"

## 750212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それほどでもない<end>"
EN: "Not especially.<end>"
KO: "그 정도는 아니야<end>"

## 750213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう…ですよね…。<line-break>本当におきれいな方ですよね。<end>"
EN: "Yes... of course. She really is beautiful, isn't she?<end>"
KO: "그렇…죠…. 정말 아름다운 분이네요.<end>"

## 750214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、私に気を使って<line-break>いただかなくても結構ですわ。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様は<line-break>本当におやさしいですね。<end>"
EN: "Hehe, you needn't say that for my sake. You really are very kind, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "후후, 저를 신경 쓰지 않으셔도 괜찮아요. <value:$28>님은 정말 상냥하시네요.<end>"

## 750215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実は、このあいだ<line-break>ティアナ様にお会いしたのです。　　　<line-break>…本当に、お美しい方でした。<line-break>それに、とても気さくで<line-break>おやさしい方ですね。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Actually, I met Lady Tiana the other day. She truly was beautiful. And so friendly and kind.<end>"
KO: "사실 얼마 전에 티아나 님을 뵈었어요. …정말 아름다운 분이셨어요. 그리고 아주 소탈하고 상냥하시더군요.<end>"

## 750216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この間、私が部屋から出ているところを<line-break>見つかってしまって…。<line-break>　<line-break>ティアナ様は、私の目が不自由だと<line-break>気遣ってくださって、私を部屋まで<line-break>連れていってくださろうとしたのです。<end>"
EN: "The other day, Lady Tiana happened to find me while I was out of my room. She was worried because she thought I couldn't see, and offered to lead me back to my room.<end>"
KO: "얼마 전 제가 방에서 나온 걸 들켜 버렸어요…. 티아나 님은 제가 앞을 못 보는 걸 걱정해 주셔서 제 방까지 데려다주려고 하셨어요.<end>"

## 750217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも私、ひとりで帰れるからと<line-break>逃げてしまいました。<line-break>　<end>そんなことをしては、<line-break>目が見えることを知られてしまう<line-break>かもしれないとも思ったのですが、<end>"
EN: "But I told her I could manage on my own and ran away.<end>I knew that might give away the fact that I can see, but...<end>"
KO: "하지만 저는 혼자 돌아갈 수 있다고 말하고 도망쳐 버렸습니다.<end>그러면 제가 앞을 볼 수 있다는 걸 들킬지도 모른다고 생각했지만,<end>"

## 750218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…この暗い部屋を見られるのが、<line-break>恥ずかしかったのです。<end>"
EN: "I was embarrassed to let her see this dark room.<end>"
KO: "…이 어두운 방을 보여 주는 게 부끄러웠어요.<end>"

## 750219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ様には<line-break>もうお会いになりましたか？<end>"
EN: "Have you met Lady Tiana yet?<end>"
KO: "티아나 님은 이미 만나셨나요?<end>"

## 750220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "真っ先に会いに行った<end>"
EN: "I went to see her first.<end>"
KO: "가장 먼저 만나러 갔다<end>"

## 750221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナではなく、アトレイアに会いに来た<end>"
EN: "I came to see Atleia, not Tiana.<end>"
KO: "티아나가 아니라 아트레이아를 만나러 왔다<end>"

## 750222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですよね。ティアナ様は<line-break>本当に輝いておられますもの。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、ついででもいいんです。<line-break>私に会いに来てくださって、<line-break>うれしいです。<end>"
EN: "Of course. Lady Tiana truly does shine. But I don't mind if I was only a stop along the way. I'm glad you came to see me.<end>"
KO: "그렇죠. 티아나 님은 정말 빛나시는 분이니까요. 그래도 덤이라도 좋아요. 저를 만나러 와 주셔서 기뻐요.<end>"

## 750223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…。<end>"
EN: "Oh...<end>"
KO: "아…<end>"

## 750224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私、うれしいです。<end>"
EN: "I'm... so glad.<end>"
KO: "…저, 기뻐요.<end>"

## 750225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様、<line-break>もう、行かれるのですか…？　　　<line-break>　<line-break>…いえ、お引き留めして<line-break>申しわけありませんでした。<line-break>どうかまた、いらしてください。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>... are you leaving already? No, I'm sorry. I shouldn't have kept you. Please come and see me again.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님, 벌써 가시는 건가요…? …아니요, 붙잡아서 죄송했습니다. 부디 또 와 주세요.<end>"

## 750226
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーもう、また来たの？<line-break>意外と<value:$28>って<line-break>せっかちなんだね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, come on, back again already? I didn't expect you to be so impatient, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아, 또 왔어? 의외로 <value:$28>도 성급하네.<end>"

## 750227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、まだまだだよ。<line-break>もっともっと、たくさんの<line-break>冒険をしてきてくれなくちゃ。<line-break>そしたら、僕がちゃんと<line-break>王女様に会わせてあげるからさ。<line-break>じゃ、また、がんばってきてよ。<end>"
EN: "But you're still nowhere near ready. You'll have to go on lots and lots more adventures first. Then I'll make sure you get to meet the princess. So off you go. Do your best, and come back later.<end>"
KO: "하지만 아직 멀었어. 더더욱 많은 모험을 하고 와야 해. 그러면 내가 제대로 왕녀님을 만나게 해 줄 테니까. 그럼 또 힘내고 와.<end>"

## 750228
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "暗くて何も見えないかい？<line-break>じゃ、今明かりをつけよう。<end>"
EN: "Too dark to see anything? Then let me turn on the light.<end>"
KO: "어두워서 아무것도 안 보여? 그럼 지금 불을 켤게.<end>"

## 750229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>また会えると思ってたよ。<end>"
EN: "Hello, <value:$28>. I thought we'd meet again.<end>"
KO: "여, <value:$28>. 다시 만날 줄 알았어.<end>"

## 750230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はじめまして。<line-break>僕はシャリ。人は東方の博士なんて<line-break>呼んでくれたりもするんだ。<line-break>よろしくね、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Hello. I'm Shalli. Some people even call me the Scholar from the East. It's nice to meet you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "처음 뵙겠습니다. 저는 샤리. 사람들은 동방의 박사라고도 부르죠. 잘 부탁해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 750231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここは、君のまだ知らない　　　　　<line-break>お姫様の部屋だよ。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、彼女のことを話すには<line-break>君はまだちょっと頼りないな。<line-break>だから、会わせるわけにもいかない。<end>"
EN: "This is the room of a princess you haven't met yet. But I can't tell you about her when I'm not sure I can count on you just yet. So I can't let you meet her, either.<end>"
KO: "여긴 네가 아직 모르는 공주님의 방이야. 하지만 그녀 이야기를 하기엔 넌 아직 좀 미덥지 못해. 그래서 만나게 해 줄 수도 없어.<end>"

## 750232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もっと、いろんな冒険を<line-break>体験してきてくれなくちゃ。<line-break>お姫様を守る騎士様としてね。<line-break>じゃ、またおいでよ。<line-break>いつかきっと、王女様に<line-break>会わせてあげるよ。じゃあね。<end>"
EN: "You'll have to go on plenty more adventures of all kinds―as the knight who'll protect the princess. Come back again sometime. One day, I'll make sure you get to meet the princess. See you.<end>"
KO: "더 여러 모험을 경험해 와야 해. 공주님을 지키는 기사님으로서 말이지. 그럼 또 와. 언젠가 꼭 왕녀님을 만나게 해 줄게. 잘 가.<end>"

## 750233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気をつかっていただいて　　　　<line-break>ありがとうございます。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>様が、こうして<line-break>部屋に来てくださるだけで<line-break>私は、充分です…。<end>"
EN: "Thank you for being so considerate. Just having you visit me here in my room is more than enough, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "신경 써주셔서 감사합니다. <value:$28>님께서 이렇게 방에 와주시는 것만으로 저는 충분합니다…<end>"

## 750234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様……。<line-break>………………。<line-break>いえ、なんでもありません。<line-break>退屈な部屋でしょうが<line-break>ゆっくりしていってください…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... ...No, it's nothing. I'm afraid this is a rather dull room, but please make yourself comfortable...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님……. …아니요, 아무것도 아닙니다. 지루한 방이겠지만 편히 쉬다 가세요….<end>"

## 750235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec075-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…これは、予備メッセージ１０…。<line-break>アトレイア、Ｖ１です。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "…이건 예비 메시지 10…. 아트레이아, V1입니다.<end>"

## 750236

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア　ボリューム１～終了<end>"

KO: "아트레이아 볼륨1 끝<end>"

## 760000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア　ボリューム３～開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "아트레이아 볼륨3 시작<end>"

## 760001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主…。<line-break>　<line-break>君が生きていてくれて<line-break>僕は嬉しいよ。<line-break>フフッ、ホントさ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Bearer of the Infinite Soul... I'm glad you're alive. Heh. I truly am.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자…. 네가 살아 있어 줘서 기뻐. 후후, 진심이야.<end>"

## 760002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう肩肘はらないでよ。<line-break>ただ、かわいそうな女の子のために<line-break>一肌脱ごうってだけなんだからさ。<end>"
EN: "No need to be so tense. I'm only asking you to help a poor girl, that's all.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 딱딱하게 굴지 마. 그냥 불쌍한 여자아이를 위해 한몫하자는 것뿐이니까.<end>"

## 760003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんにちは。　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>…だっけ？<line-break>　<line-break>僕はこの大陸から東にある<line-break>遠い遠い所から来た魔道学士、<line-break>東方の博士シャリ。<end>"
EN: "Hello. You're <value:$28>, was it? I'm Shalli, a scholar of magic known as the Scholar from the East. I come from a far, faraway land east of this continent.<end>"
KO: "안녕하세요. <value:$28>…였죠? 저는 이 대륙에서 동쪽으로 아주 멀리 떨어진 곳에서 온 마도학사, 동방의 박사 샤리입니다.<end>"

## 760004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人によっては僕のことを<line-break>道化と呼んでいるようだけどね。<line-break>まあ、そんなことは置いといて…。<end>"
EN: "Some people apparently call me a jester. But never mind that...<end>"
KO: "사람에 따라선 나를 광대라고 부르는 모양이지만. 뭐, 그런 건 됐고….<end>"

## 760005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君は、ロストールの王女様を　　<line-break>知ってるかい？　っていっても<line-break>ティアナ王女じゃないよ。<line-break>もうひとりの…そう、<line-break>とてもかわいそうな、<line-break>孤独で忘れられた王女のことを。<end>"
EN: "Do you know the princess of Rostorl? Not Princess Tiana. I mean the other one... the poor, lonely princess everyone has forgotten.<end>"
KO: "너는 로스톨 왕녀님을 아니? 티아나 왕녀 말고. 또 한 명의… 그래, 아주 불쌍하고 외롭고 잊힌 왕녀를 말이야.<end>"

## 760006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未使用：王家の分家であるリューガ家の<line-break>若く、才気溢れる、野心的な当主　　　<line-break>レムオン・リューガをね。<line-break>だけど、実は、順番的には<line-break>ティアナ王女とエリエナイ公レムオンの<line-break>間に、ひとりの少女が入るんだ。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 왕가의 분가인 류가 가문의 젊고 재기 넘치며 야심찬 당주 레무온 류가를 말이지. 하지만 사실 순서로 따지면 티아나 왕녀와 에리에나이 공 레무온 사이에 한 소녀가 있어.<end>"

## 760007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その王女の名前はアトレイア・リュー。<line-break>先代王の遺子で、ティアナ王女にとって<line-break>従姉にあたるお姫様さ。<line-break>かわいそうに、赤ん坊の時に<line-break>大人の醜い争いに巻き込まれて、<line-break>視力を、光を失ったんだ。<end>"
EN: "Her name is Atleia Ryu. She's the previous king's daughter, and Princess Tiana's cousin. Poor thing. When she was just a baby, she was caught up in an ugly struggle between adults and lost her sight―her light.<end>"
KO: "그 왕녀의 이름은 아트레이아 류. 선왕의 유복자이자 티아나 왕녀의 사촌인 공주님이야. 불쌍하게도 아기였을 때 어른들의 추악한 다툼에 휘말려 시력, 빛을 잃었지.<end>"

## 760008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それから今日までの彼女は、無力で<line-break>害のない王族として、王城の暗く<line-break>静かな一室で孤独な人生を送ってきた。<line-break>ティアナ王女の、華やかで、<line-break>周りのみんなに慕われる生活とは<line-break>正反対の、暗く孤独な生活をね。<end>"
EN: "Ever since, she's lived alone as a powerless, harmless member of the royal family, shut away in a dark, quiet room of the castle. A bleak, lonely life―the very opposite of Princess Tiana's glittering life, surrounded by people who adore her.<end>"
KO: "그날부터 오늘까지 그녀는 무력하고 해가 되지 않는 왕족으로서 왕성의 어둡고 조용한 방 하나에서 외로운 인생을 보내 왔다. 티아나 왕녀의 화려하고 모두에게 사랑받는 생활과는 정반대인, 어둡고 외로운 삶을 말이야.<end>"

## 760009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ね、そろそろ、彼女が幸せになっても<line-break>いいころだと思わない？<line-break>君に協力して欲しいんだ。<line-break>色惑の瞳という魔法の宝石がある。<line-break>この色惑の瞳の力を使えば、<line-break>彼女に光を与えられる。<end>"
EN: "Don't you think it's about time she found some happiness? I want your help. There's a magical jewel called the Eye of Allure. Its power can give her back the light.<end>"
KO: "이제 슬슬 그녀도 행복해질 때가 됐다고 생각하지 않아? 네가 도와줬으면 해. 색혹의 눈동자라는 마법의 보석이 있어. 그 힘을 쓰면 그녀에게 빛을 줄 수 있어.<end>"

## 760010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼女のために、取ってきてほしいんだ。<line-break>もっちろん、報酬は用意するよ。<end>"
EN: "I want you to go and get it for her. Of course there'll be a reward.<end>"
KO: "그녀를 위해 가져와 줬으면 해. 물론 보수는 준비할게.<end>"

## 760011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受ける<end>"
EN: "Accept<end>"
KO: "수락한다<end>"

## 760012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受けない<end>"
EN: "Decline<end>"
KO: "맡지 않는다<end>"

## 760013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホント？　ありがとう。<line-break>色惑の瞳はロストール北西の　<line-break>邪眼の迷宮にあるんだ。<line-break>期待してるよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>僕と王女はロストールの王城で<line-break>待ってるからねえ。フフフッ。<end>"
EN: "Really? Thank you. The Eye of Allure lies in the Evil Eye Labyrinth, northwest of Rostorl. I'm counting on you, <value:$28>. The princess and I will be waiting at Rostorl Castle. Heh heh heh.<end>"
KO: "정말? 고마워. 색혹의 눈동자는 로스톨 북서쪽 사안의 미궁에 있어. 기대할게, <value:$28>. 나와 왕녀는 로스톨 왕성에서 기다리고 있을게. 후후후.<end>"

## 760014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうそう、これを渡さなきゃね。<line-break>これは、銀竜の首飾りっていって…、<line-break>ま、早い話が通行手形さ。<line-break>ロストールのお城の正門の右側に<line-break>秘密通路があるんだけど、<line-break>これがあれば通れるようになるから。<end>"
EN: "Oh, yes, I need to give you this. It's called the Silver Dragon Necklace... but to put it simply, it's a pass. There's a secret passage to the right of Rostorl Castle's main gate, and this will let you through.<end>"
KO: "아, 맞다. 이걸 줘야지. 이건 은룡의 목걸이라고 하는데…, 쉽게 말하면 통행증이야. 로스톨 성 정문 오른쪽에 비밀 통로가 있는데, 이게 있으면 지나갈 수 있어.<end>"

## 760015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、よろしくね、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I'll leave it to you, then, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 잘 부탁해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 760016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冷たいんだねぇ、君って。<line-break>いいよ。別口を当たるから。<line-break>じゃあねえ。フフフッ。<end>"
EN: "You're a cold one, aren't you? Fine. I'll ask someone else. See you. Heh heh heh.<end>"
KO: "차갑네, 너. 됐어. 다른 쪽을 알아볼 테니까. 잘 있어. 후후훗.<end>"

## 760017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>さん！<line-break>同じ宿に泊まっていたんですね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>! I didn't realize you were staying at this inn too.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님! 같은 여관에 묵고 있었군요.<end>"

## 760018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0あら、ボウヤ。<line-break>ちょうど、今あなたの話を<line-break>していたところなのよ。<line-break>ちょっと聞いてもいいかしら？<end>あら、子猫ちゃん。<line-break>ちょうど、今あなたの話を<line-break>していたところなのよ。<line-break>ちょっと聞いてもいいかしら？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Well, hello, young man. We were just talking about you. Mind if I ask you something?<end>Well, hello, kitten. We were just talking about you. Mind if I ask you something?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0어머, 꼬마야. 마침 지금 네 이야기를 하고 있었어. 하나 물어봐도 될까?<end>어머, 아기 고양이야. 마침 지금 네 이야기를 하고 있었어. 하나 물어봐도 될까?<end>"

## 760019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなた、アトレイア王女から<line-break>色惑の瞳を探してくるように<line-break>頼まれていたわよね？<line-break>あなたなら、その依頼をこなすことが<line-break>できたはずだわ。それなのに、<line-break>どうして引き受けなかったの？<end>"
EN: "Princess Atleia asked you to find the Eye of Allure, didn't she? You could have completed that job. So why did you turn it down?<end>"
KO: "당신, 아트레이아 왕녀에게 색혹의 눈동자를 찾아 달라는 부탁을 받았지? 당신이라면 그 의뢰를 해낼 수 있었을 텐데. 그런데 왜 맡지 않았어?<end>"

## 760020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜ、その話を知ってるのかって<line-break>顔してるわね。王女の使いの人から<line-break>話を聞いたのよ。<line-break>あなたがこの依頼を拒否したってね。<line-break>だから、かわりにあたしたちが、<line-break>その依頼を受けておいたわ。<end>"
EN: "You're wondering how I know, aren't you? I heard from the princess's messenger that you'd turned down the job. So we took it in your place.<end>"
KO: "왜 그 이야기를 알고 있냐는 표정이네. 왕녀의 사자에게 들었어. 네가 이 의뢰를 거절했다면서. 그래서 대신 우리가 그 의뢰를 받아 뒀지.<end>"

## 760021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "色惑の瞳の力で、<line-break>目が見えるようになった王女は<line-break>とても喜んでおられたわ。<line-break>ねぇ、<value:$28>、<line-break>どうしてこの仕事を受けなかったの？<line-break>王女がかわいそうだと思わなかったの？<end>"
EN: "The princess was overjoyed when the Eye of Allure restored her sight. Tell me, <value:$28>, why didn't you take the job? Didn't you feel sorry for her?<end>"
KO: "색혹의 눈동자 힘으로 앞을 보게 된 왕녀님은 아주 기뻐하셨어. 있잖아, <value:$28>, 왜 이 일을 맡지 않았어? 왕녀님이 불쌍하다고 생각하지 않았어?<end>"

## 760022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリの依頼は受けられない<end>"
EN: "I couldn't accept Shalli's request.<end>"
KO: "샤리의 의뢰는 받을 수 없다<end>"

## 760023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…返す言葉もない<end>"
EN: "…I have no answer.<end>"
KO: "…할 말이 없군<end>"

## 760024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリ、ですって？<line-break>いいえ、あたしたちに仕事を依頼<line-break>してきたのは王女の侍女だったわ。<end>"
EN: "Shalli? No. It was the princess's lady-in-waiting who hired us.<end>"
KO: "샤리라고요? 아니요, 우리에게 일을 의뢰한 건 왕녀님의 시녀였어요.<end>"

## 760025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうやら、私たちは<line-break>一杯食わされたようだな。<end>"
EN: "It seems we've been duped.<end>"
KO: "아무래도 우리가 한 방 먹은 모양이군.<end>"

## 760026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんですって？<end>"
EN: "What did you say?<end>"
KO: "뭐라고요?<end>"

## 760027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "例の侍女は、あの空中都市を<line-break>復活させた強大な力を持つ<line-break>少年が化けた者だったようだ。<end>"
EN: "It appears that lady-in-waiting was actually the boy with the immense power that revived the Floating City, in disguise.<end>"
KO: "그 시녀는 저 공중도시를 부활시킨 강대한 힘을 지닌 소년이 변장한 모습이었던 모양이야.<end>"

## 760028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな…、<line-break>でも…………。<end>"
EN: "No... But...<end>"
KO: "그럴 수가…, 하지만………….<end>"

## 760029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ノエル、<line-break>ひとつ、聞いておく。<line-break>　<line-break>お前はアトレイア王女のそばに、<line-break>シャリという奴がいるのを知っていたら<line-break>王女を助けるのを断ったか？<end>"
EN: "Noel, I want to ask you one thing. If you'd known that someone called Shalli was at Princess Atleia's side, would you have refused to help the princess?<end>"
KO: "…노엘, 하나 물어 두지. 네가 아트레이아 왕녀 곁에 샤리라는 녀석이 있다는 걸 알았다면 왕녀를 돕는 걸 거절했겠나?<end>"

## 760030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいえ。<end>"
EN: "No.<end>"
KO: "아니요.<end>"

## 760031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、王女を助けたことを<line-break>後悔してはいないな？<end>"
EN: "So you don't regret helping the princess. Is that right?<end>"
KO: "그럼 왕녀를 구한 걸 후회하진 않는 거지?<end>"

## 760032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい。<end>"
EN: "Yes.<end>"
KO: "네.<end>"

## 760033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それなら、いい。<end>"
EN: "That's all that matters.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 됐어.<end>"

## 760034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたしもノエルと同じよ。<line-break>結果的には、そのシャリって奴の<line-break>思惑に乗ってしまったけど、<line-break>後悔はしていないわ。<line-break>かわいそうな王女を放っておくなんて<line-break>できないもの。<end>"
EN: "I feel the same way Noel does. We may have ended up playing right into Shalli's hands, but I don't regret it. I couldn't have just abandoned the poor princess.<end>"
KO: "나도 노엘과 같은 생각이야. 결과적으로는 샤리라는 녀석의 의도대로 움직인 셈이지만 후회는 없어. 불쌍한 왕녀를 내버려 둘 수는 없잖아.<end>"

## 760035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0呼び止めてしまって悪かったわね。<line-break>あたしたち、そろそろ行くわ。<line-break>またね、ボウヤ。<end>呼び止めてしまって悪かったわね。<line-break>あたしたち、そろそろ行くわ。<line-break>またね、子猫ちゃん。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Sorry for stopping you. We should be going. See you around, young man.<end>Sorry for stopping you. We should be going. See you around, kitten.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0불러 세워서 미안했네. 우리는 이제 가 볼게. 또 보자, 꼬마야.<end>불러 세워서 미안했네. 우리는 이제 가 볼게. 또 보자, 아기 고양이.<end>"

## 760036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの、<value:$28>さん。<line-break>今回は違った行動を<line-break>とってしまいましたね。<line-break>でも、わたし、<line-break><value:$28>さんなら、<line-break>わかってくださると信じてますから。<end>"
EN: "Um, <value:$28>... We ended up making different choices this time. But I trust that <value:$28> will understand.<end>"
KO: "저, 저기, <value:$28> 씨. 이번에는 다른 행동을 해 버렸네요. 하지만 전 <value:$28> 씨라면 이해해 주실 거라 믿어요.<end>"

## 760037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、失礼します。<end>"
EN: "Please excuse me.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 이만 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 760038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…何も言うことはないの？<line-break>まぁ、ぐだぐだと言いわけするよりは<line-break>カッコいいかもしれないけど…<line-break>ちょっと、残念ね。<line-break>あなたなら、王女を放っておくなんて<line-break>しないと思っていたのに。<end>"
EN: "You have nothing to say? Well, I suppose keeping quiet is more impressive than making a lot of tedious excuses... but I'm still disappointed. I never thought you'd leave the princess to her fate.<end>"
KO: "…아무 말도 할 게 없어? 뭐, 장황하게 변명하는 것보다는 멋질지도 모르지만… 조금 아쉽네. 당신이라면 왕녀를 내버려 두진 않을 줄 알았는데.<end>"

## 760039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0呼び止めてしまって悪かったわね。<line-break>あたしたち、そろそろ行くわ。<line-break>またね、ボウヤ。<end>呼び止めてしまって悪かったわね。<line-break>あたしたち、そろそろ行くわ。<line-break>またね、子猫ちゃん。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Sorry for stopping you. We should be going. See you around, young man.<end>Sorry for stopping you. We should be going. See you around, kitten.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0불러 세워서 미안했네. 우리는 이제 가 볼게. 또 보자, 꼬마야.<end>불러 세워서 미안했네. 우리는 이제 가 볼게. 또 보자, 아기 고양이.<end>"

## 760040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの、<value:$28>さんには<line-break><value:$28>さんの事情が<line-break>あったんですよね。<line-break><value:$28>さんが、<line-break>何の理由もなしに、かわいそうな王女を<line-break>放っておくはずありませんから。<end>"
EN: "Um, <value:$28>... You must have had your reasons, <value:$28>. I know you, <value:$28>, would never abandon a poor princess for no reason.<end>"

## 760060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やぁ。色惑の瞳、遅いよん。<line-break>まさか、猫ばばしようと<line-break>してたんじゃないよね。<end>"
EN: "Hey, you're late bringing the Eye of Allure! You weren't thinking of keeping it for yourself, were you?<end>"
KO: "안녕. 색혹의 눈동자, 늦었네. 설마 슬쩍 먹으려고 했던 건 아니지?<end>"

## 760061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "毎度あり～。<line-break>これは謝礼だよ、じゃあね。<end>"
EN: "Much obliged! Here's your reward. See you around.<end>"
KO: "매번 고마워~. 이건 사례야, 그럼 안녕.<end>"

## 760062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "３０００ギア手に入れた。<end>"
EN: "Obtained 3,000 Gea.<end>"
KO: "3000기어를 손에 넣었다.<end>"

## 760063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Oh, no. Don't even think about it. I'll be taking that for the poor princess.<end>"
KO: "안 돼. 그런 생각 하면. 불쌍한 공주님을 위해 그건 가져갈 거야.<end>"
