jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：けっ。実力もねぇのに<line-break>カッコ付けやがって！<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 쳇. 실력도 없으면서 폼 잡기는!<end>"

## 760070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：アトレイア…姫様。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 아트레이아… 공주님.<end>"

## 760071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：放っておいて！！<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 내버려 둬!!<end>"

## 760072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：目なんて見えなければよかった。<line-break>ずっと闇の中にいれば、<line-break>自分の姿に気が付かなかったのに。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 눈 같은 건 보이지 않았으면 좋았어. 계속 어둠 속에 있었다면 내 모습도 몰랐을 텐데.<end>"

## 760073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：ごめんなさい。あなたに恥をかかせて。<line-break>私みたいに醜いものが付いていって、<line-break>ごめんなさい。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 미안해요. 당신을 창피하게 해서. 저처럼 추한 것이 따라다녀서 미안해요.<end>"

## 760074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：おっと、うらみっこなしだぜ？<line-break>お互い仕事次第で敵味方だ。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 어이, 서로 원망은 없기다? 일에 따라 적도 되고 아군도 되는 거잖아.<end>"

## 760075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：よう、久しぶりだな。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미번역: 여어, 오랜만이군.<end>"

## 760076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：あんなにほめられたのは、<line-break>生まれて初めてだったものですから…。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 그렇게 칭찬받은 건 태어나서 처음이라서요….<end>"

## 760077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：歌なら歌えます！<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 노래라면 부를 수 있어요!<end>"

## 760078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：おいおい、この娘、<line-break>一滴も飲んでないよなぁ。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 이봐, 이 아가씨 한 방울도 안 마셨지?<end>"

## 760079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：ハハハ。<line-break>おおかた雰囲気に酔ったんだろう。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 하하하. 아마 분위기에 취했겠지.<end>"

## 760080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：ちぇ、せっかく僕の綺麗な<line-break>歌の出番だったのになぁ。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ま、いいや。<value:$28>の<line-break>ためにいい曲弾いちゃうよ！<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 쳇, 모처럼 내 아름다운 노래가 나올 차례였는데. 뭐, 됐어. <value:$28>을 위해 좋은 곡을 연주해 줄게!<end>"

## 760081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：ハハハ、あなたの歌のおかげで<line-break>客も盛り上がって、こっちも大繁盛さ。<line-break>少ないけどとっときな。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 하하하, 당신 노래 덕분에 손님들도 신나고 우리도 장사가 잘됐어. 적지만 받아 둬.<end>"

## 760082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：お金は結構ですから、あの…。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 돈은 됐으니까, 저기….<end>"

## 760083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：…ん？なんだい。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: …응? 뭐야.<end>"

## 760084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：この…メニューを<line-break>いただけませんでしょうか…。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 이… 메뉴를 받을 수 있을까요….<end>"

## 760085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：フフ、妙なものが気に入っちまったんだなぁ。<line-break>よし、特別だ。お嬢ちゃんに差し上げよう。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 후후, 묘한 물건이 마음에 들었나 보군. 좋아, 특별히 아가씨에게 주지.<end>"

## 760086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：私、今日、世界中に人がたくさんいて<line-break>よかったって思います。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 오늘은 세상에 사람이 많이 있어서 다행이라고 생각해요.<end>"

## 760087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：（これは…人形だ。）<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미번역: (이건… 인형이다.)<end>"

## 760088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：困るよ、調教の邪魔されちゃ…。<line-break>…さて、自己紹介も終わったことだし<line-break>…死んでくれる？<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 곤란한데, 조교를 방해하면…. …자, 자기소개도 끝났으니… 죽어 줄래?<end>"

## 760089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未：うふ…ふふ…ふ…。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용: 우후… 후후… 후….<end>"

## 760090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーもう、また来たの？<line-break>意外と<value:$28>って<line-break>せっかちなんだね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, honestly. Back again? You're surprisingly impatient, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아, 또 왔어? 의외로 <value:$28>도 성급하네.<end>"

## 760091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここに来る前にまず、　　　　　<line-break>冒険してきたらどうかな？<line-break>ほら、君って冒険者なんだし。<line-break>この町にもギルドはあるんだし、<line-break>まずはそこに行って、<line-break>色々と経験をつんできてよ。<end>"
EN: "Why don't you do some adventuring before coming here? You are an adventurer, after all. There's a Guild in this town, so go there first and get some experience.<end>"
KO: "여기 오기 전에 우선 모험부터 해 보는 게 어때? 너 모험가잖아. 이 마을에도 길드가 있으니까 거기 가서 여러 경험을 쌓아 와.<end>"

## 760092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "暗くて何も見えないかい？<line-break>じゃ、今明かりをつけよう。<end>"
EN: "Too dark to see anything? Then let me turn on the light.<end>"
KO: "어두워서 아무것도 안 보여? 그럼 지금 불을 켤게.<end>"

## 760093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>またあえると思ってたよ。<end>"
EN: "Hello, <value:$28>. I thought I'd see you again.<end>"
KO: "여, <value:$28>. 다시 만날 줄 알았어.<end>"

## 760094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はじめまして。<line-break>僕はシャリ。人は東方の博士なんて<line-break>呼んでくれたりもするんだ。<line-break>よろしくね。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I'm Shalli. Some people even call me the Scholar from the East. Nice to meet you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "처음 뵙겠습니다. 저는 샤리. 사람들은 동방의 박사라고도 부르죠. 잘 부탁해요, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 760095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここは、君のまだ知らない<line-break>王女様の部屋だよ。<line-break>君には、ぜひ彼女に会ってもらいたい。<line-break>でも、その前に、もっとこの国のこと<line-break>知ってもらいたいな。<line-break>例えば、町のこと、貴族のこと…。<end>"
EN: "This room belongs to a princess you haven't met yet. I'd really like you to meet her. But first, I'd like you to learn more about this country―the town, the nobility...<end>"
KO: "여긴 네가 아직 모르는 왕녀님의 방이야. 꼭 그녀를 만나게 해 주고 싶어. 하지만 그 전에 이 나라에 대해 좀 더 알았으면 해. 예를 들면 마을이나 귀족 같은 것들….<end>"

## 760096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、もうひとりの　　　　　　<line-break>お姫様のこと。<line-break>　<line-break>だから、今日はまだおあずけ。<line-break>もっと経験をつんできたら<line-break>きっと、彼女に会わせてあげるよ。<end>"
EN: "And about another princess. So you'll have to wait for now. Once you've gained more experience, I'll be sure to introduce you to her.<end>"
KO: "그리고 또 한 명의 공주님 이야기. 그러니 오늘은 아직 안 돼. 경험을 더 쌓고 오면 분명 그녀를 만나게 해 줄게.<end>"

## 760097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、頼んだからね。<line-break>色惑の瞳、よろしく～。<end>"
EN: "All right, I'm counting on you. Bring me the Eye of Allure, okay?<end>"
KO: "그럼 부탁할게. 색혹의 눈동자, 잘 부탁해~.<end>"

## 760098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれ？　<value:$28>じゃない。<line-break>タイミング悪いねえ。お姫様は今　<line-break>侍女と一緒に湯浴みをしてるよ。<line-break>それよりも、ここに来たってことは<line-break>あれ、持って来てくれたの？<line-break>さっすがー！<end>"
EN: "Oh, if it isn't <value:$28>. Bad timing―the princess is bathing with her lady-in-waiting right now. Never mind that. Since you're here, does that mean you brought it? I knew you'd come through!<end>"
KO: "어라? <value:$28>잖아. 타이밍 안 좋네. 공주님은 지금 시녀와 함께 목욕 중이야. 그보다 여기 왔다는 건 그거 가져온 거야? 역시~!<end>"

## 760099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　あ、まだなの？<line-break>じゃ、今日は何しに来たの？<line-break>まさか、お姫様に会うためだけ？　<line-break>ちょっと～、そんなヒマあったら<line-break>早く色惑の瞳持って来てよ～。<line-break>それがお姫様のためなんだからさ。<end>"
EN: "What? Oh, you haven't brought it yet? Then what are you doing here today? Don't tell me you came just to see the princess. Come on, if you've got time for that, hurry up and bring the Eye of Allure. It's for the princess's sake.<end>"
KO: "어? 아, 아직이야? 그럼 오늘은 뭐 하러 온 거야? 설마 공주님 만나려고 온 것뿐이야? 야~ 그럴 시간 있으면 빨리 색혹의 눈동자나 가져와. 그게 공주님을 위한 일이니까.<end>"

## 760100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、頼むからね、ホントに。<end>"
EN: "All right, I'm counting on you. I mean it.<end>"
KO: "그럼 부탁할게, 정말로.<end>"

## 760101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、そうそう、<line-break>言い忘れてたけど念のためね。<line-break>君、銀竜の首飾り、持ってるよね？<line-break>それ使ってお城の地下通路に入って<line-break>奥のガレキを登ってきてよ。<line-break>僕たち、そこで待ってるからさ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, right. I forgot to mention this, but just to be safe: you have the Silver Dragon Necklace, don't you? Use it to enter the castle's underground passage, then climb up the rubble farther in. We'll be waiting there.<end>"
KO: "아, 맞다. 말하는 걸 잊었는데 혹시 모르니. 너 은룡의 목걸이 가지고 있지? 그걸 써서 성 지하통로로 들어가 안쪽 잔해를 타고 올라와. 우리는 거기서 기다릴게.<end>"

## 760102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec076-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "色惑の瞳を奪われた。<end>"
EN: "The Eye of Allure was taken.<end>"
KO: "색혹의 눈동자를 빼앗겼다.<end>"

## 770001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…、<value:$28>様。<line-break>またいらしてくださって<line-break>本当にうれしく思います。<end>"
EN: "Oh... <value:$28>. I'm truly glad you've come to see me again.<end>"
KO: "아… <value:$28> 님. 또 와 주셔서 정말 기쁩니다.<end>"

## 770002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、この部屋はこんなに薄暗くて…。<line-break>私、あなたがいらっしゃらなければ<line-break>いいと思う時があるんです。<end>"
EN: "But this room is so dim... Sometimes I find myself wishing you wouldn't come.<end>"
KO: "하지만 이 방은 이렇게 어둑하고…. 가끔은 당신이 오시지 않았으면 좋겠다고 생각할 때도 있어요.<end>"

## 770003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは広く明るい世界で<line-break>冒険をしていらっしゃいます。<line-break>そんなあなたにはこの薄暗い部屋は…。<end>"
EN: "You go adventuring in that vast, bright world. For someone like you, this dim room...<end>"
KO: "당신은 넓고 밝은 세계를 모험하고 계세요. 그런 당신에게 이 어두컴컴한 방은….<end>"

## 770004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だんだん、あなたはこの薄闇を　　　　<line-break>お嫌いになるでしょう。<line-break>そして、わたしのことも…。<line-break>でも、あなたが<line-break>ここにいらっしゃらなければ<line-break>これ以上、嫌われることだけはない…。<end>"
EN: "In time, you'll come to hate this gloom. And me as well... But if you stopped coming, at least you couldn't grow to hate me any more...<end>"
KO: "점점 당신은 이 어스름을 싫어하게 되겠죠. 그리고 저도…. 하지만 당신이 이곳에 계시지 않으면 더 이상 미움받을 일만큼은 없을 테니까….<end>"

## 770005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヒクツなことを言わないで<end>"
EN: "Don't put yourself down.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 비굴한 말 하지 마<end>"

## 770006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "外の世界を見に行こう<end>"
EN: "Let's go see the outside world.<end>"
KO: "바깥세상을 보러 가자<end>"

## 770007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…すみません。<line-break>…つい。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. It just slipped out.<end>"
KO: "…죄송합니다. …그만.<end>"

## 770008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そんなこと…。<line-break>私なんかはこの暗闇の中でずっと…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, no... Someone like me has been in this darkness all along...<end>"
KO: "…그런 건…. 나는 이 어둠 속에서 계속….<end>"

## 770009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…え、あの。<line-break><value:$28>様！？<end>"
EN: "What? Um... <value:$28>?!<end>"
KO: "…어, 저기. <value:$28>님!?<end>"

## 770010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "空気が軽い…。<line-break>それに、月の光が、<line-break>こんなにやさしく、明るいなんて…。<line-break>これが…外の世界…。<end>"
EN: "The air feels so light... And I never knew moonlight could be so gentle, so bright... So this is the outside world...<end>"
KO: "공기가 가벼워…. 달빛이 이렇게 부드럽고 밝다니…. 이게… 바깥세상….<end>"

## 770011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "手を…手を離してください！<line-break>タルテュバ様。<end>"
EN: "My hand... Please let go of my hand, Tartuba!<end>"
KO: "손을… 손을 놓아 주세요! 타르튜바 님.<end>"

## 770013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバ様、<line-break>おやめになってください。<line-break>この方はアトレイア様です。<end>"
EN: "Please stop, Tartuba. This is Atleia.<end>"
KO: "타르튜바 님, 그만두세요. 이분은 아트레이아 님입니다.<end>"

## 770014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア？<end>"
EN: "Atleia?<end>"
KO: "아트레이아?<end>"

## 770015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の父上の兄に当たる先王様の<line-break>ご息女、アトレイア王女。<line-break>…目がご不自由なのです。<end>"
EN: "Princess Atleia is the daughter of the former king, my father's elder brother. She... cannot see.<end>"
KO: "제 아버님의 형이신 선왕의 따님, 아트레이아 왕녀님입니다. …눈이 보이지 않으십니다.<end>"

## 770016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先王の娘だと！？<line-break>だからどうした？<line-break>ノコノコと出てきやがって…。<end>"
EN: "The former king's daughter?! So what? She has some nerve coming out here...<end>"
KO: "선왕의 딸이라고!? 그래서 어쨌다고? 태평하게 나타나긴….<end>"

## 770017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前みたいな女、<line-break>ティアナとは段違いなんだよ。<line-break>部屋に帰って、鏡でも見てこい！<end>"
EN: "A woman like you isn't even in Tiana's league. Go back to your room and take a look in the mirror!<end>"
KO: "너 같은 여자, 티아나와는 차원이 달라. 방으로 돌아가서 거울이나 보고 와!<end>"

## 770018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、なんだ貴様？<line-break>こっ、この俺のジャマをする気か？<line-break>クズのくせにクズのくせにクズの…！<line-break>殺す！<end>"
EN: "W-who the hell are you? You think you can stand in my way? You worthless, worthless, worthless...! I'll kill you!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐냐 네놈? 이, 이 나를 방해할 셈이냐? 쓰레기 주제에, 쓰레기 주제에, 쓰레기…! 죽여 버리겠다!<end>"

## 770019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>。<line-break>こ、この…。<line-break>く、くそっ！　覚えていろ！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Y-you... Damn you! You'll pay for this!<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>. 이, 이…. 젠장! 두고 봐!<end>"

## 770021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア…様。<end>"
EN: "Lady Atleia...<end>"
KO: "아트레이아… 님.<end>"

## 770022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "放っておいて！！<end>"
EN: "Leave me alone!<end>"
KO: "내버려 둬!!<end>"

## 770023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ。実力もないくせに<line-break>クズがカッコ付けおって！<line-break>興が失せた。帰る。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. A worthless wretch putting on airs without the strength to back it up! You've spoiled my amusement. I'm leaving.<end>"
KO: "큭. 실력도 없는 쓰레기가 멋있는 척이나 하고! 흥이 깨졌다. 돌아간다.<end>"

## 770025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア…様。<end>"
EN: "Lady Atleia...<end>"
KO: "아트레이아… 님.<end>"

## 770026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "放っておいて！！<end>"
EN: "Leave me alone!<end>"
KO: "내버려 둬!!<end>"

## 770027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイアを追う<end>"
EN: "Go after Atleia.<end>"
KO: "아트레이아를 쫓는다<end>"

## 770028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナのそばにいる<end>"
EN: "Stay with Tiana.<end>"
KO: "티아나 곁에 있는다<end>"

## 770029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…<value:$28>様！<end>"
EN: "Oh... <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "아… <value:$28> 님!<end>"

## 770030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…アトレイア様は<line-break>本当の先王の娘、本当の王女。　<line-break>でも、私は…<line-break>だから…みんな…<line-break>私のそばを…<line-break>すりぬけて…いく…。<end>"
EN: "Lady Atleia is the former king's true daughter, the true princess. But I... That's why everyone... just slips past me...<end>"
KO: "…아트레이아 님은 진짜 선왕의 딸, 진짜 왕녀. 하지만 나는… 그래서… 모두가… 내 곁을… 빠져나가… 가 버려….<end>"

## 770031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私はティアナ様に比べて醜い。　<line-break>…私はブザマで醜い。<line-break>…私は…。<line-break>目なんて見えなければよかった。<line-break>ずっと闇の中にいれば、<line-break>自分の姿に気がつかなかったのに。<end>"
EN: "I'm ugly beside Lady Tiana. I'm wretched and ugly. I... I wish I'd never been able to see. If I'd stayed in the darkness forever, I would never have known what I looked like.<end>"
KO: "…나는 티아나 님에 비하면 못생겼어. …나는 꼴사납고 추해. …나는…. 눈이 보이지 않았으면 좋았을 텐데. 계속 어둠 속에 있었다면 내 모습을 깨닫지 못했을 텐데.<end>"

## 770032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい。<line-break>私みたいに醜い者がついていって、<line-break>あなたに恥をかかせて…<line-break>すみません。<line-break>行って…ください…。<line-break>お願い…ひとりに…して…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. Someone as ugly as me went with you and embarrassed you... I'm sorry. Please go... Please... leave me alone...<end>"
KO: "미안해요. 저처럼 못생긴 사람이 따라다녀서 당신을 부끄럽게 하고…. 죄송해요. 가 주세요…. 부탁이니… 혼자 있게 해 줘….<end>"

## 770033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア様…、<line-break>大丈夫でしょうか？<end>"
EN: "Do you think Lady Atleia will be all right?<end>"
KO: "아트레이아 님… 괜찮으실까요?<end>"

## 770034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日は…ありがとうございます。<line-break>タルテュバ様に迫られていて<line-break>どうしようかと思っていました。<line-break>…つかれました。<line-break>申しわけありませんが<line-break>これで部屋に戻ります。<end>"
EN: "Thank you for today. Tartuba was pressing his attentions on me, and I didn't know what to do. I'm tired. Please excuse me, but I'll return to my room now.<end>"
KO: "오늘은… 감사합니다. 타르튜바 님께서 다가오셔서 어쩌나 했어요. …지쳤습니다. 죄송하지만 이만 방으로 돌아가겠습니다.<end>"

## 770035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…、<value:$28>様…。<end>"
EN: "Oh... <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아…, <value:$28> 님….<end>"

## 770036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この前は…。<end>"
EN: "About what happened before...<end>"
KO: "지난번에는….<end>"

## 770037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え…？<line-break>また、外へ…ですか？　　　<line-break>　<line-break>でも…この前のような…。<end>"
EN: "What...? You want to go outside again? But what if it turns out like last time...?<end>"
KO: "네…? 또 밖으로… 가시는 건가요? 하지만… 지난번처럼….<end>"

## 770038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ…わかりました。<line-break>で、でも、ちょっと…。<line-break>考えさせてください。<end>"
EN: "I... I understand. B-but please, give me a little time to think.<end>"
KO: "아… 알겠습니다. 하, 하지만 조금…. 생각하게 해 주세요.<end>"

## 770039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます。<end>"
EN: "Thank you.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다.<end>"

## 770040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかりました…。<line-break>一緒に連れていってください。<end>"
EN: "All right... Please take me with you.<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다…. 함께 데려가 주세요.<end>"

## 770041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すみません。<line-break>何時間も考え込んでしまって…。<line-break>でも、決心がつかなくて…。<line-break>お願い…します。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. I've been thinking about it for hours, but I still can't make up my mind... Please... take me with you.<end>"
KO: "죄송합니다. 몇 시간이나 고민했는데…. 그래도 결심이 서지 않아서…. 부탁…드릴게요.<end>"

## 770042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "外の…空気…。<line-break>なんだか、心が、<line-break>のびやかになってきます。<end>"
EN: "The air outside... Somehow, I feel my heart opening up.<end>"
KO: "밖의… 공기… 왠지 마음이 탁 트이는 것 같아요.<end>"

## 770043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "スラムの酒場に行くって…<line-break>…酒場ってなんですか？<end>"
EN: "You said we're going to a tavern in the Slums... What is a tavern?<end>"
KO: "슬럼가 술집에 간다니… 술집이 뭐예요?<end>"

## 770044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい。<line-break>とにかくついていきます。<line-break>スラムの酒場ですね。<end>"
EN: "Yes. I'll follow you. A tavern in the Slums, then.<end>"
KO: "네. 일단 따라가겠습니다. 슬럼의 술집이군요.<end>"

## 770045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わぁ。<line-break>きれい…。<end>"
EN: "Oh! It's beautiful...<end>"
KO: "와. 예쁘다….<end>"

## 770046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…っと、<value:$28>？<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>おっと、恨みっこなしだぜ？<line-break>お互い仕事次第で敵味方だ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>? Now, no hard feelings, all right? Our work can put us on the same side or against each other.<end>"
KO: "…어라, <value:$28>? 아, 서로 원망은 없기다? 일에 따라 적도 아군도 되는 사이니까.<end>"

## 770047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう、久しぶりだな。<end>"
EN: "Hey. Long time no see.<end>"
KO: "여어, 오랜만이군.<end>"

## 770048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>様と<line-break>お知り合いですか？<end>"
EN: "Are you acquainted with <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>님과 아는 사이이신가요?<end>"

## 770049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0知り合いですとも。<line-break>私の身分は彼が保証してくれます。<end>知り合いですとも。<line-break>私の身分は彼女が保証してくれます。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Indeed I do. He can vouch for me.<end>Indeed I do. She can vouch for me.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0아는 사이죠. 제 신분은 그가 보증해 줄 겁니다.<end>아는 사이죠. 제 신분은 그녀가 보증해 줄 겁니다.<end>"

## 770050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願いです。<line-break>もう一度、今の光を見せてください。<end>"
EN: "Please. Show me that light again.<end>"
KO: "부탁입니다. 한 번만 더 방금 그 빛을 보여 주세요.<end>"

## 770051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気に入ってもらえたかな？<end>"
EN: "Did you like it?<end>"
KO: "마음에 들었으려나?<end>"

## 770052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。<line-break>だって、とてもきれいなんですもの。<line-break>あんな美しいものを見たのは初めて。<end>"
EN: "Yes. It was so beautiful. I've never seen anything like it.<end>"
KO: "네. 정말 아름답잖아요. 저렇게 아름다운 건 처음 봤어요.<end>"

## 770053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そいつはどうかな。<line-break>ここには、<line-break>もっときれいなものがあるぜ。<end>"
EN: "I'm not so sure. There's something even more beautiful right here.<end>"
KO: "그건 어떨까. 여기엔 더 아름다운 게 있는데.<end>"

## 770054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　どこに？<end>"
EN: "What? Where?<end>"
KO: "어? 어디로?<end>"

## 770055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君だよ。<line-break>俺が作り出した幻なんかより、<line-break>君はずっと美しい。<end>"
EN: "You. You're far more beautiful than the illusion I created.<end>"
KO: "너야. 내가 만들어낸 환상 같은 것보다 네가 훨씬 아름다워.<end>"

## 770056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私が…？　美しい？<end>"
EN: "Me...? Beautiful?<end>"
KO: "내가…? 아름다워?<end>"

## 770057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、とてもきれいだ。<line-break>この世のものとは<line-break>思えないほどに。<line-break>透き通るように白い肌。<line-break>神秘的に輝く瞳。<end>"
EN: "Yes, very. So beautiful you hardly seem of this world. Skin so fair it seems translucent, and eyes that shine with a mysterious light.<end>"
KO: "그래, 정말 아름다워. 이 세상의 것이라곤 믿기 어려울 정도로. 투명할 만큼 하얀 피부, 신비롭게 빛나는 눈동자.<end>"

## 770058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…うそです、そんな。<end>"
EN: "That can't be true...<end>"
KO: "…거짓말이에요, 그런….<end>"

## 770059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は生まれてこの方、<line-break>うそを言ったこなど一度もない。<line-break>　<line-break>こんな美人がこの国にいるって、<line-break>知らなかった自分に腹が立つよ。<line-break>今までどこに隠れていたのかな？<end>"
EN: "I've never told a lie in my life. I'm angry with myself for never knowing there was such a beauty in this country. Where have you been hiding all this time?<end>"
KO: "난 태어나서 거짓말 같은 건 한 번도 한 적 없어. 이런 미인이 이 나라에 있는 줄 몰랐던 내가 화날 정도야. 지금까지 어디 숨어 있었지?<end>"

## 770060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当…ですか…？<end>"
EN: "Do you... really mean it?<end>"
KO: "정말…인가요…?<end>"

## 770061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、本当だ。<line-break>その宝石がみすぼらしく<line-break>見えるね。<line-break>それ、はずしたら？<end>"
EN: "Of course. That jewel looks positively shabby beside you. Why not take it off?<end>"
KO: "아, 정말이네. 그 보석이 초라해 보여. 그거 빼는 게 어때?<end>"

## 770062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この腕輪…ですか…？<line-break>え…ええ。<line-break>では…そうします。<end>"
EN: "This bracelet...? Oh... yes. Very well, I will.<end>"
KO: "이 팔찌… 말인가요…? 네… 네. 그럼… 그렇게 하겠습니다.<end>"

## 770063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかったら、そいつを　　　　　<line-break>俺にくれないか？<line-break>　<line-break>美しい君が身に付けていたものを<line-break>持っていたいんだ。<line-break>今日、出会った記念にね。<end>"
EN: "If you don't mind, could I have it? I'd like to keep something worn by someone as beautiful as you, as a memento of the day we met.<end>"
KO: "괜찮다면 그걸 나한테 주지 않을래? 아름다운 네가 몸에 지니고 있던 걸 갖고 싶어. 오늘 만난 기념으로 말이야.<end>"

## 770064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、構いません。<line-break>どうぞ。<end>"
EN: "Yes, of course. Here.<end>"
KO: "네, 괜찮습니다. 어서요.<end>"

## 770065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "You should take that ring off as well.<end>"

## 770066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…。<line-break>それでは、これも<line-break>持っていってください。<end>"

## 770067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうした方がいい。<line-break>その方がずっと輝いてみえる。<end>"

KO: "그러는 게 좋아요. 그쪽이 훨씬 빛나 보여요.<end>"

## 770069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいんです。<end>"
EN: "It's all right.<end>"
KO: "괜찮습니다.<end>"

## 770070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あんなにほめられたのは、<line-break>生まれて初めてでしたから…。<end>"
EN: "It's just... no one's ever praised me like that before.<end>"
KO: "…그렇게 칭찬받은 건 태어나서 처음이었으니까요….<end>"

## 770071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おもしろい模様…。<end>"
EN: "What an interesting pattern...<end>"
KO: "재미있는 무늬….<end>"

## 770072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "模様じゃないよ。<line-break>それは、飲んで歌える酒場って　　<line-break>書いてあるんだ。<line-break>お姉ちゃん、字が読めないの？<end>"
EN: "That's not a pattern. It says, “A tavern where you can drink and sing.” Miss, can't you read?<end>"
KO: "무늬가 아니야. '마시고 노래할 수 있는 술집'이라고 쓰여 있어. 누나, 글을 못 읽어?<end>"

## 770073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの模様に、そんな意味があるの？<end>"
EN: "That pattern means all of that?<end>"
KO: "그 무늬에 그런 의미가 있어?<end>"

## 770074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、それは模様じゃなくって　<line-break>文字だよ。<line-break>　<line-break>ほら、こっちには<line-break>お食事もどうぞって<line-break>書いてあるのさ。<end>"
EN: "I'm telling you, it's not a pattern. Those are letters. Look, this one says, “Food also served.”<end>"
KO: "그러니까 그건 무늬가 아니라 글자야. 봐, 이쪽에는 식사도 하시라고 쓰여 있잖아.<end>"

## 770075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すてき！<line-break>あなた、みんなわかるのね？<end>"
EN: "Wonderful! You understand all of them?<end>"
KO: "멋져! 당신, 전부 아는 거지?<end>"

## 770076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へへへっ。ホントは、パパに<line-break>教えてもらったばかりなんだ。<line-break>でもパパは、なんだって読めるよ。<line-break>ボクもパパみたいに<line-break>なんでも読めるようになるんだ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh! Actually, Papa only just taught me. But Papa can read anything. Someday I'll be able to read anything too, just like him.<end>"
KO: "헤헤. 사실 아빠한테 막 배운 참이야. 하지만 아빠는 뭐든 읽을 수 있어. 나도 아빠처럼 뭐든 읽게 될 거야.<end>"

## 770077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お…しょくじ…も…どう…ぞ？<end>"
EN: "“Food... also... served”?<end>"
KO: "식…사…도… 드…세요?<end>"

## 770078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さくらんぼ…の…じゅーす。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>それなら、こっちは<line-break>なんて書いてあるのです？<end>"
EN: "“Cherry... juice.” Then what does this one say?<end>"
KO: "체리… 주스. 그럼 이쪽에는 뭐라고 적혀 있나요?<end>"

## 770079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "나지라네… 팬케이크? 팬케이크가 뭐지?<end>"

## 770080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "当店自慢のデザートです。<line-break>ナジラネの果実に卵とハチミツを加えて<line-break>強火で軽く焼いたものです。<end>"
EN: "It's our specialty dessert. We mix Najirane Fruit with egg and honey, then lightly cook it over high heat.<end>"
KO: "저희 가게의 자랑인 디저트입니다. 나지라네 열매에 달걀과 꿀을 넣어 센 불에 살짝 구운 것이죠.<end>"

## 770081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、おいしそう。<end>"
EN: "Oh, that sounds delicious.<end>"
KO: "어머, 맛있겠네.<end>"

## 770082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "나지라네 팬케이크로 괜찮으시죠?<end>"

## 770083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 770084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃん、姉ちゃん！　　　<line-break>あんまり店のもんを<line-break>いじめるなって。<line-break>そいつは、もう３０分も<line-break>オーダーを待ってるんだぜ。<end>"

KO: "누나, 누나! 가게 물건 좀 그만 괴롭혀. 저 사람은 벌써 30분이나 주문을 기다리고 있다고.<end>"

## 770085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい。<line-break>でも、知らない模様…<line-break>いえ、文字ばかりで…。<end>"

## 770086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へへへ…。<line-break>おもしれえ姉ちゃんだ。<line-break>どれ、俺が教えてやるぜ。　　　　<line-break>この店で食うんなら、これだ。<line-break>スライムのあんかけ。<end>"

## 770087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すらいむの…あんかけ？<end>"

## 770088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 770089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 770094

## 770096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 770097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 770098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 770099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 770100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 770101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハハハ。<line-break>おおかた、酒場の雰囲気に<line-break>酔ったんだろう。<end>"

KO: "하하하. 아마 술집 분위기에 취한 거겠지.<end>"

## 770102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちぇ～！<line-break>せーっかく、ぼくのキレイな　　<line-break>歌の出番だったのになぁ。<line-break>ま、いいや！<line-break><value:$28>のためだ！<line-break>キレイな、いい曲弾いちゃうよ！<end>"

## 770103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちぇ～！<line-break>せーっかく、ぼくのキレイな　　　　　<line-break>歌の出番だったのになぁ。<line-break>よーし！<line-break>キレイなきみのために<line-break>キレイな曲弾いちゃうよ！<end>"
EN: "Aww! Just when it was finally time for my beautiful singing. All right! For a beautiful girl like you, I'll play a beautiful tune!<end>"
KO: "쳇~! 모처럼 내 아름다운 노래가 나설 차례였는데. 좋아! 아름다운 너를 위해 아름다운 곡을 연주해 줄게!<end>"

## 770104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハハハハハハ！<line-break>あんたの歌のおかげで<line-break>客も盛り上がって、大繁盛さ。　<line-break>少ないけど、とっときな。<end>"

## 770105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お金は結構ですから、あの…。<end>"
EN: "I don't need the money, but, um...<end>"

## 770106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 770107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 770108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へぇ、妙なものが<line-break>気に入っちまったんだなぁ。　　<line-break>よし、特別に差し上げよう。<end>"
EN: "Huh. You've taken a liking to a strange thing. All right, you can have it―just this once.<end>"
KO: "헤에, 별난 게 마음에 들었구나. 좋아, 특별히 줄게.<end>"

## 770109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあさあ、みんな！<line-break>人生は短いぞ！　飲め、楽しめ！<line-break>夜は長いぞ！　騒げ！　歌え！<end>"
EN: "Come on, everyone! Life is short―drink and be merry! The night is long―make some noise! Sing!<end>"
KO: "자자, 다들! 인생은 짧아! 마셔, 즐겨! 밤은 길어! 떠들고 노래해!<end>"

## 770110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…すみません、<line-break>騒ぎ過ぎてしまって。<line-break>朝になって…しまいましたね…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. I got rather carried away. It's morning already...<end>"
KO: "…죄송합니다, 너무 떠들었네요. 아침이… 되어 버렸군요….<end>"

## 770111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私…、なんにも知りませんでした。　<line-break>…いえ、初めから<line-break>知ろうとしてませんでした。<line-break>静かな闇だけが好きで<line-break>人を避けていましたから。<line-break>でも、雑踏っていいものなんですね。<end>"
EN: "I knew nothing... No, from the very beginning, I never tried to know anything. I loved only the quiet darkness, and I kept away from people. But a crowd can be rather wonderful, can't it?<end>"
KO: "저… 아무것도 몰랐어요. …아니, 처음부터 알려고 하지 않았어요. 고요한 어둠만 좋아해서 사람을 피했으니까요. 하지만 사람들로 북적이는 것도 좋은 거군요.<end>"

## 770112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私、今日、<line-break>世界中に人がたくさんいて<line-break>本当によかったって思います。<end>"
EN: "Today, I found myself truly glad that there are so many people in the world.<end>"
KO: "저, 오늘 세상에 사람이 많이 있어서 정말 다행이라고 생각해요.<end>"

## 770113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まず、字の勉強です。<line-break>あの男の子に負けないように<line-break>いっぱい勉強しないと…。<end>"
EN: "First, I must learn to read. I'll have to study hard so that boy doesn't leave me behind...<end>"
KO: "우선 글자 공부부터예요. 그 남자아이에게 지지 않도록 많이 공부해야죠….<end>"

## 770114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その前に睡眠をとらないと<end>"
EN: "But first, you need to get some sleep.<end>"
KO: "그 전에 잠부터 자야 해<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 770116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 770118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…、もう、こんな時間。<line-break>衛兵たちが通常勤務に入る前に<line-break>出て行かれた方がよろしいですよ。<line-break><value:$28>様、また、<line-break>私の部屋にいらしてくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Oh... is it that late already? You should leave before the guards begin their regular duty. Please come to my room again, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아… 벌써 이런 시간이네요. 위병들이 평상 근무에 들어가기 전에 나가시는 게 좋겠어요. <value:$28>님, 또 제 방에 와주세요.<end>"

## 770119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア…。<line-break>私のかわいいアトレイア…。　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Atleia... My sweet Atleia...<end>"
KO: "아트레이아…. 내 귀여운 아트레이아….<end>"

## 770120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ…、<line-break>お母…様…。<end>"
EN: "Oh... Moth...er...<end>"
KO: "아아… 어머…님….<end>"

## 770121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレ…イア…。<line-break>お前だけでも…<line-break>生き…延びて……。　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Atle...ia... You, at least... must survive...<end>"
KO: "아트레…이아…. 너만이라도… 살아… 남아…….<end>"

## 770122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "キャアアアアー！！<line-break>お母様！　お母様ーッ！<end>"
EN: "Aaaaaah! Mother! Mother!<end>"
KO: "꺄아아아아!! 어머니! 어머니!!<end>"

## 770123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "キャーッキャッキャッ！<line-break>これで私の娘ティアナが　　　　　　<line-break>王位につくのよ！<line-break>アトレイア…あなたは闇の中で<line-break>さげすまれて孤独に生きるの！<line-break>そして闇の中で孤独に死ぬの！<end>"
EN: "Ah-ha-ha-ha! Now my daughter Tiana will take the throne! Atleia... you will live alone in the darkness, scorned and despised! And in that darkness, you will die alone!<end>"
KO: "캬하하하! 이제 내 딸 티아나가 왕위에 오르는 거야! 아트레이아… 넌 어둠 속에서 멸시받으며 외롭게 살아! 그리고 어둠 속에서 외롭게 죽는 거야!<end>"

## 770124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イヤ…嫌ァアアアアア！！　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>お母様！　お母様！<line-break>置いていかないで！　お母様！<end>"
EN: "No... Nooooo! Mother! Mother! Don't leave me! Mother!<end>"
KO: "싫어… 싫어어어어어!! 어머니! 어머니! 두고 가지 마세요! 어머니!<end>"

## 770125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ…、私のアトレイア…。　　　<line-break>寒くて……暗くて…、<line-break>痛いわ…、苦しいわ…。<line-break>この世界は<line-break>つらくて悲しいことばかり…。<line-break>みんな虚無に帰した方がいいの…。<end>"
EN: "Oh... my Atleia... It's so cold... so dark... It hurts... I'm suffering... This world holds nothing but pain and sorrow... Everyone would be better off returning to the Void...<end>"
KO: "아아… 나의 아트레이아…. 춥고…… 어둡고…, 아파… 괴로워…. 이 세계는 힘들고 슬픈 일뿐이야…. 모두 허무로 돌아가는 편이 좋아…<end>"

## 770126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "つらくて…悲しい…世界……。　　<line-break>だから…みんな…虚無に……。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "A world... of pain and sorrow... So everyone... should return to the Void...<end>"
KO: "괴롭고… 슬픈… 세계……. 그래서… 모두… 허무로…….<end>"

## 770127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "キャーッキャッキャッ！<line-break>その通りぃよ、アトレイア。<line-break>すべてを虚無に帰すのぉよぉ。　　　<line-break>憎いでぇしょぉお？<line-break>この世界が、この現実がぁ！<line-break>だから、なくしぃてしまうのよぉお。<end>"
EN: "Ah-ha-ha-ha! That's riiight, Atleia. Send everything back to the Voooid. You hate it, don't yooou? This world, this reality! So make it all go awaaay!<end>"
KO: "캬하하하! 맞아아, 아트레이아. 모든 걸 허무로 돌리는 거야아. 밉지이이? 이 세계가, 이 현실이이! 그러니까 없애 버리는 거야아.<end>"

## 770128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "困るなあ、<line-break>調教のジャマされちゃ…。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>まあ、人形はまた作ればいいし、<line-break>せっかくだから、君もお人形さんで<line-break>遊んでいきなよ。<end>"
EN: "Now, that's a nuisance. You've interrupted her conditioning... Oh well, I can always make another doll. Since you're here, why don't you stay and play with the doll too?<end>"
KO: "곤란하네, 조교를 방해하면…. 뭐, 인형은 다시 만들면 되고, 모처럼이니 너도 인형이랑 놀다 가.<end>"

## 770129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふーっ…。<line-break>もう、君ときたら、僕の考えを<line-break>ちっとも理解してくれないね。<end>"
EN: "Whew... Honestly, you never understand what I'm trying to do.<end>"
KO: "후우…. 정말 너란 사람은, 내 생각을조금도 이해해 주지 않는군.<end>"

## 770130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何度もこんなことするの、<line-break>本当はイヤなんだけどなあ…。<end>"
EN: "I really don't like having to do this over and over...<end>"
KO: "몇 번이나 이런 짓을 하는 건 사실 싫은데….<end>"

## 770131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "前に僕、言ったよね。<line-break>色惑の瞳でアトレイアに<line-break>この世界を見せてやるって。<line-break>この、悲しみと憎悪に満ちた<line-break>世界を見せてやるって。<line-break>それなのに……。<end>"
EN: "I told you before, didn't I? I would use the Eye of Allure to show Atleia this world―a world filled with sorrow and hatred. And yet...<end>"
KO: "전에 내가 말했지. 색혹의 눈동자로 아트레이아에게 이 세계를 보여 주겠다고. 이 슬픔과 증오로 가득한 세계를 보여 주겠다고. 그런데도…….<end>"

## 770132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "困るなあ…ああいうことされちゃ。<line-break>彼女の心の窓が開いちゃう。<line-break>いい闇の王女になれないよ。<line-break>あーあ、<line-break>僕、こういう荒っぽいのは<line-break>キライなのになあ…。<end>"
EN: "It's a real nuisance when you do things like that... It opens a window into her heart. She'll never become a proper Princess of Darkness. Oh dear. I really don't care for this sort of rough stuff...<end>"
KO: "곤란한데… 저런 짓을 하면 그녀 마음의 창이 열려 버려. 좋은 어둠의 왕녀가 될 수 없잖아. 아아, 난 이런 난폭한 건 싫은데….<end>"

## 770134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ…、あ、そうだ。<line-break>死んじゃう前に僕が何者なのか　　　　<line-break>教えてあげようね。<line-break>僕は破壊神復活のための秘密結社、<line-break>システィーナの伝道師のシャリ。<line-break>東方の博士って呼ぶ人もいるけどね。<end>"
EN: "Heh... Oh, that's right. Before you die, I'll tell you who I am. I'm Shalli, an evangelist of Sistina, the secret society devoted to reviving the God of Destruction. Some people call me the Scholar from the East, though.<end>"
KO: "후후… 아, 그렇지. 죽기 전에 내가 누군지 알려 줄게. 나는 파괴신 부활을 위한 비밀결사 시스티나의 전도사 샤리야. 동방의 박사라고 부르는 사람도 있지만.<end>"

## 770135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、自己紹介も終わったことだし<line-break>死んでくれるかな。<end>"
EN: "Well, now that the introductions are over, would you mind dying?<end>"
KO: "자, 자기소개도 끝났으니 이제 죽어 줄래?<end>"

## 770136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "キャアアアア！<end>"
EN: "Aaaaaah!<end>"
KO: "꺄아아아아!<end>"

## 770137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今君が倒したのは、彼女の心の闇。<line-break>彼女の心の一部なんだ。<line-break>傷つければ、当然彼女も傷つくよ。　<line-break>そして、そうやって<line-break>いくら心の闇を押し込んでも<line-break>また、すぐにあふれ出すよ。フフ…。<end>"
EN: "What you just defeated was the darkness in her heart. It's part of her heart. Hurt it, and of course you'll hurt her too. And no matter how many times you push that darkness back down, it'll soon come spilling out again. Heh...<end>"
KO: "지금 네가 쓰러뜨린 건 그녀 마음의 어둠, 그녀 마음의 일부야. 상처 입히면 당연히 그녀도 상처받지. 그리고 그렇게 아무리 마음의 어둠을 억눌러도 금방 또 넘쳐흐를 거야. 후후….<end>"

## 770138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、とどめだ。<end>"
EN: "Now for the finishing blow.<end>"
KO: "자, 끝장이다.<end>"

## 770139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>様は<line-break>殺させません！<end>"
EN: "I will not allow <value:$28> to be killed!<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28> 님은 죽게 두지 않겠습니다!<end>"

## 770140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーもう、やんなっちゃうな。<line-break><value:$28>、君のせいで<line-break>調教は完全に失敗だよ。<line-break>しょうがない。<line-break>他のことして遊ぼっと。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I'm sick of this. <value:$28>, thanks to you, the conditioning has been a complete failure. Oh well. I'll go find something else to do for fun.<end>"
KO: "아 정말, 싫다. <value:$28>, 너 때문에 조교가 완전히 실패했어. 어쩔 수 없지. 다른 거 하면서 놀아야겠다.<end>"

## 770141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふ…ふふ…ふ…。<line-break>だ、大丈夫…ですか？<end>"
EN: "Ah... ha-ha... A-are you all right?<end>"
KO: "후후… 후후… 후…. 괘, 괜찮…으세요?<end>"

## 770142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ、よかった…。<line-break>ふ…ふふふ…ふ…、<line-break>わ、私…は…、少し…震えています。<line-break>よろしければ、<line-break>も、もう少しだけ…<line-break>落ち着くまで…そ、そばに…。<end>"
EN: "Th-that's a relief... Ah... ha-ha... I-I'm... trembling a little. If you don't mind, could you... stay with me just a little longer... until I've calmed down?<end>"
KO: "다, 다행이다…. 후… 후후후… 저, 저는… 조금 떨고 있습니다. 괜찮다면 조, 조금만 더… 진정될 때까지… 곁에….<end>"

## 770143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フ…フフ…<line-break>私…は…。<line-break>私…は…。<end>"
EN: "Heh... heh... I... I...<end>"
KO: "후… 후후… 나…는…. 나…는….<end>"

## 770144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3お母…様…<line-break>アアア…！　<line-break>ああ…私の目の前で…死ん…だ…。<line-break>私には…虚無しか残されない。<line-break>お…お母様…。<line-break>お母様を殺さないでぇェ！！<end>すべてが…完全な静寂と…。　　　<line-break>フフフ…すべて…が…<line-break>完全なる…平等に…。<line-break>私と…あなたはひとつになる…。<line-break>フフ…それ以上…<line-break>ゼロに…なぁる…。ウフフフッ…。<end>私は…<line-break>私は…<line-break>私は…なぜ見たのかしら…。　<line-break>この世界を…見てしまった…。<line-break>キレイに…なりたい。<line-break>そして…、あの人…。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3Mo...ther... Aaaah...! Ahh... died... right before my eyes... All I have left... is the Void. M... Mother... Don't kill Mother!!<end>Everything will be... utter silence... Heh heh... everything will be... perfectly equal... You and I will become one... Heh... and beyond even that... we'll become zero... Hee hee hee...<end>I... I... I... Why did I ever have to lay eyes on this world...? I want to be beautiful. And... that person...<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3어머…니… 아아아…! 아아… 내 눈앞에서… 죽었…어…. 내게는… 허무밖에 남지 않아. 어… 어머니…. 어머니를 죽이지 마아아!!<end>모든 것이… 완전한 정적과…. 후후후… 모든 것이… 완전한… 평등으로…. 나와… 너는 하나가 된다…. 후후… 그 이상으로… 제로가… 돼에…. 우후후훗….<end>나는… 나는… 나는… 왜 본 걸까…. 이 세계를… 봐 버렸어…. 아름다워지고… 싶어. 그리고… 그 사람….<end>"

## 770145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…<line-break><value:$28>様。<line-break>また、いらしてくださったのですね。<line-break>…うれしい。<end>"
EN: "Ah... <value:$28>. You came back. I'm glad.<end>"
KO: "아… <value:$28>님. 또 와 주셨군요. …기뻐요.<end>"

## 770146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…<line-break><value:$28>様、<line-break>いらしてくださったのですね。<line-break>…うれしい。<end>"
EN: "Ah... <value:$28>. You came. I'm glad.<end>"
KO: "아… <value:$28> 님, 와 주셨군요. …기뻐요.<end>"

## 770147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この前のメニューの字は<line-break>ほぼ覚えました。そうしたら今度は、<line-break>お料理を作ってみたくなりました。<line-break>…いつか、<line-break>味見していただけると<line-break>うれしいのですけれど…。<end>"
EN: "I've almost memorized the writing on the menu from the other day. Now I'd like to try cooking. I'd be very happy if you could taste something I've made someday...<end>"
KO: "지난번 메뉴의 글자는 거의 외웠어요. 그러고 나니 이번엔 요리를 만들어 보고 싶어졌어요. …언젠가 맛을 봐 주시면 기쁠 것 같아요….<end>"

## 770148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>様のおかげで<line-break>私の人生は変わりました。<line-break>…けれど、まだ恐ろしいのです。<line-break>ひょっとしたら<line-break>真の闇の深淵がまだ、私を飲み込もうと<line-break>しているのではないかと…。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, <value:$28>, my life has changed. But I am still afraid that perhaps the abyss of true darkness is still trying to swallow me.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28> 님 덕분에 제 인생은 바뀌었습니다. …하지만 아직 무서워요. 혹시 진정한 어둠의 심연이 아직도 저를 삼키려 하고 있는 건 아닐까 하고….<end>"

## 770149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人というものは<line-break>どうして、こんなに<line-break>間違うのでしょう？　　　<line-break>そしてそれでも、<line-break>私のようにやり直せるのは<line-break>どうしてなのでしょう？<end>"
EN: "Why do people make so many mistakes? And yet, how can anyone start over, as I have?<end>"
KO: "사람은 어째서 이렇게 많이 잘못하는 걸까요? 그리고 그래도 저처럼 다시 시작할 수 있는 건 어째서일까요?<end>"

## 770150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec077-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たぶん、間違いより強い、<line-break>何か、やさしくて、大事なものが<line-break>この世には存在するんですね。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps there really is something in this world stronger than any mistake―something gentle and precious.<end>"
KO: "아마 잘못보다 더 강한 무언가, 상냥하고 소중한 것이 이 세상에는 존재하는 거겠죠.<end>"

## 770151

JP: "…これは、予備メッセージ１…。<line-break>アトレイア、Ｖ４です。 <end>"
EN: ""
KO: "…이건 예비 메시지 1…. 아트레이아, V4입니다.<end>"

## 780000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: ""

## 780002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私にはその事件の記憶はありません。<line-break>まだ、赤ちゃんでしたから…。<end>"
EN: "I have no memory of it. I was still a baby at the time.<end>"
KO: "저는 그 사건을 기억하지 못해요. 아직 아기였으니까…<end>"

## 780003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、そのとき、私は<line-break>母と、光とを失ったんです…。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>そのことを人に聞かされて、<line-break>ずいぶんエリス叔母様を恨みました。<line-break>従妹のティアナ様にも嫉妬しました。<end>"
EN: "But that was when I lost both my mother and the light. When I learned what had happened, I came to resent Aunt Eris deeply. I was jealous of my cousin Tiana, too.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그때 저는 어머니와 빛을 잃었어요…. 그 일을 남에게 전해 듣고 에리스 숙모님을 많이 원망했죠. 사촌인 티아나 님도 질투했고요.<end>"

## 780004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、私の生命を助けてくれたのは　　<line-break>実は、エリス叔母様だったのです。<line-break>　<line-break>反乱が叔母様の手腕によって失敗すると<line-break>母は私に毒を飲ませ、<line-break>無理心中しようとしました。<end>"
EN: "But in truth, Aunt Eris was the one who saved my life. When her efforts caused the rebellion to fail, my mother made me drink poison and tried to take me with her in death.<end>"
KO: "하지만 사실 제 목숨을 구해 주신 분은 에리스 숙모님이었어요. 숙모님의 수완으로 반란이 실패하자 어머니는 제게 독을 먹이고 함께 죽으려 하셨습니다.<end>"

## 780005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "叔母様は私を必死で介抱して<line-break>瀕死の私を蘇生させてくれたそうです。<line-break>ただ、視力は戻りませんでしたが…。<line-break>それでも、私に光を取り戻そうとして<line-break>叔母様は、その後、ずいぶん<line-break>毒の研究をなさったそうです。<end>"
EN: "I'm told my aunt tended to me desperately and brought me back from the brink of death. My sight never returned, though. Even so, she wanted to restore the light to me, and afterward devoted herself to researching poisons.<end>"
KO: "숙모님은 저를 필사적으로 간호해서 죽어가던 저를 살려 주셨다고 해요. 다만 시력은 돌아오지 않았지만…. 그래도 제게 빛을 되찾아 주려고 숙모님은 그 뒤로 오랫동안 독을 연구하셨다고 합니다.<end>"

## 780006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…レルラ＝ロントン様が<line-break>そうおっしゃっていました。　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "…That is what Relra-Lonton told me.<end>"
KO: "…레를라 론톤 님이 그렇게 말씀하셨어요.<end>"

## 780007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス様に、あとで確認しようと　　<line-break>お話を伺いましたら、<line-break>はぐらかされました。<line-break>フフ、あのはぐらかし方だと<line-break>レルラ＝ロントン様のお話は<line-break>事実ですね。<end>"
EN: "I later asked Xenetes about it, hoping to confirm the story, but he evaded the question. Heh. The way he did it tells me Relra-Lonton's story must be true.<end>"
KO: "제네테스 님께 나중에 확인하려고 말씀을 여쭤봤더니 얼버무리시더군요. 후후, 그 얼버무리는 모습이라면 렐라=론톤 님의 이야기는 사실이네요.<end>"

## 780008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レルラ＝ロントン様は<line-break>お顔が広く、情報に通じたお方です。　<line-break>間違いはないでしょう。<end>"
EN: "Relra-Lonton is well connected and well informed. I doubt there's any mistake.<end>"
KO: "렐라 론톤 님은 인맥이 넓고 정보에 밝으신 분입니다. 틀림없을 겁니다.<end>"

## 780009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一度、王位を狙った者である私が<line-break>赤子だったにせよ、生きていられたのも<line-break>それならば、うなずけます。<line-break>それなのに、私は何も見ようとせず、<line-break>暗闇の中で、叔母様とティアナ様を<line-break>恨みつづけていました。<end>"
EN: "If that is true, it explains why I was allowed to live, even as an infant who had once been put forward for the throne. And yet I refused to see anything. I remained in the darkness, resenting my aunt and Tiana.<end>"
KO: "한때 왕위를 노렸던 제가 아기였다고는 해도 살아남을 수 있었던 것도 그렇다면 이해가 됩니다. 그런데도 저는 아무것도 보려 하지 않고 어둠 속에서 숙모님과 티아나 님을 계속 원망했습니다.<end>"

## 780010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何も知ろうとしなかったことで<line-break>私は、自分の殻に閉じこもり、随分と<line-break>叔母様を傷つけたことと思います。<line-break>あのころはひとりで暗闇に生きている<line-break>つもりでいました。いろんな人に<line-break>生かされていたことを知ろうともせず。<end>"
EN: "By refusing to learn anything, I shut myself away and must have hurt my aunt deeply. Back then, I believed I was living all alone in the darkness, never even trying to understand that so many others had kept me alive.<end>"
KO: "아무것도 알려 하지 않은 탓에 저는 제 껍질 속에 틀어박혀 이모님께 큰 상처를 드렸다고 생각합니다. 그때는 혼자 어둠 속에서 산다고 생각했어요. 많은 사람의 도움으로 살아가고 있다는 걸 알려고도 하지 않았죠.<end>"

## 780011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、今は、知ろうと思います。<line-break>せっかく、あなたが、私を暗闇から<line-break>引きずり出してくださったんですから。<end>"
EN: "So now, I mean to learn. You went to all the trouble of dragging me out of the darkness, after all.<end>"
KO: "그래서 이제는 알고 싶어요. 모처럼 당신이 저를 어둠 속에서 끌어내 주셨으니까요.<end>"

## 780012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの…、<line-break>あなたはいったい…？<end>"
EN: "Um... who are you?<end>"
KO: "저, 저기… 당신은 대체…?<end>"

## 780013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>。<line-break>どうしたの？　まさか来てくれるなんて<line-break>思ってもみなかったよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. What brings you here? I never imagined you'd actually come.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>. 무슨 일이야? 설마 와 줄 줄은 생각도 못 했어.<end>"

## 780014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリ、この方は…？<end>"
EN: "Shalli, who is this...?<end>"
KO: "샤리, 이분은…?<end>"

## 780015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア様。この若者こそ<line-break>君に色惑の瞳を持ってきてくれた<line-break>勇者、<value:$28>だよ。<end>"
EN: "Lady Atleia, this is <value:$28>, the young hero who brought you the Eye of Allure.<end>"
KO: "아트레이아 님. 이 젊은이가 바로 당신께 색혹의 눈동자를 가져다준 용사 <value:$28>입니다.<end>"

## 780016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ…。　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>様、<line-break>本当にありがとうございました。<line-break>あなたのおかげで<line-break>私は光を知ることができました。<line-break>こんなにうれしいことはありません。<end>"
EN: "Oh... <value:$28>, thank you so very much. Because of you, I have come to know light. Nothing could make me happier.<end>"
KO: "세상에…. <value:$28> 님, 정말 감사합니다. 당신 덕분에 저는 빛을 알게 되었어요. 이렇게 기쁜 일은 없어요.<end>"

## 780017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "前に少し話したよね？　彼女が<line-break>先王の遺子にしてティアナ王女の従姉、<line-break>アトレイア王女さ。<end>"
EN: "We spoke of her briefly before, remember? This is Princess Atleia, the late king's surviving child and Princess Tiana's older cousin.<end>"
KO: "전에 조금 이야기했지? 그녀가 선왕의 유자이자 티아나 왕녀의 사촌인 아트레이아 왕녀야.<end>"

## 780018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、お礼はもういい、アトレイア？<line-break>それじゃ、僕が<value:$28>様を<line-break>王宮の外までご案内するよ。<end>"
EN: "All right, that's enough thanks, Atleia. I'll escort <value:$28> out of the palace.<end>"
KO: "자, 감사는 이제 됐지, 아트레이아? 그럼 제가 <value:$28>님을 왕궁 밖까지 안내할게요.<end>"

## 780019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの…。<end>"
EN: "Um...<end>"
KO: "저, 저기….<end>"

## 780020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの…、よろしければ…その…<line-break>…また、いらしてくださいませんか？<end>"
EN: "Um... if you don't mind... could you... come and visit again?<end>"
KO: "저기… 괜찮으시다면… 그… 또 와주시겠어요?<end>"

## 780021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、アトレイア王女もああ言ってるし、<line-break>君もたまには顔を出してよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, you heard Princess Atleia. Drop by once in a while.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 아트레이아 왕녀도 저렇게 말하니 너도 가끔 얼굴 좀 비춰줘.<end>"

## 780022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、王女様、<line-break>失礼いたします。<end>"
EN: "Well then, Your Highness, if you'll excuse me.<end>"
KO: "그럼 공주님, 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 780023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、ありがとう。<line-break>彼女は今、光を手に入れて<line-break>世界を見ることができるようになった。<line-break>悲しみと憎悪に彩られた世界をね。<line-break>くすっ、あははははは！<line-break>ホント、ありがとうね！<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>. She has light now, and she can see the world―a world colored by sorrow and hatred. Hee hee... ah ha ha ha ha! Thank you so much!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 고마워. 그녀는 이제 빛을 얻어 세상을 볼 수 있게 됐어. 슬픔과 증오로 물든 세상을 말이지. 후후, 아하하하하! 정말 고마워!<end>"

## 780024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア様、彼は私の友人で、<line-break><value:$28>といいます。<end>"
EN: "Lady Atleia, this is my friend, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아트레이아 님, 그는 제 친구로 <value:$28>이라고 합니다.<end>"

## 780025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ…。そうだったのですか。<line-break>突然なので驚いてしまいました。<line-break><value:$28>さん、はじめまして。<end>"
EN: "Oh... I see. I was simply surprised by how sudden this was. It's a pleasure to meet you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "어머…. 그랬군요. 갑작스러워서 놀랐어요. <value:$28>님, 처음 뵙겠습니다.<end>"

## 780026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "前に少し話したよね？　彼女が<line-break>先王の遺子にしてティアナ王女の従姉、<line-break>アトレイア王女さ。<end>"
EN: "She came up briefly before, remember? She's Princess Atleia, the late king's daughter and Princess Tiana's older cousin.<end>"
KO: "전에 조금 이야기했지? 그녀가 선왕의 유자이자 티아나 왕녀의 사촌인 아트레이아 왕녀야.<end>"

## 780027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいさつはもういい、アトレイア？<line-break>それじゃ、僕は<value:$28>を<line-break>王宮の外まで送っていくよ。<end>"
EN: "That is enough introductions for now, Atleia. I'll see <value:$28> out of the palace.<end>"
KO: "인사는 이제 됐지, 아트레이아? 그럼 내가 <value:$28>을 왕궁 밖까지 데려다줄게.<end>"

## 780028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。<line-break>ごきげんよう、<value:$28>さん。<line-break>また、お会いできるといいですね。<end>"
EN: "Very well. Farewell, <value:$28>. I hope we shall meet again.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 안녕히 계세요, <value:$28>님. 또 만날 수 있으면 좋겠네요.<end>"

## 780029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、王女様<line-break>失礼いたします。<end>"
EN: "Then, Your Highness, please excuse me.<end>"
KO: "그럼 공주님, 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 780030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、ちょっと意外だったよ。　　　<line-break>君って薄情だなと思ってたけど、<line-break>こうして顔出してくれるなんてさ。<line-break>それじゃ、またね<value:$28>。<line-break>僕はこれから、彼女にこの世界を<line-break>見せてあげるんだ。<end>"
EN: "Still, I'm a little surprised. I thought you were pretty heartless, but here you are, dropping by. See you, <value:$28>. Now I'm going to show her this world.<end>"
KO: "그래도 좀 의외였어. 넌 꽤 매정하다고 생각했는데 이렇게 얼굴을 비춰 주다니. 그럼 또 보자, <value:$28>. 난 이제 그녀에게 이 세계를 보여 줄 거야.<end>"

## 780031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悲しみと憎悪で彩られた世界をね。<line-break>ウフ、あははは、あはははは！<end>"
EN: "A world colored by sorrow and hatred. Hee hee... ah ha ha ha, ah ha ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "슬픔과 증오로 물든 세계를 말이지. 우후, 아하하하, 아하하하하!<end>"

## 780032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう。よく来やがったな。　　　　　　<line-break>いい匂いがしてるだろ。<line-break>アトレイアだよ。<line-break>あのメニュー読んでるだけじゃ<line-break>飽きたらなくなって、最近じゃ<line-break>いろんなレシピを読んで料理に挑戦だ。<end>"
EN: "Hey. So you made it. Smells good, doesn't it? That's Atleia. Just reading that menu wasn't enough anymore, so lately Atleia's been poring over all kinds of recipes and trying her hand at cooking.<end>"
KO: "여어. 잘 왔군. 좋은 냄새 나지? 아트레이아야. 메뉴만 읽는 걸로는 성에 안 차서 요즘은 여러 레시피를 읽고 직접 요리에 도전 중이지.<end>"

## 780033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうだい、アトレイアの料理を<line-break>味見していってやるか、<line-break><value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "What do you say, <value:$28>? Want to try some of Atleia's cooking?<end>"
KO: "어때, 아트레이아 요리를 맛보고 갈래, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 780034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "味見する<end>"
EN: "Taste it<end>"
KO: "맛을 본다<end>"

## 780036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec078-part99.toml

JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0やさしい男だな。<line-break>ま、男はやさしいだけじゃ<line-break>ダメなんだがな。<end>やさしい奴だな。女は愛嬌か？<line-break>ま、お前の場合は女は度胸の方が、<line-break>ぴったり来るがな。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0You're a kind man. Mind you, kindness alone isn't enough in a man.<end>You're a kind soul. They say a woman needs charm, eh? Well, in your case, guts seems more fitting.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0다정한 남자군. 뭐, 남자는 다정하기만 해서는 안 되지만.<end>다정한 녀석이군. 여자는 애교라고? 뭐, 너한테는 여자도 배짱이라는 쪽이 더 잘 맞지만.<end>"
